\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_joint_fit_leaves_the_gaussian_and_the_bound_was_chi2_not_sigma}{P15R4}\textbf{P15R4}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_joint\_fit\_leaves\_the\_gaussian\_and\_the\_bound\_was\_chi2\_not\_sigma}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`PO-24`; `sec:diffusion-scale`, continuing `C62` and `r6409`} \ (**THE JOINT FIT RUN: THE RESIDUAL IS THE PREDICTED GAUSSIAN TO A CORRELATION OF \$1.000000\$, AND IT SURVIVES LARGER THAN THE BOUND IT WAS TO BE MEASURED AGAINST.** `r6409` predicted a Gaussian-shaped residual no tilt removes; fitting \$A\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta n\_s\$ jointly against the signature-carrying spectrum on plik\_lite's own bandpowers and covariance returns exactly that --- the residual correlates with the analytic \$S(\textbackslash\{\}ell)-A(\textbackslash\{\}ell/1000)\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash\{\}Delta n\}\$ at \$1.000000\$. It survives at **\$1.497\$ \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$/bin** over `P15`'s 185-bin ceiling window (\$\textbackslash\{\}ell=100\$--\$1758\$, 171 covered bins).  **AND THE BOUND WAS QUOTED IN THE WRONG UNITS**: `C62` says \$0.26\$ **\$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$ per bin** and converts to \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ by a square root in its own check; `r6409` restated it as "\$0.26\$ sigma per bin" and the `r6521` order inherited that --- the looser bound quoted as the tighter, since \$0.26\$ \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$/bin is \$0.510\$ \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$/bin.  **AND IT IS THE MORE FAVOURABLE OF `C62`'s TWO**, which says in terms that the instrument route (\$0.92\$) "is the more faithful of the two and it is the LARGER, so the wash is the weaker claim and is asserted at the weaker bound".  **AND THE DISPLACEMENT IS NOT A NUMBER, NOW MEASURED**: \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta n\_s = -0.083\$ over \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=100\$--\$1760\$, \$-0.521\$ over \$1400\$--\$2508\$ --- a factor of \$6.3\$ across windows of the same data, with absorption rising monotonically as the window narrows upward (\$69.8\textbackslash\{\}\%\textbackslash\{\}to97.6\textbackslash\{\}\%\$). **In the likelihood's own units** the signature costs \$+357.3\$ \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$ with amplitude alone and \$+84.5\$ over 171 bins after the tilt is refitted: the fit **improves** the comparison by \$272.8\$, absorbing \$76\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, and does not remove it.). 
\emph{Computes:} C62's unit asserted from its own text and its own `math.sqrt` conversion; r6409's restatement asserted; C62's two-route sentence asserted after stripping comment markers; **two calibrations --- a known injected tilt recovered to \$10\textasciicircum{}\{-11\}\$, AND the SCALE, which the first is blind to because a tilt-recovery test is unit-free**; the pipeline asserted to reproduce `chi2\_of` on the same spectrum and window; the residual's correlation with the analytic shape; the residual asserted larger than both C62 routes; the displacement's spread across windows; monotone absorption; the sky-cost split; the red/blue sign difference between the two fits; and the \$r=1\$ control returning \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta n\_s\textbackslash\{\}sim10\textasciicircum{}\{-14\}\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}sim10\textasciicircum{}\{-22\}\$
 \emph{Bound:} **scope: what a tilt ABSORBS, not whether CR fits the sky.** TT only, no polarisation, no lensing, no \$\textbackslash\{\}ell<100\$ from this baseline; two parameters free, so `C62`'s scoping stands unchanged --- an upper bound on what survives in shape, and no bound at all on the tilt, since \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ moves \$r\_D\$ itself.  **\$n\_s\$ is INHERITED boundary data here**, so a displacement is a different INPUT and not a conflict; what would be a tension is a constraint on \$n\_s\$ that does not run through the damping tail, which this does not supply. The baseline is the instrument's control arm, unlensed and ceiling-limited where the sky is neither --- `C62`'s own stated reason its routes differ by about three

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_one_fitted_number_moves_the_scale_and_not_the_peak}{P15R6}\textbf{P15R6}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_one\_fitted\_number\_moves\_the\_scale\_and\_not\_the\_peak}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\

\emph{Computes:} that the configuration reproduces `P15`'s own quoted \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1=206\$ BEFORE its scan is read; that \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$'s range exceeds \$40\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1\$'s is under \$5\textbackslash\{\}\%\$; that \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ falls monotonically; that \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1\$ misses the sky at EVERY onset and the misses spread by under \$3\$ points; that the two pinnings agree to \$10\textasciicircum{}\{-3\}\$; that the ratio nonetheless crosses the sky, so reading it alone gives the opposite answer; and that the control's floor sits below `LATARG`
 \emph{Bound:} **scope: driven, `LMAXL=520`, `LSTEP=2`, each arm on its own geometry throughout.** It does not shrink the deficit and does not say the construction is wrong --- it says \$0.6830\$ and \$-6.6\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ are two spellings of one number, and that no choice of the one fitted number touches it. The `LMAXL=1300` cross-check is **run and opt-in** (`--cutoff`, **measured 1207s a point** against the scan's \$92\$s, \$943\$ modes against \$376\$): \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1=206\$ at both ends, while the HIGHER peaks do move --- so the invariance is the first peak's and not the spectrum's

But in the standard multipole decomposition \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=0\$ carries \$M\$, **\$\textbackslash\{\}ell=1\$ axial carries \$J\$ --- the slow-rotation limit, a STATIONARY solution, non-propagating** --- and only \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\}ge2\$ radiates. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***Applying a principal-series argument there assigns a decay rate to a conserved charge.***  And `P09` already carries the concept as its **"non-radiative skeleton ... the wall is where the field begins to propagate"**; it needed connecting, not inventing).

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_tower_is_robust_to_the_throat_being_squashed}{P15R9}\textbf{P15R9}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_tower\_is\_robust\_to\_the\_throat\_being\_squashed}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`prop:throat`'s equal radii; `sec:coherence`'s tower} \ (**`prop:throat` is a \$J=0\$ construction** --- its argument is "direct from \$f\$ at the Nariai mass", and \$f=1-2GM/c\textasciicircum{}\{2\}r-r\textasciicircum{}\{2\}/\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ is the **non-rotating** metric. ***So the charge whose survival `PO-31` asks about is the one whose presence would deform the geometry the argument is conducted on***, a dependency drawn nowhere. **But the tower is robust**: generalising to \$\textbackslash\{\}nu\textasciicircum{}\{2\}=\textbackslash\{\}tfrac14-\textbackslash\{\}lambda\textbackslash\{\},\textbackslash\{\}ell(\textbackslash\{\}ell+1)\$ with \$\textbackslash\{\}lambda=r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm dS\}\textasciicircum{}\{2\}/r\_\{S\}\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$, ***the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=0\$ base is \$\textbackslash\{\}tfrac14\$ for EVERY \$\textbackslash\{\}lambda\$***, and \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\}ge1\$ stays principal series for any \$\textbackslash\{\}lambda>1/(4\textbackslash\{\}ell(\textbackslash\{\}ell+1))\$ --- \$1/8\$ at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=1\$, so the damping survives until the \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm\{dS\}\_2\$ radius falls below \$0.354\$ of the \$S\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ radius, **a factor of \$2.83\$**). 
\emph{Computes:} \$\textbackslash\{\}lambda=1\$ reproducing `P15`'s tower, the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=0\$ base's independence of \$\textbackslash\{\}lambda\$, and the thresholds at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=1,2,3\$ with the higher multipoles safer

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_rotation_moves_the_throat_away_from_the_damping_threshold}{P15R10}\textbf{P15R10}\quad\texttt{P15\_rotation\_moves\_the\_throat\_away\_from\_the\_damping\_threshold}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\

The family is bounded: **\$a\_\{\textbackslash\{\}max\}=(2-\textbackslash\{\}sqrt3)\textbackslash\{\},\textbackslash\{\}alpha\$ exactly**, attained where \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\_r''=0\$ --- the **ultracold** point where the double root becomes triple. And \$\textbackslash\{\}lambda=1\$ **exactly** at \$a=0\$, rising monotonically with \$a\$, against `r6497`'s threshold of \$1/8\$).

The rotating near-horizon \$S\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ is warped through \$(r\_N\textasciicircum{}\{2\}+a\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\textbackslash\{\}cos\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\textbackslash\{\}theta)\$, so one ratio does not describe it --- `r6497` warned of exactly this. **What is established is the direction and its size at one well-defined point**; for the conclusion to fail the warping must reverse a trend running the right way by a factor of eight

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_warped_throat_keeps_every_mode_above_the_monopole_decaying}{P15R11}\textbf{P15R11}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_warped\_throat\_keeps\_every\_mode\_above\_the\_monopole\_decaying}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`PO-31`; `sec:throat` and `prop:throat`, continuing `r6497`/`r6499`} \ (**THE WARPED HARMONIC PROBLEM, DONE: EVERY MODE ABOVE THE MONOPOLE STAYS PRINCIPAL-SERIES ACROSS THE WHOLE ROTATING FAMILY, AND THE MARGINAL MEMBER IS THE NON-ROTATING ONE.** `r6499` left the ratio read at the POLE as a proxy, warning that the rotating near-horizon sphere is warped through \$r\_N\textasciicircum{}2+a\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}cos\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}theta\$ so one ratio may not describe it and \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ may not be a good label.  **The near-horizon limit is TAKEN rather than posited** and is a warped product, not a direct one: \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\_0\textasciicircum{}2\$ multiplies the \$\textbackslash\{\}dS\_2\$ and polar directions alike and the \$\textbackslash\{\}dS\_2\$ is FIBRED over the sphere by \$\textbackslash\{\}tilde k=2r\_0a\textbackslash\{\}Xi/(r\_0\textasciicircum{}2+a\textasciicircum{}2)\$, non-zero at every rotating member. A massless scalar still separates, the warp cancelling from the relation between the transverse eigenvalue and the \$\textbackslash\{\}dS\_2\$ operator.  **AND FOR THE AXISYMMETRIC MODES THE WARP CANCELS ALTOGETHER**: \$W\textbackslash\{\}propto\textbackslash\{\}sin\textbackslash\{\}theta\$ and \$P\textbackslash\{\}propto\textbackslash\{\}Delta\_\textbackslash\{\}theta\textbackslash\{\}sin\textbackslash\{\}theta\$ exactly, so the \$m=0\$ problem is \$(\textbackslash\{\}Delta\_\textbackslash\{\}theta\textbackslash\{\}sin\textbackslash\{\}theta Y')'+E\textbackslash\{\}sin\textbackslash\{\}theta Y=0\$ --- a SPHEROIDAL deformation of Legendre in which \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\_0\$ never appears. *The order's worry is answered by the structure, not by the numbers.* The decay condition is \$\textbackslash\{\}nu\textasciicircum{}2=\textbackslash\{\}tfrac14-L\textasciicircum{}2E-L\textasciicircum{}4m\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}tilde k\textasciicircum{}2\$, the fibration term non-negative, so rotation can only deepen the damping. **TWO EXACT BOUNDS SETTLE IT WITHOUT A SCAN**: \$L\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}ge1\$ on the physical branch (from \$L\textasciicircum{}2-1=-(3u-1)(u+2)/(3u\textasciicircum{}2+6u-1)\$) and \$E\_1\textbackslash\{\}ge2\$ above the monopole (Rayleigh, \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\_\textbackslash\{\}theta\textbackslash\{\}ge1\$ pointwise), each with equality only at \$a=0\$ --- so \$L\textasciicircum{}2E\_1\textbackslash\{\}ge2\$ against the threshold \$\textbackslash\{\}tfrac14\$, **a factor of eight, attained where the rotation vanishes**. \item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_growth_root_on_the_leaf_rate_is_not_a_moment_balance_and_the_gap_stands}{P15R13}\textbf{P15R13}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_growth\_root\_on\_the\_leaf\_rate\_is\_not\_a\_moment\_balance\_and\_the\_gap\_stands}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:discussion} \ (**PO-38: THE MOMENT-BALANCE FORM DOES NOT SURVIVE ON THE LEAF RATE, THE ROOT BARELY MOVES, AND IT MOVES THE OPPOSITE WAY FROM WHAT THE CONTINUED INTEGRAL IMPLIES.** PO-38 asked for the growth normalisation root re-solved on the LEAF rate and warned that radiation may break the cancellation that made the growth equation parameter-free. ** (1) IT DOES, EXACTLY. ** The derivation rests on "the decaying mode IS the rate"; applying the growth operator to D = H with radiation present gives **L[H] = (32/3) pi G H rho\_r**, which vanishes iff rho\_r = 0. With that gone the reduction of order, the G'/F' identity and the balance all go. Om\_m = sech\textasciicircum{}2(u) fails on the leaf rate (9.3e-4 at v=0.2) and the balance integral returns -1.71e-4 at the leaf root where the stacking one vanishes to 1e-13. **alpha still cancels**, so the equation stays scale-free but acquires Om\_r: the loss is parameter-freedom, not scale-freedom. ** (2) WHAT REPLACES IT ** is the growth ODE solved on the rate, with the Meszaros mode 1 + (3/2)y as an EXACT initial condition (verified to 1.6e-12) and the published (2/5)a/Om convention calibrated against the closed form where the closed form is valid (2e-12). ** (3) THE ROOT: OUTCOME 3, WITH A SIGN. ** Leaf **u\_0 = 1.180038880** against the stacking 1.180309372 -- a move of **1.1\% of the gap**, taking the offset from **0.704 to 0.713 sigma** against the corpus's measured x\_0 = 1.6648 +/- 0.0467. **The 2\% is not an artefact of the rate; the gap stands and wants a mechanism** -- and the shift goes FURTHER from the measurement, not closer. ** (4) THE CONTINUED INTEGRAL GETS THE DIRECTION BACKWARDS. ** The route behind the recorded 0.99934 moves the root TOWARD the measurement (0.693 sigma) where solving the equation moves it away, and by 1.7x the size in u\_0. **That figure does not measure how far the root moves; it measures a closed form failing on a rate it does not solve.** ** (5) THE PIVOT SPLITS RATHER THAN MOVES. ** Om\_m = 2/3 is the acceleration turnover only when matter and Lambda are the whole content; radiation decelerates too, so q = 0 needs Om\_m + 2 Om\_r = 2 Om\_Lambda. On the leaf rate the two loci separate by du = 5.08e-4 and **the Nariai radius sits BETWEEN them**. ** (6) AND A FLATNESS SLIP IN THE EXISTING SCOPE-(b) CHECK: ** its leaf integrand carries Om\_Lambda = 1 - Om rather than 1 - Om - Om\_r, so the fractions sum to 1 + Om\_r; kept flat its own figure reads 0.99940 and the slip alone is 10.4\% of the shift it is quoted for.). 
\emph{Computes:} L[H] asserted symbolically equal to (32/3) pi G H rho\_r and asserted to vanish at rho\_r = 0; the ODE asserted to reproduce the published integral to 1e-10 under the (2/5)a/Om convention; the Meszaros form asserted EXACT to 1e-9 with Lambda off; the stacking root asserted to reproduce the published 1.180309372; the leaf shift asserted under 2\% of the gap; the leaf root asserted FURTHER from the measurement and the continued integral asserted CLOSER -- the two signs asserted opposite; the recorded 0.99934 asserted to be the flatness-slipped value and 0.99940 the flat one, with the slip asserted comparable to the effect; Om\_m = sech\textasciicircum{}2 u asserted to FAIL on the leaf rate; the balance asserted to vanish on the stacking rate and NOT on the leaf; the stacking pivot asserted to be r\_N exactly, the leaf pivot and turnover asserted distinct, and r\_N asserted to lie between them. **CONTROL THAT FIRES: the (2/5) convention and the Meszaros IC are each checked against an independently known answer before either is used** -- the closed form where it is valid, and the analytic 1 + (3/2)y -- so a normalisation error would surface before it could move the root
 \emph{Bound:} ** COMPUTES A CONSEQUENCE, NOT AN ASSIGNMENT. ** It does not claim the leaf rate is the right rate for this object; the corpus's rule assigns it and this computes what follows. Om\_r is held FIXED at 8.5e-5 throughout and NOTHING is tuned. **No mechanism for the residual 2\% is offered** -- the row asked whether the rate explains it and the answer is that it does not. NOT CLAIMED: that the balance has any leaf-rate analogue; what is shown is that this one does not hold there

SUPERSEDED at c54.137 (`P15\_the\_progenitor\_vacuum\_is\_negligible\_too`): F is computed, it is a function of TWO moduli rather than one, and it carries no fixed normalisation --- the incoming amplitude is a classical datum, so the relation cannot be inverted. The hbar asymmetry itself stands unchanged.** Second handle on the same object: the spectral bound constrains total expansion past equality, this constrains the mass, and the recollapse condition caps that mass below Nariai.).

\textit{sec:amplitude} \ (**THE PROGENITOR'S OWN VACUUM, CARRIED THROUGH THE FULL PASSAGE AND AMPLIFIED BY THE BRANCH-POINT MIXING, IS ALSO NEGLIGIBLE --- SHORT BY 1e104. So `prop:amplitude` had ruled out the one vacuum the inheritance story was NOT claiming.** The collapse-side background is a single function, a = M(rho x + x\textasciicircum{}2/2) with x = \textbar{}eta - eta\_c\textbar{}, whose Mukhanov-Sasaki potential 2/(x(x+2 rho)) is **M-FREE**, so the mass enters only through the final division zeta = v/a. The passage has three stages and each contributes one factor of 1/rho: (i) the mode freezes on the beta = 2 leg as v -> D\_k/x with D\_k = sqrt(hbar/2) k\textasciicircum{}(-3/2); (ii) **the connection through equality is elementary --- v = a int dx/a\textasciicircum{}2 solves the k = 0 problem exactly and carries D\_k/x to the constant (3/2 rho)D\_k**; (iii) the crunch monodromy leaks it into the survivor with coefficient 2 pi/rho; and a'(eta\_c) = -M rho supplies the third. **THE TRANSFER LAW IS THEREFORE P = 18 k\textasciicircum{}3 D\_k\textasciicircum{}2/(M\textasciicircum{}2 rho\textasciicircum{}6), with the k\textasciicircum{}3 cancelling identically on vacuum data --- exactly scale-invariant.** On the vacuum that is A\_s = (9/4)(l\_P/M)\textasciicircum{}2 rho\textasciicircum{}(-6) = 2e-113 at the recollapse cap and the determined rho, against an observed 2.1e-9. **The mixing buys eight orders over the bare vacuum and is still short by a hundred and four.** Two corollaries: A\_s does NOT measure the progenitor's mass, D\_k being free; and the classical datum has a size --- the parent's growing-mode amplitude exceeded its own vacuum's by \textasciitilde{}1e47. **The same closed form also returns c54.131's monodromy analytically, the (x/rho)ln x term continuing to -2 pi i/rho, where it had been obtained by numerical continuation.**). 
\emph{Computes:} the M-free potential asserted symbolically; the connection coefficient asserted in closed form and against integration from vacuum data to 0.10\% over a decade in rho and a factor 15 in k; the transfer law asserted k-independent; the shortfall asserted between 1e101 and 1e106
 \emph{Bound:} **bound: z = a with c\_s = 1 is assumed on the beta = 2 leg --- c54.122's own hypothesis and the standard matter-contraction idealisation, genuine dust having c\_s = 0 and no vacuum oscillation to normalise. The 1e104 is far too large for any O(1) convention or plausible c\_s treatment to touch**
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_progenitor\_vacuum\_is\_negligible\_too.py}

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_spacing_deficit_survives_the_one_fitted_parameter}{P15R93}\textbf{P15R93}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_spacing\_deficit\_survives\_the\_one\_fitted\_parameter}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:tensions} \ (**THE LAST FREE THING UPSTREAM IS THE CORPUS'S OWN ONE FITTED NUMBER, AND MOVING IT BY 31\% MOVES THE SPACING DEFICIT BY 7\%.** Front \#2, lead `L-504`. c54.188 closed both seam-datum freedoms and named this: **`Z\_START` is solved so that l\_A = pi\ensuremath{\cdot}D\_M/r\_s hits a TARGET rather than coming out as an output.** *That is declared, not hidden --- P15 sec:tensions says "the measured acoustic scale is reproduced at the directly measured H\_0 by a single z\_onset" and calls it **"the one fitted number"**. What had not happened is that the number was a literal buried in a `brentq` call, so nothing downstream could vary it and nothing had.* **PART 1 MOVING THE PIN MOVES EVERYTHING IT SHOULD:** pinning l\_A at 260/280/301.6/320/340 drives **z\_onset from 11009 to 5066 and r\_s from 157.1 to 120.2 Mpc**. **PART 2 AND THE SPACING DEFICIT DOES NOT FOLLOW IT:** the mean peak spacing stays at **0.7647--0.8205 of l\_A --- a spread of 1.07 against the pin's 1.31, mean 0.798**. *So the \textasciitilde{}20\% spacing deficit is a property of the construction and not of the value its one fitted parameter was fitted to.* **PART 3** l\_1/l\_A runs 0.5176--0.6308 (1.22x) and **never approaches the sky's 0.7312** --- and its drift is almost entirely l\_A's, the peak itself moving only 164 -> 176 while l\_A moves 260 -> 340. **PART 4 THE STRUCTURAL STATEMENT UNDER IT:** in ordinary acoustics l\_1 goes as D\_M/r\_s, so a 24\% fall in r\_s should raise l\_1 by 31\%; **here it raises it by 7\%**. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***This construction's peaks track its own sound horizon at 24\% of the rate acoustics requires, and that --- rather than any one ratio --- is what the comparison with the sky is registering.***  ***CORRECTED c54.190 (`L-505`): the spacing figure in this row is the mean of the FIRST THREE GAPS, which is all four peaks at LMAXL = 1000 can give. At production depth the asymptotic spacing is 0.975 of l\_A --- 2.5\% short, not 21\% --- and what is wrong is the ACOUSTIC PHASE, by 0.62 pi. The robustness this row measures stands; the name it gives the quantity does not.***).

\item[\rcptlabel{C59_the_control_reproduces_camb_and_the_height_defect_was_k_truncation}{P15R107}\textbf{P15R107}\quad\texttt{C59\_the\_control\_reproduces\_camb\_and\_the\_height\_defect\_was\_k\_truncation}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:coherence} \ (**PO-24 STEP ONE: THE CONTROL RUN, AND IT COMES OUT THE OTHER WAY. THE HEIGHT MACHINERY IS NOT BROKEN.** `PO-24` records the bespoke transfer as getting the positions right and the HEIGHTS wrong by 22.7\% and 97.5\% on the arm whose answer is known, and the r3869 handoff named the live lead: two projection paths that are not the same physics, with **which one produced the 2.721 not established**. ** (1) IT WAS los\_spectrum, AND THAT IS THE SMALLER HALF. ** `\_project` also carries the polarisation source g Pi/4 + (3/4k\textasciicircum{}2) d\textasciicircum{}2[g Pi], so the comparison to CAMB was not like for like. ** (2) THE LARGER HALF NOBODY HAD LOOKED FOR: THE k-INTEGRAL IS TRUNCATED WHERE IT IS NOT CONVERGED. ** The instrument built its k-grid from LMAXL -- the grid of multipoles to PRINT -- so choosing what to report chose where to stop integrating C\_l = INT P(k) Delta\_l(k)\textasciicircum{}2 dk. Reported ell grid held FIXED and k\_max alone moved: **2.721 -> 2.446 -> 2.399 -> 2.393**. ** (3) BOTH ARE NEEDED AND NEITHER ALONE SUFFICES. ** los\_spectrum at converged k\_max gives 2.393; \_project at truncated k\_max gives 2.516; both together give **2.197**. ** (4) THE RESULT: THE CONTROL REPRODUCES CAMB. ** Peaks **220 / 540 / 812** against the sky 220.6 / 538.1 / 809.8, **P1/P2 = 2.197 against CAMB 2.200 -- 0.14\%** and inside the sky's 1 sigma; P1/P3 = 2.192 against 2.277. Converged in k\_max and stable to 2e-3 over a 1.5-fold change in mode count. ** (5) THE FORK IS SETTLED: REPAIR. ** The two-arm architecture carries the transfer; a fresh build would have discarded a working instrument on the strength of an artefact. ** (6) AND THE RECORDED LN SCAN IS INERT: ** LN=12 and LN=25 give identical ratios to 1e-3 with the state vector genuinely wider, against a recorded 4.496 -> 8.009.). 
\emph{Computes:} the recorded 2.721/4.496 asserted REPRODUCED (so the row is not wrong about what it ran); k-truncation asserted to move both ratios on ONE path; the polarisation source asserted to move them again at converged k\_max; NEITHER-ALONE asserted; the headline asserted within 1\% of CAMB; every peak asserted within 9 of the sky's; convergence asserted to 2e-3 across NK 280 vs 420; Jac\_of asserted == 1 to 1e-12 on the control so r3512's HIER composition defect cannot reach this result; LN inertness asserted with the state width checked. **CONTROL THAT FIRES: at the TRUNCATED k\_max the same NK widening is EQUALLY STABLE and equally WRONG** -- so stability in the knob you varied is not convergence, which is the defect this receipt removes
 \emph{Bound:} ** CONTROL ARM ONLY. No CR number is produced or corrected. ** The CR arm is truncated by the same mechanism and is NOT re-measured; r3512's HIER composition defect is a no-op on the control (Jac == 1) and stays LIVE for CR, which the next step must settle first. NOT CLAIMED: that the bespoke transfer is finished, that PO-24 is closed, or anything about CR's peak heights. What is established is that its control works
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/C59\_the\_control\_reproduces\_camb\_and\_the\_height\_defect\_was\_k\_truncation.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{C60_the_hier_composition_defect_names_two_flags_its_own_tree_never_had}{P15R108}\textbf{P15R108}\quad\texttt{C60\_the\_hier\_composition\_defect\_names\_two\_flags\_its\_own\_tree\_never\_had}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:coherence} \ (**THE DEFECT FOUR DOCUMENTS DEFER TO IS NOT IN THE INSTRUMENT, AND WAS NOT IN THE ONE IT WAS WRITTEN AGAINST.** `C59` closed PO-24's control step and deferred the CR arm to r3512's `HIER` composition defect -- "the first thing the next step must settle" -- and THE\_REGISTER, INDEX and both appendices carry that deferral. ** (1) THE REMEDY NAMES TWO FLAGS ITS OWN TREE NEVER HAD. ** r3512's fix is "give the hierarchy's gravitational source the stacking clock and its diffusion the leaf", i.e. teach `HIER=1` about `SRCSTACK` and `DIFFLEAF`; both occur **zero** times in ACOUSTIC\_two\_arm.py at 95559d53, the commit r3512 IS, whose 42 os.environ flags are listed. They were real on **6beeca84** (SRCSTACK x13, DIFFLEAF x3), which is **not an ancestor** of 95559d53 -- two nodes, two lines, one instrument. ** (2) THE ASYMMETRY IT COUNTED IS `sound\_phase`. ** "The main path references two" counts evolve's RHS plus `sound\_phase`, which is CALLED ONCE in the file, from inside `qscan()`, the QSCAN=1 diagnostic that computes no spectrum. Like for like: each ODE right-hand side carries the clock **once**, and **neither projection carries it at all** -- true at HEAD and at 95559d53 alike. ** (3) THE TWO RIGHT-HAND SIDES ARE THE SAME BYTES. ** Lines 576 and 959 are character-identical, as are the rate selections at 536 and 916, and every clock-or-source operation in `evolve` is in `evolve\_hier`. ** (4) AND IT MOVES: ** on ARM=cr toggling LEAFPERT changes evolve\_hier's returned state by 2.0 relative, so the hierarchy is not ignoring the clock. **=> C59's deferral is discharged; what gates the CR arm is convergence in k, not a composition fix.**). 
\emph{Computes:} SRCSTACK and DIFFLEAF asserted absent at BOTH 95559d53 and HEAD, with the 42-flag inventory printed; 6beeca84 asserted to carry them and asserted NOT an ancestor of 95559d53; 95559d53 asserted an ancestor of HEAD; the Jac\_of call sites located by enclosing function at both commits with the RHS count asserted 1 and 1 and the projections asserted 0 and 0; sound\_phase's single call site asserted inside qscan's line span; lines 576/959 and 536/916 asserted character-identical; the operation set of evolve asserted a subset of evolve\_hier's; Jac\_of asserted != 1 on ARM=cr and the LEAFPERT toggle asserted to move evolve\_hier's state. **CONTROL THAT FIRES: the receipt EXITS 1 rather than passing if 95559d53 is not in the clone** -- a shallow checkout makes every PART 1 assertion vacuous, which is the hollow-assertion failure the fork names
 \emph{Bound:} ** ESTABLISHES AN ABSENCE, NOT A CORRECTNESS. ** That the named defect is not there; NOT that the HIER path is right. No CR number, no peak position and no height ratio is produced. r3512's gate order 1, 2 and 4 -- validate Pi on the control, the PISRC subtraction, the CR run -- are untouched and remain PO-13 Run 1's business. NOT CLAIMED: that r3512 was careless; two lines of work on one file is a corpus-mechanics failure

\item[\rcptlabel{C62_the_diffusion_signature_is_a_wash_and_the_isw_is_not_what_it_turned_on}{P15R109}\textbf{P15R109}\quad\texttt{C62\_the\_diffusion\_signature\_is\_a\_wash\_and\_the\_isw\_is\_not\_what\_it\_turned\_on}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:coherence} \ (**PO-24: THE DIFFUSION-SCALE SIGNATURE IS RELOCATED INTO THE INFERRED TILT --- NEITHER A TT-SHAPE TENSION NOR A WASH.** PO-24, narrowed at r4337, asks what the enlarged r\_D does to the OBSERVED high-ell power, "which turns on a parameter refit and on the early ISW term". ** (1) THE JUDGEMENT: the early ISW is carryable, and the leaf assignment MULTIPLIES it rather than creating it. ** \_rt, which sets the radiation fractions in the perturbation source, is the stack "both arms" by the instrument's own comment, so radiation is in the source under either assignment; measured, the post-recombination ISW is 0.348 of the SW amplitude on the leaf against 0.229 on the stacking rate. ** (2) BUT NOISW REACHED NO REPORTING PATH ** -- read at one site in main()'s analytic block, zero references in los\_spectrum, \_project and hier\_run -- so the switch for the term this row turns on measured nothing. Repaired r4492; asserted at source here. ** (3) THE EARLY ISW IS NOT WHAT A HIGH-ELL QUESTION TURNS ON: ** 3.49 sigma/bin over l = 100-1296 and **0.77 sigma/bin over 700-1296**, propagated against plik\_lite rather than quoted as a fraction. ** (4) THE SIGNATURE THROUGH A REFIT. ** Scaling r\_D by the measured ratio multiplies the damping by a GAUSSIAN in ell; a tilt is a POWER LAW. Imposed on the measured sky and refitted on amplitude and tilt: **0.26 chi\textasciicircum{}2/bin over P15's 185-bin ceiling, and MORE completely absorbed the higher one looks** (0.06/bin over 700-1760, 96\% over 1000-2508) -- because over a restricted range a Gaussian looks like a power law. ** (5) BUT THE ABSORPTION IS NOT FREE: dn\_s = -0.0304 against sigma = 0.00323 derived from the SAME covariance that scores the fit -- a 9.4 sigma cost. ** On the instrument's own DSCAN pair the same fit gives 0.92/bin and 22.7 sigma. **The signature does not vanish under a refit; it MOVES, out of TT shape and into an inferred parameter.**). 
\emph{Computes:} the r4492 repair asserted at source in both paths; r\_D measured from the instrument at 7.1033 and 7.6398 Mpc, +7.55\% raw, with DAMPX asserted to BE that ratio squared; the ISW's imprint asserted larger on the leaf and asserted under 1 sigma/bin at high ell; the 185-bin ceiling asserted; the shape residual asserted under 0.6 sigma/bin on the data route and under 1.1 on both; the tilt cost asserted above 5 sigma on both routes. **CONTROL THAT FIRES: an injected tilt of +0.02 and +0.05 is recovered to within 5\% with >99.9\% absorbed** -- a fitter that reports absorption is shown not to be manufacturing it
 \emph{Bound:} ** THE MAGNITUDE IS NOT ROBUST TO A FACTOR OF THREE AND THE RECEIPT SAYS SO. ** The data-shape route multiplies the final spectrum by the Gaussian; the instrument applies the damping inside the source where it also enters the Doppler term through a derivative, on an unlensed ceiling-limited spectrum. The two differ 3.6x in residual and 2.8x in tilt; the larger is the more faithful route, so the wash is stated at the WEAKER bound and closing the factor of three is named as work not done here. NOT CLAIMED: that the CR arm as a whole is consistent (r4136 has its heights at -20.7\% and -29.2\%); that dn\_s of this size IS a tension, which is a statement about a joint fit this receipt does not perform. **A FORTIORI, BOTH WAYS: on the SHAPE residual, freeing only the named degeneracy is the least absorption a real refit could achieve, so the wash is a bound; on the TILT it is NOT a bound, since more parameters would SHARE the displacement**
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/C62\_the\_diffusion\_signature\_is\_a\_wash\_and\_the\_isw\_is\_not\_what\_it\_turned\_on.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{C61_the_undriven_premise_is_false_on_the_rate_the_framework_assigns_the_perturbations}{P15R110}\textbf{P15R110}\quad\texttt{C61\_the\_undriven\_premise\_is\_false\_on\_the\_rate\_the\_framework\_assigns\_the\_perturbations}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:coherence} \ (**PO-13's DIAGNOSIS RESTS ON A CROSSING CENSUS, AND THE CENSUS COMES OUT THE OTHER WAY ON THE RATE THE FRAMEWORK ASSIGNS THE PERTURBATIONS.** P07 sec:frontiers states "the standard shift that carries it is universal only where every mode crosses the horizon while there is a plasma to be driven, and **on this rate the acoustic modes re-enter above the onset, so none of them does**", and PO13\_WORKING\_STATE gives the same premise as the structural reason the arm cannot reach the sky. **r3409 made LEAFPERT the default: the perturbations run on the LEAF congruence, and Hleaf carries the radiation term where the CR stacking rate does not.** Horizon crossing for the driving is a perturbation-sector event, so the census that decides the diagnosis has to be run on the leaf rate. ** (1) THE LEAF BACKGROUND HAS AN EQUALITY AND THE ONSET PRECEDES IT: ** z\_eq = Om/Or - 1 = **3936** (eta 236.4) against the onset at z = **6761** (eta 180.4). ** (2) THE FIRST PEAK IS SUPER-HORIZON WHEN THE PLASMA STARTS: ** on the leaf, k\_1/aH(onset) = **0.858 < 1** for l\_1 = 204, and it enters at **z = 5590**, above equality -- a radiation-dominated crossing, **while there is a plasma**. ** (3) THE BAND IS NOT A SINGLE MODE: ** every l < 237.7 enters after the onset and **155.6 < l < 237.7** enters in radiation. ** (4) AND THE OTHER RATE GIVES THE OPPOSITE ANSWER: ** on the stacking rate k\_1/aH(onset) = **1.415 > 1** (already inside) and the background carries no radiation term at all, so it has no equality and the sentence is not merely true there but necessary. **=> The diagnosis was stated on the rate that carries the ruler and tested against a spectrum computed on the rate that carries the content.**  And r3733's leaf reading for l=220 reproduces at 0.926 against its 0.92 -- the premise's refutation was already in the record as a caveat.). 
\emph{Computes:} both sentences asserted present verbatim in CR\_framework.tex and PO13\_WORKING\_STATE.md, with the same P07 paragraph asserted to name the leaf congruence; LEAFPERT asserted on and RAD\_IN\_RATE asserted off; the leaf rate asserted to exceed the geometric rate at a=1e-4 (read, not assumed); the onset asserted to precede leaf equality; the after-onset and in-radiation bands computed from the instrument's own aH/c splines and the latter asserted to CONTAIN l=204; l\_1 asserted super-horizon on the leaf and sub-horizon on the stack; the stacking rate asserted equal to Om/a\textasciicircum{}3+OL to 1e-12 at a=1e-6 where a radiation term would have been 254x the rest; r3733's 0.92 asserted reproduced. **CONTROL THAT FIRES: the same census on the STACKING rate returns the diagnosis's own answer** -- so the census is not a construction that refutes everything put to it
 \emph{Bound:} ** REFUTES A PREMISE, DOES NOT MEASURE A MECHANISM. ** No claim that the acoustic residual is explained, reduced or removed, and no measurement of how large the driving these modes receive is -- NODRIVE=1 is what measures that and is not run here. The position deficit is untouched: the converged arm reports l\_1/l\_A = 0.6764 against the sky's 0.7312 whichever way the premise falls. NOT A VERDICT ON P07's SENTENCE: "this rate" is ambiguous in that paragraph between the stacking rate the section is about and the leaf rate the same paragraph names, and the sentence is TRUE on the first; what is established is that the reading supporting the diagnosis is the one the rate rule does not select. NOT CLAIMED: anything about z\_onset as a fitted parameter, the alternation, or the Hubble-tension join P07 makes next
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/C61\_the\_undriven\_premise\_is\_false\_on\_the\_rate\_the\_framework\_assigns\_the\_perturbations.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{C63_no_single_source_term_carries_the_undriven_split_and_the_first_probe_read_an_unwired_knob}{P15R111}\textbf{P15R111}\quad\texttt{C63\_no\_single\_source\_term\_carries\_the\_undriven\_split\_and\_the\_first\_probe\_read\_an\_unwired\_knob}]\hfill\textsf{[PASS]}\\
\textit{sec:coherence} \ (**NO SINGLE LINE-OF-SIGHT SOURCE TERM CARRIES PO-13's 23\% UNDRIVEN SPLIT.** Undriven, the CR arm's first peak sits at 1.1273 of its own angular acoustic scale and the control's at 0.9158 -- a 23\% split with Q=1 on both arms, so the oscillator is excluded and the split is in the projection. The DIFFUSION DAMPING is excluded too: DAMPX=0 removes it entirely and the split WIDENS to 26.0\%. Removing the monopole, the Doppler dipole and the integrated term ONE AT A TIME leaves the split at 23.1\%, 23.8\% and 29.4\% -- it never collapses, and removing the integrated term WIDENS it. So the answer to "Psi, integrated, or Doppler" is NONE OF THE THREE. ALSO PINS THE ROW'S PREMISE: "the same acoustic scale on both arms" is TRUE of the ANGULAR scale l\_A (301.6 vs 301.4, 1 part in 1507) and FALSE of the PHYSICAL sound horizon r\_s (135.46 vs 144.53 Mpc, 6.3\% apart). The l\_A match is a conspiracy -- D\_M differs by 6.2\% too, within 0.07\% of r\_s's own difference -- not a shared scale.). 
\emph{Computes:} eight runs of ACOUSTIC\_two\_arm.py under NODRIVE=1, both arms x (baseline, NOISW=1, DPSRC=0, SWSRC=0), at LMAXL=520 where all eight first peaks reproduce the LMAXL=1300 values exactly; each switch is CALIBRATED before any null is read off it
 \emph{Bound:} A NEGATIVE result: it says where the split is NOT and is not a mechanism. Only SINGLE removals were run, so a cancellation between two terms is not excluded. The damping half of the projection kernel is now excluded and the VISIBILITY half is not: the arms last-scatter at eta = 449.6 and 280.8, a 60\% difference in epoch against 7\% in width, and that is the named unrun candidate. ZSTART is NOT a free knob -- r\_s integrates from the start, so moving it moves the acoustic scale itself (a trial took l\_A from 301.6 to 172.8). Does not touch the driving (DRC/DRE are identically zero under NODRIVE=1). The KCONT=1 sampling control leaves all four CR peaks unchanged, which bounds the sampling shift below one LSTEP=8 grid step (2.65\%) against a 23\% effect -- a bound, not an identity.
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/C63\_no\_single\_source\_term\_carries\_the\_undriven\_split\_and\_the\_first\_probe\_read\_an\_unwired\_knob.py}

Corrected from three agreeing routes to \$r=1.0816\$: the ratio at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_D\$ moves 0.823 \ensuremath{\to} 0.844, at \$1.5\textbackslash\{\}ell\_D\$ 0.645 \ensuremath{\to} 0.682, \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_D(\textbackslash\{\}rm CR)\$ 1281 \ensuremath{\to} 1294. **FIFTEEN receipts carried it --- eight SILENTLY (exiting zero, six of them evaluating the envelope with the wrong value) and seven LOUDLY (failing every full run since r2755 --- and they are the seven that PRODUCED the correction).** CONTROL: C10's gate was built so the paper and the receipt "cannot drift apart silently in either direction", and it worked --- they were consistent with each other and both wrong.).

\item[\label{rcpt:P1_the_pin_test_run_the_spacing_follows_the_pin_so_the_ratio_does_not}\textbf{}\quad\texttt{P1\_the\_pin\_test\_run\_the\_spacing\_follows\_the\_pin\_so\_the\_ratio\_does\_not}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:tensions} \ (THE PIN TEST, RUN --- the runs the observer line's container killed twice. **The peak spacing tracks the one fitted number PROPORTIONALLY** (`LATARG` 280/301.6/320 \ensuremath{\to} mean spacing 229.3/245.3/261.3), so spacing/\ensuremath{\ell}\_A = 0.8190/0.8134/0.8167 --- **0.69\% spread across a 14\% pin range**, against the **13.4\%** a constant-spacing arm would give: a factor of twenty, so the measurement DISCRIMINATES. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **The routed dichotomy is inverted: following the pin is exactly what makes the RATIO --- which is what the deficit is --- independent of the pin. THE DEFICIT SURVIVES THE FIT.** Also: `KCONT=1` at NK=320 returns the same four peaks, discharging the instrument's own standing sampling caveat; and **\ensuremath{\ell}\_1 = 172 at all three pins**, so \ensuremath{\ell}\_1/\ensuremath{\ell}\_A is pin-dependent while the spacing ratio is not. LCDM control on the same grid: 0.9909, reproducing the sky to 6 in \ensuremath{\ell}.). 
\emph{Computes:} COMPUTES: five banked runs parsed from their own printed blocks; the spacing ratio and its spread; the CONSTANT-spacing alternative computed as the control and the discrimination factor between them; the KCONT identity and its sampling-guard margin; \ensuremath{\ell}\_1's invariance and \ensuremath{\ell}\_1/\ensuremath{\ell}\_A's spread; and the LCDM arm checked against the sky before being used (21 checks)
 \emph{Bound:} NOT-A-PAPER-CLAIM --- **and the declaration is deliberate: this is a measurement on the instrument's own fitted parameter, delivered to a routed question, and whether it lands in `sec:tensions` is the observer line's call under `PO-7`.** A node that banked it into the paper on its own initiative would be taking a step toward the verdict the row protects. **bound: this reports a number and does not convert it into a verdict --- `PO-7` is protected.** Not that r2789's 258 was wrong (different grid; the control was re-run here rather than compared across grids). Not that peaks are known better than the \ensuremath{\ell}-grid: `LSTEP=8`, \ensuremath{\pm}4 per peak, \ensuremath{\pm}8 per spacing --- the invariance claim is 20\ensuremath{\times} outside that and the individual spacings are not. Not that the deficit is explained, only that the fitted number does not produce it.
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/L562\_the\_pin\_test/P1\_the\_pin\_test\_run\_the\_spacing\_follows\_the\_pin\_so\_the\_ratio\_does\_not.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_driving_is_bought_by_decay_during_oscillation}{P15R149}\textbf{P15R149}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_driving\_is\_bought\_by\_decay\_during\_oscillation}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:transmission} \ (THE DRIVING SHIFT IS BOUGHT BY POTENTIAL DECAY OCCURRING WHILE A MODE OSCILLATES, so a mode whose oscillation begins after the potential has settled inherits the UNDRIVEN phase. ** Coupled photon/matter/potential solve on a matter-and-radiation background, with the potential closed by the ENERGY CONSTRAINT rather than evolved and the acoustic turnover taken at the first zero of the photon velocity that BEGINS A SEQUENCE SPACED BY pi -- the free oscillator's own spacing, which the undriven gate validates at 1.0000 on both arms. ** SCOPE: the ASYMPTOTIC statement only, Q -> 1 at large k, which is what P15 and P07 assert; NOT the low-k values, and NOT a magnitude (neutrino free-streaming omitted, \textasciitilde{}15\% low). A sign flip in the initial amplitude is a pi phase shift and moves WHICH ZERO IS FIRST by one half-period, so four initial signs agree to 0.012 at k=160). 
\emph{Computes:} COMPUTES: a coupled photon/matter/potential solve on a matter-and-radiation background, the potential closed by the ENERGY CONSTRAINT rather than evolved; the UNDRIVEN gate validated at \$1.0000\$ on both arms before use; the driven arm asserted to FALL toward \$1\$ from above and to reach the free-oscillator value to \$0.02\$ at large \$k\$; the control asserted well below \$1\$ and flat in \$k\$ to \$0.10\$; the two shapes asserted qualitatively distinct by a factor of two in range; and the robustness leg showing a factor of two in the radiation budget barely moves it. Re-run r3537, ALL PASS
 \emph{Bound:} SCOPE, and the receipt states it: the ASYMPTOTIC statement only, \$Q\textbackslash\{\}to1\$ at large \$k\$, which is what `P15` and `P07` assert; **NOT the low-\$k\$ values and NOT a magnitude** --- neutrino free-streaming is omitted, some \$15\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ low
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_driving\_is\_bought\_by\_decay\_during\_oscillation.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{N8_the_transmission_dichotomy_is_a_capacity_dichotomy}{P15R150}\textbf{P15R150}\quad\texttt{N8\_the\_transmission\_dichotomy\_is\_a\_capacity\_dichotomy}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{sec:transmission (prop:transmission, rem:transmission-leg)} \ (P15 proves a transmission dichotomy --- a non-degenerate horizon (p=1) imprints a scale, a degenerate one (p=2) transmits --- and rem:transmission-leg says "the leg multiplies the spectrum by a constant and the seam imprints nothing: neither carries a scale, and a spectrum can only be tilted by something that does". That is a CHANNEL claim, and "carries a scale" is what decides its capacity). 
\emph{Computes:} fits the output tilt on four disjoint k-bands: p=2 returns the same tilt on every band (spread 1.7e-16) while p=1 spreads by 7.93, which IS the scale; the control puts a cutoff into the p=2 kernel by hand and the spread jumps to 1.91, so the test can fail; and the p=1 spread shrinks monotonically 30.2 -> 7.9 -> 2.3 as kappa rises, tracking the kernel's scale
 \emph{Bound:} SUPPORTS P15 --- transparent means the answer does not depend on where you look, and imprinting means it does. NOT a Bogoliubov calculation; no Hawking temperature claimed
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/N8\_the\_transmission\_dichotomy\_is\_a\_capacity\_dichotomy.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_history_is_spherically_symmetric_throughout_so_the_chern_simons_number_never_moves}{P15R151}\textbf{P15R151}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_history\_is\_spherically\_symmetric\_throughout\_so\_the\_chern\_simons\_number\_never\_moves}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\

Which comparison is legitimate, on what run, with the floor beside it} \ (**There are TWO instruments here, and the body's pair is the other one's --- which is why the body and the receipt are both accurate.**  **Q1** `fit.py`+CAMB fits **five parameters on 215 bins** and its CR arm is CAMB's \$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda\$CDM TT ***times an \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$-only damping envelope***; `ACOUSTIC\_two\_arm`+`chi2\_of\_spectrum` fits **one amplitude on 185 covered bins** and its CR arm is the construction's own spectrum. The second is the one to report, and the first is **not malformed** --- it answers the damping question  **Q2** the second gives \$51817.0\$ against \$1320.5\$ --- ***and the crossing configuration was unreachable***, the CR arm's background being written in as literals (\$H\_0=73.00\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}Omega\_m=0.3066\$); `CRH0`/`CROM`/`CROMBH2` exposed, defaults byte-identical  **Q3** \$F\_2=+1114.0\$, \$F\_3=+50496.5\$, forty-five times it --- **and the body's \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2=190.7\$ is *below* that floor**, readable on the first instrument only because its control **is** the CAMB reference so its floor is identically zero  **Q4** the deficit is in the second's spectrum and ***structurally absent*** from the first's  **Q5** ***both of the order's two options at once***: `cr.json` banks a genuine five-parameter optimum at \$H\_0=78.13\$, and `fit.py` shows the shape refitted was CAMB's \$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda\$CDM times the envelope).

\textit{The crossing configuration's spectrum on a SECOND implementation: node 66's code seat integrated it on the polarisation path over 133 bins, and `PO-10`'s row routed the full-spectrum run away from this seat at `r6772+66.36` --- so what is left to ask is whether an independent integration on a different path, a different wavenumber grid and a different bin range returns the same spectrum} \ (**It does, and the agreement is the result rather than any \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$.** Hierarchy path, 185 covered bins, \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ 100--1996, one fitted amplitude, at \$(68.62,\textbackslash\{\},0.2973)\$ with the arm's own handover datum: peaks \$220/540/820/1132\$ against node 66's \$222/538/818/1134\$, heights \$2.273\$/\$2.319\$ against \$2.264\$/\$2.298\$, the \$700\$--\$1000\$ band \$4.02\$ per bin against \$4.23\$ --- ***within one grid step on every peak, within one per cent on the heights, neither seat knowing the other had run it***  **and node 66's fourth-peak excess reproduces at \$1132\$ against \$1123.9\$, which makes it the spectrum's and not either implementation's**  against the configuration this replaces, on the same instrument and bins: \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2 = 1205.4\$ where the pinned arm gives \$51817.0\$ and the control \$1320.5\$, with positions \$-0.3\textbackslash\{\}\%/+0.4\textbackslash\{\}\%/+1.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ where the deficit stood at \$7.5\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- the first two peaks inside one grid step and ***the third at \$1.28\$ steps and not inside it***). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 the banked pair reproduced on this scorer first (\$1320.5\$ and \$51817.0\$) before any new number was read  **W1 the substituted-datum control, which reversed a reading of mine**: the first run carried `CRIC=branchpoint`, the control's super-horizon datum, and repeating it with the arm's own datum left the positions and \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$ alone (\$1191.0\textbackslash\{\}to1205.4\$) while ***moving the heights \$2.152/2.168\textbackslash\{\}to2.273/2.319\$*** --- so "the heights did not come in" was the substitution's artefact and is withdrawn  the amplitude is not doing the work either: the crossing arm wants the **control's** amplitude to \$0.04\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ where the pinned arm wanted one \$8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ away  three spectra banked with their exact commands in `spectra/README.md`
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT a preference over \$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda\$CDM**: \$-115.1\$ against \$F\_2\$'s floor of \$+1114.1\$, a tenth of it, and this control carries its own truncation error (`c54.186`) --- ***the papers quote node 66's lensed \$2.57\textbackslash\{\}times\$ and this receipt quotes nothing against it***, `PO-7` protected exactly there  **NOT the discreteness control**, which is named and in flight rather than omitted: at the run's own `NK=600` a continuum grid gives \$2.8\$ points per Bessel period and ***the instrument refuses to run***, clearing the guard taking `NK=900` and \$2700\$ modes against the ladder's \$1452\$ --- until it lands what stands against aliasing is node 66's independent grid, which is weaker for that question and is stated as weaker  **NOT a discrepancy with the body's comb**: the paper's answer is the **leaf**, so \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A=172.3\$ is the radiation-free ruler and a different object --- that reading of mine is withdrawn here  and the heights are the **path's**, the line-of-sight path overshooting at \$2.543\$/\$3.264\$
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_crossing\_spectrum\_reproduces\_on\_a\_second\_instrument\_and\_the\_172\_is\_the\_radiation\_free\_ruler.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_refit_leaves_the_background_where_the_distances_put_it_and_does_not_close_the_phase}{P15R166}\textbf{P15R166}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_refit\_leaves\_the\_background\_where\_the\_distances\_put\_it\_and\_does\_not\_close\_the\_phase}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\

\emph{Computes:} a measured response rather than a search -- 18 runs giving gradient and diagonal curvature per arm, the amplitude closed-form; the minimum VERIFIED by real runs at the best-fit parameters, predicted 118.2 and 170.6 against measured 118.3 and 171.1; every parameter flatness-tested against a one-step excursion, all four constrained on both arms; and the bin count checked at 132

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_there_is_no_phase_residual_and_the_fourth_peak_is_not_the_damping_envelope}{P15R171}\textbf{P15R171}\quad\texttt{P15\_there\_is\_no\_phase\_residual\_and\_the\_fourth\_peak\_is\_not\_the\_damping\_envelope}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\

\emph{Computes:} one locator applied identically to the sky and both arms -- bin to plik\_lite's bins, convert to D\_l, spline, parabola over a window -- with the window swept over seven values so the locator's own spread is the uncertainty the comparison carries; the locator first shown to reproduce sec:intro's own sky quartet, with the second peak identified as the hardest and its spread the largest; the three procedure mismatches in the earlier reading priced one at a time; the damping test validated by its inverse, imposing the arm's envelope on the control and getting an equal and opposite shift; and the residual decomposed against the arm's own comb width, which accounts for about half of peaks three and four
 \emph{Bound:} the per-cent offset does NOT shrink under the matched procedure -- 3.4 becomes 4.2 -- so what dissolves the residual is the sky's own uncertainty and not the central value, and the receipt says so rather than the other way round; the three leftovers after the comb-width term are 1.4, 0.6 and 1.0 in ell, the same sign and order but not a clean constant, and a finer statement is not available at these locating spreads; order (3), the neutrino sector, is NOT run because the order made it conditional on a uniform phase offset surviving and none does; and nothing here says the arm fits, the verified refit leaving it at 1.58 per bin against 1.01
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_there\_is\_no\_phase\_residual\_and\_the\_fourth\_peak\_is\_not\_the\_damping\_envelope.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_three_quarters_of_the_arms_residual_is_the_controls_and_what_is_left_is_position_not_amplitude}{P15R172}\textbf{P15R172}\quad\texttt{P15\_three\_quarters\_of\_the\_arms\_residual\_is\_the\_controls\_and\_what\_is\_left\_is\_position\_not\_amplitude}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`PO-47`'s successor and the acoustic figure; `sec:refit-bound`} \ (**THREE QUARTERS OF THE ARM'S RESIDUAL IS THE CONTROL'S, AND WHAT IS LEFT IS POSITION RATHER THAN AMPLITUDE --- BUT IT IS UNDER TWO PER CENT OF THE MISFIT.** Whitened against the full bandpower covariance the two residual vectors sit at cosine \$+0.855\$, and ***\$73.1\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of the arm's \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ lies along the control's own direction***, projecting out to leave \$76.2\$ of \$283.0\$ --- stable at \$72.5\$--\$73.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ across three bin cuts.  **WITHDRAWN AT `r6881+cc66.34` --- the inference, not the number.** *This row read the \$73.1\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ as attribution. Both arms are fitted to the same data, so \$r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm CR\}=r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}+(m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm CR\}-m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\})\$ exactly and the statistic ranks MODEL SIMILARITY: a control deliberately tilted by \$\textbackslash\{\}delta n\_s=+0.02\$, at \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2=214.6\$, scores \$82.2\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- higher than the arm. **The corrected reading points the other way: the control already fits at \$0.994\$/bin, so the arm's \$+105.1\$ excess is the construction's.** Everything else in this row stands.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *Of the rest, one further parameter at a time:* **peak-position rescale \$\textbackslash\{\}varepsilon=-7.5\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-4\}\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}\{2\}=+4.68\$**; damping-shape \$+1.36\$; **tilt \$+0.09\$** --- and on the control the position direction buys \$+0.00\$ while the damping buys \$+0.30\$. ***So position is the construction's and the tilt is exhausted*** (the refit spent it: \$\textbackslash\{\}delta n\_s=+0.01\$ **costs** \$+11.2\$).  **But \$\textbackslash\{\}varepsilon\$ is \$0.8\$ multipoles at the fourth peak --- consistent with the \$+2.0\$ at `r6875+cc66.32` --- and \$4.7\$ of \$283\$ is \$1.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$: amplitude, tilt, damping and position together do NOT account for the shape rejection, and this receipt does not say what does.**  *And the quick render's band means were about three times these: the swing's **shape** reproduces (band 4 high, band 5 low, on both arms) and its amplitude does not --- **fitting the amplitude on diagonal errors rather than the covariance puts the arm \$0.83\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ high and roughly doubles band 4**, about half the difference*). 
\emph{Computes:} the banked refit spectra reproducing the refit's own \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ (\$186.51\$ and \$292.42\$ over \$185\$ bins) before anything is read off them; band means in sixths three ways (covariance amplitude with diagonal \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$, whitened, and diagonal amplitude) with the rms growing \$0.78\textbackslash\{\}to1.39\$; the whitened cosine, shared fraction and residue at three bin cuts; the three single-parameter scans on both arms; **and every headline number the figure plots recomputed independently and gated against `cc66\_fig\_acoustic\_numbers.npz` to seven figures, so figure and receipt cannot drift** --- 13 gates
 \emph{Bound:}  **no mechanism is offered for the bulk of the misfit** --- four directions are excluded as the whole of it and none is put in their place; "the transfer's" is the order's phrase for *not construction-specific* and is not a claim about the implementation; the refit minima are `r6825+cc66.25`'s and are used as banked; the low-multipole floor's score is `r6831`'s and untouched; whitening mixes bins, so a whitened band mean is a mean over a rotated basis and is reported **beside** the diagonal one rather than instead of it
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_three\_quarters\_of\_the\_arms\_residual\_is\_the\_controls\_and\_what\_is\_left\_is\_position\_not\_amplitude.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_shared_fraction_does_not_mean_what_i_said_and_the_peak_trough_pattern_is_the_arms_alone}{P15R173}\textbf{P15R173}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_shared\_fraction\_does\_not\_mean\_what\_i\_said\_and\_the\_peak\_trough\_pattern\_is\_the\_arms\_alone}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6881`, `sec:refit-bound`, continuing `r6879+cc66.33`: four properties of the acoustic residual measured by the chat seat **on diagonal errors**, with the burden stated at the outset --- *"all wanting the full covariance behind them"* --- and the four offered *"to be overturned"*, closing on **what sets the \$1.3\$** and on *"if the ratio is not \$1.3\$ on the full covariance, say so plainly"*} \ ( **THE SHARED FRACTION NEVER MEANT WHAT I SAID IT MEANT, AND THE THREE-QUARTERS SENTENCE IS WITHDRAWN.** Both arms are fitted to the SAME data, so ***\$r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm CR\}=r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}+(m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm CR\}-m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\})\$ exactly*** (verified to \$8.9\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-16\}\$) --- the two residuals share the \$-d\$ term by construction, so a "fraction along the control's direction" measures **how alike the two MODELS are** and cannot attribute a misfit.  *The control the statistic needed and never got:* **a flat-\$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda\$CDM control deliberately tilted by \$\textbackslash\{\}delta n\_s=+0.02\$ --- a model nobody believes, at \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2=214.6\$ --- scores \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{82.2\textbackslash\{\}\%\}\$ shared, HIGHER than the arm's \$73.1\textbackslash\{\}\%\$**, and the score falls away with the tilt (\$82\textbackslash\{\}\%\textbackslash\{\}to43\textbackslash\{\}\%\textbackslash\{\}to16\textbackslash\{\}\%\$), which is the signature of a similarity measure. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***And the corrected reading points the OTHER WAY:*** the control already fits at \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.994\}\$/bin, so there is no shared defect for the arm to be an amplification OF; the arm exceeds it by \$+105.1\$ and **\$m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm CR\}-m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}\$ alone carries \$77.3\$ of that** --- ***the excess is the construction's***.  *And of the order's four diagnostics, **three do not survive on the control**:* **(1)** the peak/trough pattern is the ARM'S ALONE --- control \$+0.03/-0.06\$ (diag) and \$+0.03/-0.07\$ (whitened) against the order's \$+0.57/-0.67\$, arm \$+0.36/-0.51\$ and \$+0.42/-0.52\$ ---  *so the composite, "that BOTH arms show it says the bulk belongs to the transfer", **fails at its premise***; **(3)** both do grow with \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ and the first third matches (\$0.67\$), but the control's last third is \$0.88\$ and not \$1.46\$; **(4)** the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\}simeq1000\$ trough is the arm's --- control \$+0.06\$ where the order has \$-1.56\$. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **WHAT DOES REPRODUCE IS THE RMS RATIO, \$1.357\$ against the quoted \$1.34\$ (\$1.261\$ whitened) --- and the order's closing question has a plain answer: nothing amplifies anything.** \$\textbackslash\{\}lvert r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}\textbackslash\{\}rvert=13.34\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}lvert m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm CR\}-m\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}\textbackslash\{\}rvert=8.79\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}lvert r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm CR\}\textbackslash\{\}rvert=16.82\$ --- ***the factor of \$1.3\$ IS the model difference's own size***, \$77.3\$ in \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$.  *What does NOT reproduce alongside it is the correlation: \$0.837\$, not \$0.96\$, so "the same series scaled" is too strong even before the objection to what that would mean*). 
\emph{Computes:} the algebraic identity proved on the actual fitted vectors before any statistic is read off them; both arms rescored from the banked refit spectra; **FOUR CONTROLS built to break the statistic and they do** --- flat \$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda\$CDM tilted by \$\textbackslash\{\}delta n\_s=+0.02,+0.05,+0.10,-0.05\$, the first scoring above the arm and the rest falling away monotonically; each of the four diagnostics computed **twice**, once on a diagonal amplitude and once whitened against `COV\_TT`, with the order's own quoted value printed beside every one; the peak/trough split taken from the sign of the CONTROL's binned \$\textbackslash\{\}mathcal\{D\}\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ curvature for **both** arms so the two are sorted by the same partition --- 14 gates
 \emph{Bound:}   **WITHDRAWING "IT IS THE TRANSFER'S" IS NOT SHOWING THE TRANSFER IS INNOCENT** --- no statistic here separates a transfer defect from a cosmology difference and none is offered; what is established is that the shared fraction was never that statistic  **no mechanism is added** --- `cc66.33` excluded four directions and this excludes none further  the rest of `cc66.33` stands: the band means, the three single-parameter scans and the figure are untouched, and it is the attribution sentence alone that is struck  the diagnostics are computed on this tree's \$179\$ scored bins over \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\}le1900\$, which is the figure's own cut and not the order's; a different cut moves the third decimal and no verdict  using each arm's own curvature for the split is not run, and is named rather than done
