\textit{node 66's work order `r6885`, `sec:refit-bound`, continuing `r6881+cc66.34`: **take the sky's noise realisation out** --- every number in the sector so far compares each arm *to the sky*, and the control fits at \$0.994\$/bin, so the control's residual essentially IS the noise. Four asks: the coefficient hiding inside the \$73\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, the exact three-term decomposition, \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$'s shape *"because it is smooth and readable where a residual is not"*, and a short list of four mechanisms --- *"match, do not fit \ldots{} a negative is worth more than a match reached by adjusting one of them"*} \ ( **THE MODEL DIFFERENCE IS AN ACOUSTIC *CONTRAST* DIFFERENCE, AND THE REJECTION IS NOT ONE SKY'S LUCK.** \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm arm\}=\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}+2\textbackslash\{\}langle r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\},\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rangle+\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}2\$ exactly: \$282.96 = 177.88 + 27.82 + 77.26\$, so ***\$\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}2\$ carries \$74\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of the excess and the cross term \$26\textbackslash\{\}\%\$*** --- the order's conditional does not fire. **Set the cross term to its expectation of zero and the arm still scores \$256.3\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{1.43\}\$ per bin on a TYPICAL sky** against the control's \$0.994\$.  *And the \$1.15\$ is \$1.078\$: the order's coefficient multiplies the WHITENED cosine \$0.855\$ by the DIAGONAL rms ratio \$1.34\$; consistently it is \$1.078\$ whitened or \$1.137\$ diagonal.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **More than that, \$b-1\$ IS \$\textbackslash\{\}langle\textbackslash\{\}Delta,r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}\textbackslash\{\}rangle/\textbackslash\{\}lVert r\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm ctl\}\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ --- the "amplification along the noise direction" and the cross term are ONE NUMBER seen twice --- and against its own sky-random null it is \$1.078\textbackslash\{\}pm0.049\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{+1.58\textbackslash\{\}sigma\}\$**, stable at \$+1.07\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ to \$+1.58\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ across four bin cuts: *a \$1.6\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ departure from \$1.00\$ is what noise looks like, so there is no amplification for the order's two candidate explanations of it to act on.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND WHAT \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ IS:** it is **trough-dominated** --- \$+0.011\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ at the peaks against \$-0.760\$ at the troughs, so *the two arms agree where the peaks are and differ between them* --- and split about a smooth envelope the **oscillatory part carries \$67\$--\$75\$ of the \$77.3\$**, peak-high and trough-low (\$+0.389/-0.420\$), antisymmetric about the envelope and not about each peak. ***That is a CONTRAST, not a phase and not an envelope: the arm's acoustic oscillation is \$1.040\$ times the control's about its own envelope.***  One direction built from the CONTROL alone --- raise the oscillation at fixed envelope, no run and nothing fitted --- carries **\$51\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of \$\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}2\$** (\$46\$--\$51\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ across a factor two in its one window), and with position, tilt and damping the span reaches \$66.8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ against **\$5.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ for the whole of `cc66.33`'s four-parameter family** --- *which is what "amplitude, tilt, damping and position together do not account for the shape rejection" was pointing at: the family was missing a direction and the direction has a name.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **THE SHORT LIST GIVES THE NEGATIVE THE ORDER SAID IT WOULD RATHER HAVE.** The handover amplitude (\$0.4835\$ against \$0.5\$) is **excluded EXACTLY and without a run** --- the instrument sets \$\textbackslash\{\}hat\textbackslash\{\}Theta\$ \$k\$-independently, so the single fitted amplitude absorbs it and \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$ moves by \$9\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-13\}\$. Switching each of the other three off on **both** arms leaves \$93.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ (neutrino depth \$LN\textbackslash\{\},12\textbackslash\{\}to24\$), \$92.8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ (early ISW) and \$87.6\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ (the driving's continuity half) of \$\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}2\$; the driving's **Euler** half takes it to \$68.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ but at cosine \$-0.16\$, so  *\$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ is REPLACED rather than reduced and the \$32\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ is a norm and not a share*. ***Not one of them removes the contrast*** (\$1.038\$--\$1.044\$ against \$1.040\$). \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND A FIFTH, WHICH THE SHAPE NAMES BY ITSELF AND WHICH THE INSTRUMENT'S OWN DOCSTRING ALREADY DESCRIBES**: a mis-weighted Doppler term *"fills the troughs at high multipole \ldots{} while leaving the FIRST peak's position almost alone"*.  *Its knob is a **KNOB SHADOW** on the refit path --- `\_SWSRC` and `\_DPSRC` are read only inside `los\_spectrum`, `HIER=1` does not take that path, and `DPSRC=0` at the refit configuration returns a **BIT-IDENTICAL** spectrum on both arms.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} Tested where the knob reaches: **removing the Doppler dipole nearly doubles each arm's oscillation about its own envelope (to \$1.85\$) and its shape sits at cosine \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.88\}\$--\$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.89\}\$ with the contrast direction --- the Doppler dipole IS the contrast knob** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  *and the MONOPOLE brackets it from the other side --- `SWSRC=0` **inverts** the oscillation, to \$-0.300\$, at cosine \$-0.79\$, so **the contrast direction is the monopole-to-dipole BALANCE**, which neither switch alone would have established* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND `r6887`'s SHARPENED ASK --- \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ read against the three features `cc66.34` proved are the arm's own --- comes back: ALL THREE ARE \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$'S.** *The trough deficit (\$-0.760\$ against the control's \$-0.200\$); the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\}simeq1000\$ trough, where the control sits at \$-0.103\$, the arm at \$-1.177\$ and **\$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ carries \$-1.074\$, \$91\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of it, in \$15\$ bins**; and the growth with multipole, which \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ does **monotonically** (\$0.33\textbackslash\{\}to0.55\textbackslash\{\}to0.68\$) where the residuals do not --- because a residual is \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ plus a noise floor of order one and the floor flattens it.*  *`r6887` also voids its own part (1); the coefficient is reported anyway because it had been run and what it shows is not the coefficient as evidence but that it **IS** the cross term*). 
\emph{Computes:} the three-term identity proved on the actual fitted vectors before anything is read off it, at four bin cuts; the coefficient shown to be the cross term ALGEBRAICALLY (to \$3\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-16\}\$) rather than numerically alongside it; the handover-amplitude exclusion done by rescaling the banked spectrum and refitting, so it is measured and not argued; the contrast direction built **twice**, from a polynomial envelope in \$\textbackslash\{\}ln\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ (three degrees) and from a running geometric mean over one acoustic period (four windows), with its one tunable reported over a factor two rather than chosen; **the candidate test chosen to be the right one and the wrong one printed beside it** --- a knob's per-arm shape can sit at cosine \$-0.63\$ with \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ and still carry none of it, because a knob that does the same thing to both arms CANCELS in the difference, so the column is \$\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}2\$ with the knob off; **the knob shadow PROVED rather than inferred** --- a static check that `\_DPSRC` occurs once and inside `los\_spectrum`, a bit-identical pair on the refit path, and a \$62\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ move on the path where it is wired, which is the calibration r4558's own note demands before any null is read off a switch; and \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ cross-checked between the two instrument paths at cosine \$+0.76\$ with the same contrast excess --- **38 gates**, four candidate pairs and three line-of-sight pairs banked at `spectra/r6885\_*` with their commands
 \emph{Bound:}  **NAMING THE SHAPE IS NOT NAMING THE MECHANISM** --- "the arm's oscillation is \$4\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ larger at fixed envelope" is a measurement, and the channel is identified rather than the cause measured: deleting the Doppler term is all-or-nothing, it leaves the arms' contrast RATIO where it was and DOUBLES \$\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}2\$, so the arms differ in HOW MUCH that channel supplies and not in whether it is there  **\$33\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of \$\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}2\$ is unnamed by any direction here**  switching a driving or source term off is a LARGE excursion and not a derivative: both arms move far from their own minima, so the candidate column answers "does \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ survive this" and not "this term contributes so much of it"  the \$LN\$ result is a DIRECTION and not a convergence test --- doubling the depth removes \$6.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ and the sign says more depth removes more  the line-of-sight path's \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$ is **not** comparable with the hierarchy path's, as this tree's own `spectra/README.md` records, so only shapes and within-path ratios are read from PART 5  nothing here bears on whether the transfer or the cosmology is at fault --- `r6881+cc66.34` withdrew the statistic that was read that way and this adds no replacement  **the knob shadow is reported, NOT repaired**: wiring a source term is a change to the instrument
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_model\_difference\_is\_an\_acoustic\_contrast\_difference\_and\_it\_is\_not\_one\_skys\_luck.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_source_decomposition_is_reachable_on_the_reporting_path_and_the_neutrinos_have_a_knob}{P15R175}\textbf{P15R175}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_source\_decomposition\_is\_reachable\_on\_the\_reporting\_path\_and\_the\_neutrinos\_have\_a\_knob}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6889`, `sec:refit-bound`, continuing `r6885+cc66.35`: **the knob shadow, before anything else.** That receipt found `\_SWSRC`/`\_DPSRC` read only inside `los\_spectrum`, so `HIER=1` --- the path every refit number in this sector is computed on --- was blind to them. The order: *"make the source decomposition reachable on the hierarchy path, or establish that it cannot be and say why,"* then re-run the two bracketing tests there; and on the free-streaming phase shift, *"either a knob is buildable --- in which case say what it would cost --- or the phase shift cannot be tested on this instrument at all"*} \ ( **THE SHADOW IS CLOSED, AND THE DEFAULT IS PROVED RATHER THAN ASSERTED.** `\_SWSRC` and `\_DPSRC` now reach all three source constructions --- `los\_spectrum`, the hierarchy path and the low-multipole path --- where they reached one; the monopole bracket is **split** so the switch multiplies \$g(\textbackslash\{\}Theta\_0+\textbackslash\{\}Psi)\$ and not the polarisation term that shares it. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The unset configuration reproduces the banked spectra with \$\textbackslash\{\}max\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}mathcal\{D\}\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\}rvert = \textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.0\}\$ on BOTH arms --- exactly zero, not nearly*** --- so nothing this sector has reported moves.  *And that took a second attempt, which is on the record: written `\_SWSRC * g\_ * (\textbackslash\{\}Theta\_0+\textbackslash\{\}Psi) + \textbackslash\{\}ldots` the default sums in a different **order**, and floating-point addition is not associative --- measured, \$1.1\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-16\}\$. Keeping the factor INSIDE the bracket makes it exact, because \$x\textbackslash\{\}times1.0\$ is.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE PAIR THAT MAKES THE SHADOW A PROOF RATHER THAN A STORY**: the same switch, the same two commands, **exactly \$0.0\$ before the repair and \$62.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$/\$61.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ after** --- *a null and an unwired knob are indistinguishable until both halves are on the record, which is `r4558`'s own rule and the reason this revision exists.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **THE TWO BRACKETING TESTS SURVIVE THE MOVE TO THE REPORTING PATH**: `DPSRC=0` at cosine \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{+0.798\}\$ with the contrast direction (LOS: \$+0.893\$), taking the arm's own oscillation to \$1.828\$ (LOS: \$1.855\$); `SWSRC=0` at \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{-0.765\}\$ (LOS: \$-0.791\$), **inverting** it to \$-0.399\$ (LOS: \$-0.300\$) --- ***the same opposite-sign bracket, so `cc66.35`'s reading that the contrast direction IS the monopole-to-dipole balance was not an artefact of the path it had to be measured on.***  *The two deletions are not equally inert on the arms' contrast ratio and that is reported rather than averaged: the dipole moves it by \$-0.0025\$ and the monopole by \$+0.042\$, and neither collapses it toward \$1.000\$.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE FREE-STREAMING PHASE SHIFT NOW HAS A KNOB --- the answer to "what would it cost" is FOUR LINES.** What makes a neutrino free-stream is its anisotropic stress \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\_\textbackslash\{\}nu=F\_2/2\$, at exactly two dynamical sites (the Euler equation and \$\textbackslash\{\}Psi\$'s shear term), so `NUFS` multiplies \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\_\textbackslash\{\}nu\$ and the \$F\_2\$ source on both solver paths; **the quadrupole's initial condition is exactly zero**, so at `NUFS=0` it is never sourced, the whole \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\}ge2\$ ladder stays zero and the sector is a perfect fluid at the SAME background density --- `FNU` and the density fractions untouched. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***It moves \$\textbackslash\{\}mathcal\{D\}\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ by \$36.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$/\$37.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ and pushes EVERY peak on BOTH arms to LARGER \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$, \$+9.00\$ uniformly across the first four*** --- which is the sign the Bashinsky--Seljak pull has and the shape a **phase** shift has rather than a rescaling of the acoustic scale.  *Its size is exactly one binned grid step (\$8.9999\$), so the sign and order of magnitude are established and the value is not*). 
\emph{Computes:} the shadow stated as a property of the SOURCE TEXT and not of a memory --- `\_DPSRC` occurring once, inside `los\_spectrum`, before the repair --- beside the banked bit-identical pair that found it, re-gated here; the repair's default gated at **exactly zero** on both arms and on identical multipole grids, before any other number in the receipt is read; **each newly wired switch calibrated where it was newly wired**, because `r4558`'s own note is the rule this revision enforces and a new knob that had not been shown to move something would be the same defect landed twice; the `NUFS` claim carried by three static checks rather than by assertion --- four use sites, `y0 = np.zeros(...)` with no assignment to the quadrupole anywhere in the file, and no `\_NUFS` within the density-fraction block, which is what makes `NUFS=0` a perfect fluid rather than a frozen state; and the peak shift gated on its **sign** and its **uniformity across the first four peaks** rather than on its size, with the size then shown to be one grid step --- 22 gates, four pairs banked at `spectra/r6889\_*` with the launcher at `computations/beyond\_the\_wall/r6889\_directions/`
 \emph{Bound:}  **NO MECHANISM FOR THE CONTRAST IMBALANCE** --- `r6889` said that boundary is right and is not moving it, and neither does this  **and no VALUE for the phase shift as a result about this construction**: the knob is built and calibrated, its size is grid-limited, and pointing it at the question is the next order's  the drag half --- the amplitude suppression free-streaming also causes, \$36\$--\$37\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ in \$\textbackslash\{\}mathcal\{D\}\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ --- is the large effect and must not be quoted as the same number as the phase shift  the low-multipole path is wired for consistency and is **not exercised** here; naming it rather than quietly leaving it is the point  **no other knob is audited**: three shadows have now been found in this sector by three different routes, and nothing here says there is not a fourth
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_source\_decomposition\_is\_reachable\_on\_the\_reporting\_path\_and\_the\_neutrinos\_have\_a\_knob.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_monopole_zero_point_is_not_the_channel_and_the_two_residuals_point_opposite_ways}{P15R176}\textbf{P15R176}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_monopole\_zero\_point\_is\_not\_the\_channel\_and\_the\_two\_residuals\_point\_opposite\_ways}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6897`: **is the contrast excess and the alternation excess one number?** --- *"do not remove a term; measure a quantity both arms have"*: **(a)** the value the monopole oscillation swings **about** at last scattering, in units of that arm's own \$\textbackslash\{\}Psi\$; **(b)** what each arm's own loading \$R=3\textbackslash\{\}rho\_b/4\textbackslash\{\}rho\_\textbackslash\{\}gamma\$ at its own visibility peak accounts for; **(c)** *"from the residual alternation alone, solve for the effective \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta R\$ that would produce it; from the residual contrast alone, solve for the effective \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta R\$ that would produce it --- do the two agree?"*, the order adding that **no mechanism is being asked for and none should be claimed**, and asking for `ETA\_LS\_W` in the header while I was in there} \ ( **THE MONOPOLE'S ZERO POINT IS NOT THE CHANNEL, AND THE SIGN IS WHAT SAYS SO.** On both arms the offset of \$\textbackslash\{\}Theta\_0+\textbackslash\{\}Psi\$ approaches the tight-coupling equilibrium \$-R\textbackslash\{\}Psi\$ **from below**, reaching \$0.78\$ of it on the control and \$0.76\$ on the arm at the visibility peak --- *so (b)'s absolute question answers NO on both arms and by nearly the same amount, which is the approach to equilibrium and not a property of either arm: \$\textbackslash\{\}Psi\$ decays inside the horizon and \$R\$ grows.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **And calibrated against a KNOWN \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta R\$** --- the control re-run with \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ displaced \$\textbackslash\{\}pm8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, the estimator tracking \$77\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of it, which is `r4558`'s rule applied to a *measurement* rather than to a knob --- ***the arm's offset FALLS SHORT of what its own loading accounts for, by an effective \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ of \$-2.9\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, on top of the \$-2.0\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ its fitted \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ already is.*** **Too much loading is what deepens troughs and raises alternation; this arm's monopole behaves as though it had too little, and its troughs are deeper anyway.**  *On the order's own branching this is a THIRD outcome --- neither "the construction supplies an \$R\$-like offset" nor "both arms sit on their own \$(1+R)\$" --- and it refutes the loading reading **by sign** rather than leaving it undetermined.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE TWO RESIDUALS ARE NOT ONE NUMBER: THEY DIFFER BY A FACTOR OF SIXTEEN AND IN SIGN.** Against the control, the arm's **contrast** is \$+4.0\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ and its **alternation** --- the odd-even amplitude, with the envelope's own curved decline *fitted* rather than assumed away --- is about \$2\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ **lower**. Converted through the control's own measured responses (\$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d(\textbackslash\{\}text\{contrast\})/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b = +7.97\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d(\textbackslash\{\}text\{alternation\})/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b = +1.85\$, both positive, so more loading gives more of both), **the contrast asks for an effective \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ of \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{+22.9\textbackslash\{\}\%\}\$ and the alternation for \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{-1.4\textbackslash\{\}\%\}\$.** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE \$+22.9\$ IS A LOWER BOUND, BECAUSE THE CONTRAST RESPONSE SATURATES**: over the whole \$\textbackslash\{\}pm8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ range the contrast spans only \$0.028\$ and its increments fall monotonically (\$+0.0119, +0.0087, +0.0054, +0.0020\$), so a straight line *overstates* what \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ can deliver --- ***a four per cent contrast excess is beyond what the baryon density reaches in this construction at any value, not merely at an implausible one.***  *And the monopole offset agrees with the alternation, at \$-2.9\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ against \$-1.4\textbackslash\{\}\%\$: two independent measurements of the loading displacement agree and it is the contrast that stands apart.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND PART OF THAT IS A CORRECTION TO THE ORDER'S PREMISE, WHICH MIXED TWO REFERENCES**: the alternation excess is read against the **sky** (\$P\_1/P\_2=2.264\$ against \$2.217\$) and the contrast against the **control** (\$1.040\$). *Against the control, on the banked spectra, the arm's \$P\_1/P\_2\$ is \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{2.141\}\$ against \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{2.191\}\$ --- **lower**.* **The two were never pointing the same way; the appearance that they were is the two references.** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **ONE POSITIVE LOCALISATION, WITH ITS OWN SIGN PROBLEM STATED**: the arm's **dipole-to-monopole** amplitude ratio at the visibility peak is \$+1.8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ and **grows** with wavenumber.  *But the Doppler term FILLS troughs --- `cc66.36` measured `DPSRC=0` taking the arm's oscillation from \$1.040\$ to \$1.828\$ --- so a larger dipole fraction makes troughs shallower, and this arm's is larger while its troughs are deeper. It works AGAINST the contrast excess, which leaves a source larger than four per cent and partly cancelled --- a statement about size, not a mechanism.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} And the header now carries the visibility peak, its redshift, its FWHM and \$R\$ there on **every** path: the two arms' last-scattering widths side by side for the first time on the reporting path, \$38.04\$ Mpc against \$43.59\$). 
\emph{Computes:} `ZPSAVE` is new and wired into `hier\_run` --- the **reporting** path --- because `PHISAVE` is one of the nine switches `r6893+cc66.37` measured as OFF it, so using it would have measured the low-multipole construction and called it the reporting one; its default gated **bit-identical** on both arms before any number is read, and it saves the **undamped** monopole because the envelope multiplies the offset too; the offset estimator's three predecessors recorded with the bias each carried (a midpoint of extrema alternating by a factor of three; a quarter-half-quarter combination leaving \$\textbackslash\{\}pm15\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of curvature; a one-period window ill-conditioned by collinearity) and the verdict shown unchanged across five window widths; the calibration against a known \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta R\$ carried as its own gate; the contrast statistic reproducing `cc66.35`'s \$1.0401\$ so the space is that receipt's; the alternation's sign shown independent of the trend degree; and both \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ responses shown non-null and linear over the range, so neither residual is converted through a response that is not there --- every long run sliced on `KBATCH`, which `r6895+cc66.38` measured as exact to \$10\textasciicircum{}\{-16\}\$
 \emph{Bound:}  **NO MECHANISM FOR THE CONTRAST IMBALANCE** --- the order says none is asked for and none is claimed  **the dipole excess is NOT claimed to produce the contrast excess**, its sign being wrong for it  the offset's shortfall against \$-R\$ is **not** a defect of either arm: it is the same shortfall on both  the \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$ response is measured on the **control** and the two arms differ in more than \$\textbackslash\{\}omega\_b\$, so each implied displacement is *effective* and is called that  the width story the order withdrew before sending is **not** reinstated --- nothing here attacks its reasoning
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_monopole\_zero\_point\_is\_not\_the\_channel\_and\_the\_two\_residuals\_point\_opposite\_ways.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_free_streaming_knob_is_common_to_both_arms_and_the_switch_sweep_finds_no_further_shadow}{P15R177}\textbf{P15R177}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_free\_streaming\_knob\_is\_common\_to\_both\_arms\_and\_the\_switch\_sweep\_finds\_no\_further\_shadow}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6891`, `sec:refit-bound`, continuing `r6889+cc66.36`, in two parts: **point `NUFS` at the question on a grid that can resolve it** --- *"a locator on a finer \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ grid, both arms, so the shift is measured rather than bracketed at one bin step --- and the two arms' shifts compared, since a phase shift common to both is not a candidate for \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ and one that differs between them is"*, with *"the drag half reported separately"*; and **sweep the switches, because the rate is the finding** --- *"enumerate every environment switch this instrument reads, and for each one record which of the three source constructions and which of the two solver paths actually reads it --- from the source text, not from memory --- and, for every switch reachable on the reporting path, whether setting it away from its default moves the spectrum there"*, the order adding that **the output wanted is the table and not a repair campaign**} \ ( **THE SWEEP IS EXHAUSTIVE AND THE ANSWER IS AN ACCOUNTING, NOT A NEGATIVE.** Sixty environment switches enumerated from the source text through `ast`, each tagged with which of the three source constructions and which of the two solver right-hand sides reads it and whether that site is *written into* the construction or merely downstream of it; the reporting path declared from the dispatch's own three lines, with `main`'s live prefix ending at the `return 0` of the `HIER` branch.  *A **binding** is not a **use**, and that distinction is the whole of the static half --- a first pass keyed to binding sites called fifty-five of sixty live, because a module-level `os.environ.get` executes on every path and proves nothing.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **Fifty-one have a live use site there; the nine that do not come back BIT-IDENTICAL on the reporting path --- all eighteen runs at exactly \$0.0\$, twice over, and again at full \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ reach where `DAMPX` and `RD` would have shown if they touched the damping tail.** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***AND FIFTY-FIVE SWITCHES MEASURED ON BOTH ARMS, ONE AT A TIME: the set of runs that came back bit-identical is EXACTLY the set four readings predict --- off-path, rate-identity on the control arm, the arm branch, and gated by another switch --- no unexplained null, and nothing explained away that in fact moved.***  *The rate-identity family is the pleasing one: `GSRC`, `LEAFSCALES`, `PHASEONLY`, `PHASEPOW`, `STACKPERT` are bit-identically inert on the **control** and every one moves the arm, because on the control `Hphys` and `Hleaf` are the same expression, the Jacobian is \$1\$ and \$x\textbackslash\{\}times1.0\$ is exact --- the same floating-point fact `cc66.36`'s reassociation episode turned on.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **TWO THINGS THE SWEEP DID TURN UP, neither of the `r6476` class and neither moving a reported number:** **(i)** `LRSFROM` moves the instrument's reported \$r\_s\$ by a quarter (\$145.38\textbackslash\{\}to110.49\$ Mpc, \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ \$301.5\textbackslash\{\}to396.8\$) and \$\textbackslash\{\}mathcal\{D\}\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ by **exactly zero** --- \$R\_S\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ are **diagnostics**, reaching a `print` and the `SAVE` metadata and nothing else, and `hier\_run` accepts \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$, \$D\_M\$ and \$r\_s\$ and **loads none of the three**;  *`r6476` certified that switch on the ground that the header moved, and **the header is not the reported number** --- the certification was of the wrong quantity, and no number moves*; \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *and the consequence points the useful way --- because no transfer function reads \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$, the printed \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1/\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A=0.7312\$ against the sky's \$220.6/301.7\$ is an agreement between **two independently computed quantities** and not a value fed in*; **(ii)** `LATARG`, which the file calls the corpus's one fitted number, has **no root** at the arm's adjudicated background --- the onset solve raises across the whole bracket `r6760+cc66.1` had widened --- which is why the refit command supplies `ZSTART=3e7`. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE FREE-STREAMING SHIFT IS MEASURED.** Two independent locators --- a parabola vertex at each acoustic extremum and a sub-bin cross-correlation of the **envelope-normalised** oscillation --- on both arms, and again at \$\textbackslash\{\}mathtt\{LSTEP\}=2\$, four times the multipole sampling, where they agree to better than half a multipole.  ***IT IS NOT UNIFORM, WHICH CORRECTS `r6889`***: band by band it RISES monotonically, \$+2.9/+5.6/+9.4/+10.9/+12.6\$ on the control and \$+3.1/+5.7/+9.3/+10.9/+12.5\$ on the arm --- *the uniformity `cc66.36` gated was the grid, and the gate was a tolerance of \$0.05\$ on integers that could only differ by a whole bin*;  *and a pure rescaling does not fit it either, the straight line having an intercept of three multipoles.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***AND IT IS COMMON TO THE TWO ARMS --- largest band-by-band difference \$0.17\$ of a multipole, and in units of each arm's own \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ the extremum-averaged shift agrees to \$1.7\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-4\}\$ --- so on the order's own criterion it is NOT a candidate for \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$.*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **And the drag half, kept apart and split in two because it is two things: an ENVELOPE rescale of \$1.2573\$ and a peak-to-trough CONTRAST ratio of \$0.9875\$, the two arms agreeing to \$0.0004\$ and \$0.0006\$ --- and \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$ is a CONTRAST difference, so the knob's large effect is in the wrong quantity.**  *In \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$'s own space the arms' `NUFS` directions sit at cosine \$0.99918\$; their difference freely rescaled reaches \$8.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of \$\textbackslash\{\}lVert\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}rVert\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ at a **negative** coefficient, and no such freedom exists because both arms carry one neutrino sector*).

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_skys_own_fourth_peak_cannot_tell_the_arms_apart_and_the_displacement_is_shared_with_the_control}{P15R178}\textbf{P15R178}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_skys\_own\_fourth\_peak\_cannot\_tell\_the\_arms\_apart\_and\_the\_displacement\_is\_shared\_with\_the\_control}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`PO-47`; `sec:intro`'s locator, continuing `r6835+cc66.28`} \ (**THE STOPPING RULE FIRES --- AND THE SPREAD THAT FIRES IT IS NOT THE ONE `cc66.28` QUOTED.** That receipt gave the sky's fourth peak as \$1121.9\textbackslash\{\}pm0.87\$, and **that \$0.87\$ is a PROCEDURE spread** --- how far the answer moves as the parabola window is swept on one realisation. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} Propagating `plik\_lite`'s **bandpower covariance** through the identical parabola gives the sky's own uncertainty: ***\$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_4=1121.9\textbackslash\{\}pm2.03\$ (statistical) \$\textbackslash\{\}pm0.87\$ (procedure) \$=\textbackslash\{\}pm2.21\$ combined*** at the reference window, \$\textbackslash\{\}pm1.57\$ at the tighter one.  **Against that, the construction's own displacement --- arm minus control, \$+2.00\$ in \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ --- is \$0.90\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ at \$W=55\$ and \$1.27\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ at \$W=80\$: it does not reach significance at either admissible window, so THE SKY CANNOT TELL THE TWO MODELS APART AT THE FOURTH PEAK.** *What is established is "not resolved" rather than "zero", and the receipt says so rather than smoothing it.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The three candidates are NOT run, per the order's own stopping rule.***  **AND WHAT DOES NOT DISSOLVE IS SHARED AND IS NOT THIS CONSTRUCTION'S**: both models sit high of the located sky --- the control by \$+2.65\$ (\$1.2\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$) and the arm by \$+4.65\$ (\$2.1\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$), **\$57\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of it the control's** --- named and not pursued.  *And the order's \$+10.1\$ was never this number: it is the paper's stored quartet against the paper's stored sky, two procedures; through one locator the arm-minus-sky offset is \$+4.65\$.*  **AN INSTRUMENT TRAP IS REPORTED AS A FINDING**: `cc66.28`'s locator finds peaks by free extremum search, which is right on a smooth spectrum and **unusable on a noisy one** --- under the covariance it returns \$1051\textbackslash\{\}pm132\$ for the fourth peak, seventy multipoles off. **Error propagation through it must anchor the window**). 
\emph{Computes:} the sky quartet and its procedure spreads reproduced against `cc66.28`; \$600\$ covariance realisations through the anchored parabola at four windows with per-peak bias and spread; the window chosen on the **bias--variance trade** (\$W=40\$ rejected --- the parabola inverts on \$12\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of realisations and \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\_4\$ explodes to \$122\$; \$W=110\$ rejected --- bias reaches \$-6.4\$, larger than the displacement under test); seed-independence; and the three pairings in \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ --- **11 gates with TWO controls that break as they must**: a planted displacement of \$4\$ and \$10\$ in \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ recovered to \$+4.56\$ and \$+11.71\$ (so a real displacement WOULD show), and the free-search failure itself
 \emph{Bound:}  **not a full sky-uncertainty budget** --- `COV\_TT` is the shipped bandpower covariance with foregrounds and calibration marginalised, with no beam or theory-side term added; the central value is the locator's on `plik\_lite`'s binning and `sec:intro`'s own \$1123.9\$ sits two multipoles away, inside.  **None of `PO-47`'s three candidates is run** --- the third acoustic spacing, the baryon loading's effect on position and the transfer's resolution at the fourth peak are left, a convergence check being named as where the shared offset would go. Nothing re-opens the phase intercept, the damping envelope, the driving or the refit

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_ladders_discreteness_does_not_set_the_spectrum_and_the_check_was_run_in_pieces}{P15R179}\textbf{P15R179}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_ladders\_discreteness\_does\_not\_set\_the\_spectrum\_and\_the\_check\_was\_run\_in\_pieces}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{The control `r6788` declared open and node 66 asked for by name (`FOR\_60` item 5): `KCONT=1`, the CR arm's discrete \$k\$-ladder replaced by a continuum grid on the crossing configuration. The alias gate waives its 4-points-per-Bessel-period requirement for this arm because the ladder is physical, and the waiver's own text says that is only not aliasing if the answer does not depend on it} \ (**It does not, and the waiver is honest here.** Ladder (\$1452\$ modes, \$2.3\$ pts/period) \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2 = 1205.3755\$ against continuum (\$2700\$ modes, \$4.3\$) \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2 = 1205.3745\$ --- ***peaks identical*** at \$220/540/820/1132\$, heights identical to three decimals, the single fitted amplitude \$3\$ parts in \$10\textasciicircum{}\{8\}\$ apart, spectra agreeing to \$6.7\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-8\}\$ of the peak  **and the reason it agrees far better than \$2.3\$ points per period predicts is measured rather than guessed**: \$\textbackslash\{\}sqrt\{L(L+2)\}\textbackslash\{\}to L+1\$, so the "discrete ladder" is asymptotically *uniform* --- within \$1\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of its median spacing over \$99.9\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of its gaps --- making this a comparison of two near-uniform samplings differing in spacing, not of resolved against aliased   **run in six slices, because neither seat's container survives it whole**: the check costs \$2700\$ modes against the run's \$1452\$, mine was killed three times and `cc66` restarts every \$1\$--\$15\$ min). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 **the segmentation proved exact before anything was read through it** --- a whole run against three slices summed, \$\textbackslash\{\}max\textbackslash\{\}lvert\{\textbackslash\{\}rm sum\}-\{\textbackslash\{\}rm whole\}\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$ relative \$2.195\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-16\}\$, machine precision  `KSLICE=lo:hi` added with its default proved byte-identical  both spectra scored on the same \$185\$ covered bins over the same \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ range  **and a property `KBATCH` already had, measured and sized**: `\_project` takes \$dk = \textbackslash\{\}nabla k\$ from the *batch*, so on the ladder's non-uniform bottom modes the batch boundary moves the weights --- one batch against two differs by \$1.205\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-8\}\$ relative
 \emph{Bound:}  **Settles one thing only**: that discreteness does not set this spectrum. Says nothing about whether the spectrum is right, and ***nothing that touches the floor*** --- \$F\_2 = +1114.1\$ still forbids reading \$1205.4\$ against the control's \$1320.5\$ as a preference, exactly as `r6788` said  measured at **one** configuration, so `c54.186`'s \$0.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ at the pinned one is a different ladder density and is not superseded  the `KBATCH` figure is **negligible at \$10\textasciicircum{}\{-8\}\$** --- eight orders below anything physical, a reproducibility note (quote `KBATCH` with a ladder command) and not a defect, which is why no banked spectrum is revisited  and `KSLICE` is exact on a **uniform** grid only, so it is a continuum tool and a ladder run should be done whole

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_leg_contributes_no_tilt_because_a_fixed_phase_handover_is_scale_free_and_a_fixed_time_one_is_a_running}{P15R180}\textbf{P15R180}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_leg\_contributes\_no\_tilt\_because\_a\_fixed\_phase\_handover\_is\_scale\_free\_and\_a\_fixed\_time\_one\_is\_a\_running}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`PO-31`'s work order from node 66: where the tilt comes from, if it comes from the collapse leg. The row has a target --- the like-for-like refit prefers \$n\_s=0.9949\$ against the control's \$0.9559\$ (`r6805`) --- and the order asks the first calculable thing beyond it: **can the leg produce a departure of that size at all?**} \ (**No, and for a stronger reason than a small number: what the leg can produce is not a tilt.** Both the potential and the temperature on the leg are functions of \$x=k\textbackslash\{\}eta/\textbackslash\{\}sqrt3\$ **alone**, so a handover at a fixed *phase* is exactly scale-free --- ***the ratio of amplitudes at two wavenumbers is \$1\$, not nearly \$1\$*** --- and the only surviving scale is a handover at a fixed *time*, through \$x\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm seam\}=0.7638\textbackslash\{\},(k/k\_s)\$  **what a fixed-time handover gives is red in both channels and goes as \$k\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$**: \$\textbackslash\{\}dd\textbackslash\{\}ln\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}hat\textbackslash\{\}Theta\textbackslash\{\}rvert/\textbackslash\{\}dd\textbackslash\{\}ln k=-x\textbackslash\{\}tan x=-x\textasciicircum{}\{2\}+O(x\textasciicircum{}\{4\})\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}dd\textbackslash\{\}ln T/\textbackslash\{\}dd\textbackslash\{\}ln k=-x\textasciicircum{}\{2\}/5+O(x\textasciicircum{}\{4\})\$ --- a **running**, where a constant \$n\_s\$ shift needs a constant log-derivative, with an honest zero of the amplitude at \$k/k\_s=2.057\$   **so the size question answers itself by shape**: the target needs \$k/k\_s=0.065\$, and thirty times that wavenumber the same expression gives \$1-n\_s=41.9\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***and on the adjudicated configuration it is exactly zero*** --- `r6774`'s crossing is \$x\textbackslash\{\}to0\$, where \$T\textbackslash\{\}to1\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}hat\textbackslash\{\}Theta\textbackslash\{\}to\textbackslash\{\}Psi\_i/2\$ and both log-derivatives vanish quadratically). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 the three banked amplitudes reproduced before anything is read --- \$T(1/\textbackslash\{\}sqrt3)/2=0.48353\$ (the coded handover), \$\textbackslash\{\}cos(1/\textbackslash\{\}sqrt3)/2=0.41896\$ (the exact entry temperature), \$1/2\$ (the envelope and the crossing value) --- the closed form being **established** (`P15` \S{}coherence, \S{}transmission; `r6760+cc66.1`) and re-run rather than re-derived  the quadratic approach checked, so the vanishing at the crossing is not an artefact of where the limit is taken  *and one coding slip of mine was caught before landing: the band evaluation used the wrong factor and printed \$1.4\$ where the docstring said \$41.9\$*
 \emph{Bound:}  **Bounds the LEG's contribution only** --- nothing about what the progenitor supplies, and nothing about \$A\_s\$, whose fixed-point route the entropy monotone closed  the fixed-time numbers are evaluated at the seam because that is the only fixed-time locus the corpus ever coded: **a demonstration that such a handover gives a running, not a claim the corpus uses one** --- it does not, since `r6774`  the progenitor's mass enters only through \$k\_s\$, so no independent mass dependence appears  ***nothing is tuned to \$0.995\$***: the target enters once, to be refuted on shape, and `PO-7` is untouched --- this is a statement about a transfer function and not a verdict
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_leg\_contributes\_no\_tilt\_because\_a\_fixed\_phase\_handover\_is\_scale\_free\_and\_a\_fixed\_time\_one\_is\_a\_running.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_substrate_cannot_tilt_the_spectrum_and_the_collapses_duration_enters_only_as_a_decaying_oscillation}{P15R181}\textbf{P15R181}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_substrate\_cannot\_tilt\_the\_spectrum\_and\_the\_collapses\_duration\_enters\_only\_as\_a\_decaying\_oscillation}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's order (2) in `FOR\_60` (`r6825`), `PO-31` one step in: with the leg closed as a source, the tilt is the progenitor's vacuum, so **what does the substrate ALONE constrain about that vacuum, before any interior is modelled?** Three candidates named --- the progenitor's mass, the finite duration of the collapse, and the substrate's curvature radius --- with the order asking which can enter the vacuum's two-point function *at all*, and the sign of \$n\_s-1\$ for any that can} \ (**One of the three can enter, and what it produces is not a tilt: the substrate alone fixes \$n\_s=1\$ exactly.**  **the curvature radius CANNOT enter** --- in conformal time the substrate's mode equation \$u''+(k\textasciicircum{}2-2/\textbackslash\{\}eta\textasciicircum{}2)u=0\$ contains no length at all, so the only variable is \$k\textbackslash\{\}eta\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}alpha\$ survives solely as the prefactor \$P=1/(4\textbackslash\{\}pi\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}2)\$: it sets \$A\_s\$ and reaches \$n\_s\$ *for every* \$\textbackslash\{\}alpha\$, not for large \$\textbackslash\{\}alpha\$  **the progenitor's mass CANNOT either, and on this construction that is arithmetic** --- the forced member is Nariai, \$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda M\textasciicircum{}2=1/9\$ with \$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda=3/\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}2\$, so \$M=\textbackslash\{\}sqrt3\textbackslash\{\}alpha/9\$ and \$M/\textbackslash\{\}alpha=0.192450\$ is a **pure number**: the mass *is* the curvature radius up to a constant and brings no new length, so the two candidates are one and fall together   **only the finite duration enters** --- setting the vacuum at finite \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\_0\$ gives \$\textbackslash\{\}alpha\_k=1-i/x-1/2x\textasciicircum{}2\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}beta\_k=-e\textasciicircum{}\{2ix\}/2x\textasciicircum{}2\$ with \$\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}alpha\_k\textbackslash\{\}rvert\textasciicircum{}2-\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}beta\_k\textbackslash\{\}rvert\textasciicircum{}2=1\$ exactly and \$R(x)=1+\textbackslash\{\}cos2x/x\textasciicircum{}2-\textbackslash\{\}sin2x/x\textasciicircum{}3+(1-\textbackslash\{\}cos2x)/2x\textasciicircum{}4\$, \$x=k\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}eta\_0\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$, whose log-derivative is \$-2\textbackslash\{\}sin(2x)/x+O(x\textasciicircum{}\{-2\})\$: **an oscillation with a decaying envelope, sign-alternating 24 times on \$x\textbackslash\{\}in[1,40]\$, its four octave means disagreeing in sign** --- a *running*, and by the corpus's own operational test a scale, not a tilt \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***so a constant tilt needs the interior, and its sign is fixed in advance***: the only quantity giving a band-independent log-derivative on a maximally symmetric background is an effective mass, exactly \$n\_s-1=3-2\textbackslash\{\}sqrt\{9/4-m\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}2\}=\textbackslash\{\}tfrac23 m\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}2+O(m\textasciicircum{}4)\$, **blue for \$m\textasciicircum{}2>0\$** --- so the measured \$n\_s=0.995\$ requires \$m\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}2=-1201/160000=-0.0075\$, slightly tachyonic). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 the Nariai constants reproduced before anything is read --- \$M=\textbackslash\{\}sqrt3\textbackslash\{\}alpha/9\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}Lambda M\textasciicircum{}2=1/9\$ on substitution, \$f(r\_N)=f'(r\_N)=0\$ --- **established** (`P15` Fig. \$f(r)\$, `r6804`) and re-run rather than re-derived, because finding (2) rests entirely on them; the Bunch--Davies mode verified to solve the mode equation and to carry Wronskian \$i\$, so the amplitudes are not rescalable  the \$R(x)\$ identity checked twice by independent simplification routes  *and one stated criterion of mine was wrong and was corrected to the computation rather than the reverse: I had claimed the octave means sit within \$0.021\$ of zero, and the \$[2,4]\$ band averages \$-0.095\$ --- the true and stronger statement is that they do not agree with one another and do not share a sign*
 \emph{Bound:}  **the one modelling step is named and it is the standard one**: the corpus's scalar perturbation is treated on the substrate as a massless minimally coupled field. Findings (1) and (2) are robust to that choice --- they are statements about which *lengths* appear, and none appears for any mass --- but the exact value \$n\_s=1\$ is not, which is precisely what (4) says  the duration channel computes a **sharp** initial time, the one unambiguous prescription: a smooth onset changes the envelope's power and the phase and cannot change the sign alternation, so the claim is the SHAPE and not the coefficient  bounds what the **substrate** supplies --- nothing about \$A\_s\$, whose fixed-point route the entropy monotone closed, and nothing about the interior beyond the one requirement  ***nothing is tuned to \$0.995\$***: the target enters once, at the end, and only to be inverted into a requirement on a quantity this receipt does not compute; `PO-7` untouched

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_interiors_vacuum_is_blue_everywhere_and_a_running_so_the_substrates_mass_template_does_not_apply}{P15R182}\textbf{P15R182}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_interiors\_vacuum\_is\_blue\_everywhere\_and\_a\_running\_so\_the\_substrates\_mass\_template\_does\_not\_apply}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order in `FOR\_60` (`r6845`) --- `PO-31` on the progenitor's **interior**. `r6826` left one channel, \$n\_s-1=3-2\textbackslash\{\}sqrt\{9/4-m\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\}\$ asking the interior for a slightly negative \$m\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$, **but that formula is the substrate's and the fluctuations are generated in the interior**. Three questions: the spectrum handed over and whether it is a tilt or a running; its sign; and whether the substrate's formula applies at all} \ (**All three answer, and they answer against the construction.**   first a correction to the order's premise: **the interior is not newly built** --- `P15\_the\_progenitor\_vacuum\_is\_negligible\_too` already banks \$a=M(\textbackslash\{\}rho x+x\textasciicircum{}\{2\}/2)\$ and the \$M\$-free potential \$2/(x(x+2\textbackslash\{\}rho))\$, and it is *the same object*: with \$M=A/2\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=2\textbackslash\{\}sqrt B/A\$ the two agree **exactly** to \$O(\textbackslash\{\}eta\textasciicircum{}\{2\})\$ and \$a''/a=A/(2a)-1\$ reduces to it  **(1) a RUNNING, not a tilt**: the potential interpolates between \$2/x\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ (the \$p=+2\$ matter root, scale-free) and \$1/(\textbackslash\{\}rho x)\$ (not), so at the determined \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=0.0539\$ the log-derivative runs \$+0.30\$ to \$+1.98\$ over \$k=5\$--\$2000\$, tending to the radiation value \$+2\$ --- no constant to fit, by the same band test that closed the leg  **(2) BLUE at every \$k\$**, flattest \$+0.304\$, against a measured **red** \$-0.002\$: wrong sign and \$\textbackslash\{\}sim100\textbackslash\{\}times\$ the size. The radiation content is the cause --- flattest slope \$\textbackslash\{\}simeq6.7\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ --- **so red would need \$\textbackslash\{\}rho<0\$** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **(3) the substrate's template DOES NOT APPLY**: \$3-2\textbackslash\{\}sqrt\{9/4-m\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\}\$ comes from a \$1/\textbackslash\{\}eta\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ potential whose coefficient is *dimensionless*; the interior's carries the scale \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$. ***So \$m\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\textbackslash\{\}alpha\textasciicircum{}\{2\}=-0.0075\$ is an artefact of applying the substrate's template off the substrate, and the corpus should stop carrying it as a requirement***). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 **the extractor calibrated on four power laws before being believed** --- \$p=-1\$ and \$p=+2\$ both returning \$0\$ to \$5\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-6\}\$, \$p=+1\$ giving \$+2\$, \$p=+3\$ giving \$-2\$, against \$3-2\textbackslash\{\}lvert p-1/2\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$: *two of them are the corpus's own scale-invariant roots, so the instrument is checked on the cases the argument turns on*  *an earlier version read every \$k\$ on a **different** surface and returned \$+6\$ where \$0\$ was right --- the calibration caught it, and it is recorded because the failure was silent*  the background identities and the two potential limits derived symbolically; the surface-convergence measured rather than assumed
 \emph{Bound:}  **the vacuum is set at maximum expansion** (\$a'=0\$, so every mode sub-horizon), with \$k\textasciicircum{}\{2\}/\textbackslash\{\}lvert a''/a\textbackslash\{\}rvert\textbackslash\{\}ge10\$ enforced per mode --- **below that band the interior supplies no initial condition**, which is the order's own stopping clause, reported as a bound and not stepped over  the slope **converges** as the reading surface approaches the branch point (last two decades agree to \$1.2\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-4\}\$, the shallowest differing by \$1.4\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-2\}\$) --- *stated as convergence and not insensitivity, because the shallowest surface is not yet converged*  \$A=1\$ sets the time unit and every result is a slope or a ratio; \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=0.0539\$ is the **determined** composition, taken from the banked receipt and not chosen here  a massless minimally coupled field, the same modelling step `r6826` named  **the banked receipt is not contradicted**: its scale-invariance is of a *transfer* and its number an *amplitude*; the slope of the generated spectrum is a different object  nothing bears on \$A\_s\$ or `PO-7`

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_transfer_is_the_same_object_as_the_vacuum_spectrum_and_the_crossing_carries_no_wavenumber}{P15R183}\textbf{P15R183}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_transfer\_is\_the\_same\_object\_as\_the\_vacuum\_spectrum\_and\_the\_crossing\_carries\_no\_wavenumber}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order in `FOR\_60` (`r6897`) --- **(2) `PO-31`, released** after the switch sweep validated its target (\$n\_s=0.9949\$ against the control's \$0.9559\$). Four channels closed and the measured red tilt supplied by none, so: ***what supplies a red tilt when the only vacuum the construction has computed is blue?*** The order named one thing to look at first --- *every closed channel was tested as a **spectrum-generating** mechanism, but the tilt is a ratio of amplitudes at two wavenumbers and there is a second place one can enter, the **transfer** from the progenitor's vacuum to the boundary datum* --- *"established for the collapse leg and not for the interior-to-leg join... if it is already closed, say so and it costs a paragraph"*} \ (**It is not a second place, and saying so costs more than a paragraph because the reason corrects this seat's own reconciliation of two banked results.**  **(1)** the interior's transfer and the interior's vacuum spectrum are **one object**: the banked instrument, run at the *determined* \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=0.0539\$, gives a log-slope running \$+0.267\$ to \$+1.979\$ --- ***r6857's \$+0.30\$ to \$+1.98\$, from vacuum data placed somewhere else entirely*** (sub-horizon in the deep matter contraction, against `r6846`'s maximum expansion)   **(2)** and the banked scale-invariance is **not contradicted --- it has a domain**: the potential breaks at \$x\textbackslash\{\}sim2\textbackslash\{\}rho\$, so \$k\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm break\}=1/(2\textbackslash\{\}rho)\$; the banked check ran at \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=10\textasciicircum{}\{-3\},10\textasciicircum{}\{-4\}\$ with its whole band *below* \$k\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm break\}=500,5000\$, and at the determined \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ the observed band lies *above* \$k\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm break\}=9.3\$  **(3)** and the one step genuinely untested --- **the crossing** --- carries no wavenumber **exactly**: \$x=0\$ is a resonant regular singular point and its log coefficient is \$C=1/\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ with \$dC/dk=0\$, because ***\$k\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ is regular where the potential is singular*** (it first enters the analytic solution at \$x\textasciicircum{}\{3\}\$) \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  ***the chain carries no \$k\$-dependence of its own anywhere, and where one does enter it is BLUE where the target is red***). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 **the calibration is the gate**: PART 1 re-runs the banked check at the banked \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ and \$k\$ and reproduces \$3/(2\textbackslash\{\}sqrt2)\$ to \$0.20\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- *the whole of PART 2 is that same instrument pointed at a different composition, so if PART 1 fails nothing below may be read*  the \$x\textbackslash\{\}to0\$ log of the radiation crunch divided out explicitly rather than left in the residual  the resonant coefficient **determined** order by order (Frobenius, symbolic) rather than quoted  the indicial equation shown to be \$k\$-blind from \$x\textasciicircum{}\{2\}(\textbackslash\{\}text\{pot\}-k\textasciicircum{}\{2\})\textbackslash\{\}to0\$
 \emph{Bound:}  **THIS DOES NOT CLOSE `PO-31`** --- it closes the route the order named, which is a different thing; the row's question is left exactly where `r6857` left it, with one candidate eliminated rather than untested  the background, the potential and \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=0.0539\$ are **taken from banked receipts**, not rebuilt; what is new is where they are evaluated  **the crossing result is about the monodromy and not about `C19`'s \$9/10\$** --- `C19` states in its own voice that its join factor is the super-horizon one and that *"modes inside the horizon at the branch point are not covered"*, and that item is untouched  a slope is not an amplitude: the \$A\_s\$ comparison is a **ratio to the scale-free branch**, showing the banked \$A\_s\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash\{\}rm vac\}\$ understated by \$\textbackslash\{\}sim5\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{3\}\$ at the band's top --- against a shortfall of \$10\textasciicircum{}\{103\}\$, so ***a bound on how the number is read and not a correction to its conclusion***  `r6846`'s *"the slope of the generated spectrum is a different object"* is **withdrawn**: they are one object, and the reconciliation was wrong *in the direction that let a conflict pass*

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_interiors_transfer_imprints_its_own_running_so_the_channel_hunt_answered_the_wrong_question}{P15R184}\textbf{P15R184}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_interiors\_transfer\_imprints\_its\_own\_running\_so\_the\_channel\_hunt\_answered\_the\_wrong\_question}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\

The order: the banked transfer law's exact scale-invariance is a property of the **vacuum normalisation** (\$k\textasciicircum{}\{3\}\$ cancelling \$D\_k\$'s \$k\textasciicircum{}\{-3\}\$), not of the transfer --- so ***applied to an arbitrary input rather than to vacuum data, is the interior's transfer a scale-free multiplier, or does it imprint its own running?*** Forbidden: building a progenitor interior, importing a tilt} \ (**It IMPRINTS, and the order's diagnosis of the mechanism is confirmed exactly.**  **(1)** on **unit** incoming amplitude the multiplier's log-slope runs \$-0.8663\$ at \$k=7\$ to \$-0.0105\$ at \$k=1400\$ --- a spread of \$0.856\$, so ***neither scale-free nor even a power law***; what it adds to any tilt is twice that, \$-1.73\$ to \$-0.02\$  **(2)** and  ***the banked exact scale-invariance is that same function's LOW-\$k\$ limit***: \$d\textbackslash\{\}ln T/d\textbackslash\{\}ln k\textbackslash\{\}to-1\$ (\$-0.9992\$ at \$k=0.3\$) and \$kT(k)\textbackslash\{\}to27.86\$, a constant --- and \$P\textbackslash\{\}sim k\textasciicircum{}\{3\}\textbackslash\{\}lvert c\_0\textbackslash\{\}rvert\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ is scale-invariant *exactly* when the slope is \$-1\$, so the cancellation is the normalisation meeting a power law that holds only below the break  **(3)** the decomposition as an **identity**, \$s(k)=d\textbackslash\{\}ln T/d\textbackslash\{\}ln k+1\$ --- ***the \$k\$-dependence was always the transfer's and the constant always the normalisation's***, reproducing `r6898`'s \$+0.134\$ and \$+0.990\$ from the transfer alone  **(4)** the same function for **every** input to \$4\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-13\}\$, including one deliberately not a power law \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  ***the channel hunt is over: five results stand, the question was mis-aimed, and the row becomes a statement about the interior model plus one computed requirement***). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 **the banked check is the gate** --- \$3/(2\textbackslash\{\}sqrt2)\$ reproduced at the banked \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ and \$k\$ to \$0.20\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, and everything below is that same instrument pointed at a different object  **linearity measured, not invoked** (\$7\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-15\}\$) --- *it is what licenses the order's phrase "whatever the progenitor supplies"*  the starting **phase** varied (\$6\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-6\}\$) and then the convention **replaced outright** by a fixed \$x\_i\$ (\$2.9\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-5\}\$), because a phase artefact here would look exactly like a running  the slope shown to **converge** in the reading surface between the two deepest, \$2.9\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-5\}\$
 \emph{Bound:}  **the band is bounded at \$k<2783\$ and the bound is the PREMISE, not the arithmetic**: at \$x\_i=300/k\$ the mode starts outside the break only while \$300/k>2\textbackslash\{\}rho\$, and above that "enters from the matter contraction" is false ---  *an earlier pass read \$T>1\$ at \$k=10\textasciicircum{}\{5\}\$, tolerance-converged to \$2\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-10\}\$ and still meaningless, and that number is discarded as a misplaced initial condition rather than reported as a limit; **no high-\$k\$ asymptote is claimed***  **the slope is claimed and the amplitude is not** --- \$\textbackslash\{\}lvert T\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$ carries \$\textbackslash\{\}sim1\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of reading-surface dependence, which cancels in a log-slope; the shallowest surface differs by \$2.9\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-3\}\$ and is reported as **not yet converged**, the same clause `r6846` attached to its own  nothing bears on \$A\_s\$, whose \$10\textasciicircum{}\{103\}\$ shortfall is a **vacuum** statement  no progenitor interior built; \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=0.0539\$ and the potential taken from banked receipts  **`PO-31` does not close**, as the order said it would not

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_acoustic_contrast_is_not_in_the_source_and_the_projection_makes_it_without_the_distance}{P15R185}\textbf{P15R185}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_acoustic\_contrast\_is\_not\_in\_the\_source\_and\_the\_projection\_makes\_it\_without\_the\_distance}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6911`: **where is the acoustic contrast made --- in the source, or in the projection?** --- *"take the statistic you already have and apply it before the projection as well as after"*: **(a)** in \$k\$-space at last scattering, the arm-and-control contrast of the **source** (the monopole-plus-Doppler combination as a function of wavenumber, same envelope split, same peak-to-trough measure), ratio arm to control; **(b)** compare with the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$-space \$1.040\$ --- the order naming three outcomes in advance (\$\textbackslash\{\}approx1.04\$ = made in the dynamics; \$\textbackslash\{\}approx1.00\$ = manufactured between \$k\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$, *"live rather than exotic, because the two arms' distances differ by six per cent"*; or a split, *"and the split itself is the measurement"*), with **one guard**: *"the envelope split must be defined identically on the two arms and at the two stages, and the smallest difference it can express must be stated beside whatever tolerance gates it"*, and **no mechanism asked for**} \ ( **THE CONTRAST IS NOT IN THE SOURCE: IT IS MANUFACTURED BETWEEN \$k\$ AND \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$.** The same statistic reads \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.996\}\$ on the source at last scattering, \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.992\}\$ on the \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$-integrated source, \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{1.045\}\$ on the raw spectrum and \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{1.047\}\$ on the banked one --- *and over four envelope windows, both envelope definitions, three term subsets (monopole alone, monopole plus Doppler, the full source), four \$q\$ sub-windows and with or without the \$k\$-measure, the source rungs span \$0.971\$--\$1.005\$ and the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ rung \$1.042\$--\$1.052\$, against a statistic floor of \$0.6\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ set by its own bias on a **known injected** contrast.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THAT IS WHY FOUR-FOR-FOUR NEEDED NO EXPLAINING.** The order's difficulty was that four channels which each *shallow* the troughs are all larger on this arm, so something upstream had to overcome all four. ***Upstream the arm's oscillation is \$0.996\$ of the control's --- very slightly SHALLOWER, which is exactly the direction all four point.*** **Nothing overcomes them because nothing had to: the effect to explain is not bigger than four per cent, it is not upstream at all.** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **THE NAMED ROUTE IS OUT TWICE OVER, AND THE FIRST HALF IS A PREMISE CORRECTION.** *The order's \$13005\$ against \$13865\$ Mpc is the **superseded** configuration's (\$r\_s=135.46/144.53\$), where the arm's was the smaller; at the adjudicated minima \$D\_M\$ is \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{14017.04\}\$ on the arm against \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{13954.35\}\$ on the control --- \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{+0.449\textbackslash\{\}\%\}\$ and the **arm's is the LARGER**, wrong in size and in sign.*  *The cancellation the order relies on is real --- \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ agree to \$0.085\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- so it is the six per cent and not the reasoning that fails.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **And the route is eliminated by measurement anyway**: `SRCXS` projects one arm's own source through the *other's* distance, one arm at a time, moving its contrast by \$-0.63\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ (control) and \$+0.59\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ (arm) --- *and removing the difference altogether takes the arm/control ratio **UP**, \$1.045\textbackslash\{\}to1.051\$.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **WHERE IN THE PROJECTION, AS FAR AS THIS GOES**: the arm's projection **retains \$1.054\$ times as much** of its own source oscillation as the control's does (\$0.2543\$ against \$0.2413\$); the excess **rises** with wavenumber (\$1.031\$ below \$q=3\$ to \$1.065\$ above) where the source ratio is flat (\$0.992\textbackslash\{\}to0.992\$); it is **not** the visibility width acting before the kernel (\$0.996\textbackslash\{\}to0.992\$), **not** the lensing or the binning (\$1.045\textbackslash\{\}to1.047\$), and **not** the \$k\$ grid --- `KCONT=1` puts the arm on the control's kind of uniform grid at the same mode count with the physics untouched and the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ rung is unchanged at \$1.045\$. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE GUARD CHANGED THE DEFINITION, WHICH IS PART OF THE MEASUREMENT**: `r6885+cc66.35`'s running **geometric** envelope cannot be carried to the source at all --- the source power comes within a part in \$10\textasciicircum{}\{8\}\$ of its own median at the troughs, where a log-mean is dominated by near-zeros --- *so the envelope is a running **arithmetic** mean at **every** rung, both are reported, and they differ by \$0.003\$ on the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ rung against the \$0.05\$ step being measured*). 
\emph{Computes:} `SRCSAVE` is new and wired into `\_project` --- the **reporting** path --- writing the source term by term at last scattering *and* \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$-integrated, with the four terms gated to **sum to the instrument's own `S`** (\$2.8\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-17\}\$) so a future edit to `S` that forgets the block fails here; its default and `SRCXS`'s gated **bit-identical** on both arms before any number is read, and `SRCXS=1.5` with `SRCSAVE` unset gated bit-identical too, which is what makes the swap an *output of the save* rather than a knob; every load of either name shown to sit inside an `if \_SRCS:` block (*a binding is not a use*); the sliced runs gated to reproduce the banked \$185\$-bin refit spectra to \$4.9\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-15\}\$ relative, **which is what makes the \$k\$ end and the \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ end one run rather than two**; the abscissa's alignment **measured** rather than assumed (the arms' acoustic period in \$q\$ agreeing to \$0.22\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ at the source and \$0.09\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ in \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$, best lag \$+0.005\$ and \$-0.001\$); the statistic returning exactly \$1.000000\$ against itself, recovering a **known** injected contrast, and returning \$0.9996\$ through the other arm's abscissa and back; and the one artefact that could have produced the whole answer --- the two arms' different *kinds* of \$k\$ grid --- run out with `KCONT=1` rather than argued, that switch shown non-null so the result is not `r4558`'s unwired knob
 \emph{Bound:}  **NO MECHANISM** --- "the projection makes it" names a **stage**, not a cause, and nothing here says what within the projection produces \$5.4\textbackslash\{\}\%\$; the order's bound is unmoved  **NOT that the projection is wrong** --- nothing here bears on the flat kernel, which `prop:flat` settles; *a projection can manufacture a contrast difference while being exactly the correct projection for both arms*  **NOT that the visibility width is its route**: the arm's is \$15\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ wider, which *shallows* contrast, and the rung where an \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$-average acts **without** the kernel moves the ratio by only \$-0.4\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- a bound on one route, not the identification of another  **NOT that the source is identical on the two arms**: it is \$0.992\$, a real deficit of about the statistic's floor, and its sign is the four channels' own  **NOT that `cc66.35`'s \$1.0401\$ is superseded** --- it is reproduced exactly under its own definition and comes out \$1.047\$ under this receipt's, and the difference is the envelope definition, reported  the \$q\$ variable rests on the sharp-visibility map \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=kD\_M\$, exact to the visibility width over \$D\_M\$ --- \$38\$--\$44\$ Mpc against \$\{\textbackslash\{\}sim\}14000\$, a part in \$300\$

Three items: (1) ***where is the break in \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$, and is any \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ putting the observed band wholly below it***, on a stated criterion; (2) ***is \$1.71\$ a number or an artefact of the band's placement***; (3) ***is the high-\$k\$ limit exactly zero, and the requirement therefore bounded by two*** ---  **argued ANALYTICALLY, because \$k\textbackslash\{\}to\$ large is exactly where this seat's \$x\_i=300/k\$ premise expires and *"a second \$k=10\textasciicircum{}\{5\}\$ would be worse than a gap"***} \ (**All three answer, and two in closed form, because the running turns out to be a function of ONE variable.**  **THE STRUCTURAL FACT** --- substituting \$x=2\textbackslash\{\}rho u\$ gives \$v\_\{uu\}=(2/(u(u+1))-\textbackslash\{\}kappa\textasciicircum{}\{2\})v\$ with \$\textbackslash\{\}kappa=2\textbackslash\{\}rho k\$ --- ***the potential in \$u\$ carries no break\}=\textbackslash\{\}kappa\_*/(2\textbackslash\{\}rho)\$ **exactly**  **(3)** near the crunch the equation is the **repulsive \$L=0\$ Coulomb wave equation** with \$\textbackslash\{\}eta=1/\textbackslash\{\}kappa\$, and \$1/C\_0(\textbackslash\{\}eta)=1+\textbackslash\{\}pi\textbackslash\{\}eta/2\$ gives \$d\textbackslash\{\}ln T/d\textbackslash\{\}ln k\textbackslash\{\}to-\textbackslash\{\}pi/(4\textbackslash\{\}rho k)\textbackslash\{\}to\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0\}\$ --- matching measurement to \$1.4\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ at \$k\textbackslash\{\}le2000\$, ***derived where the integration is not trusted and checked where it is*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **the excursion is exactly \$1\$, the tilt contribution at most \$2\$, and no band however wide can require a progenitor running above \$2\$**  **(1)** a working \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ **exists** but needs \$\textbackslash\{\}rho<9.7\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-4\}\$ (midpoint) or \$<1.4\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-4\}\$ (within \$0.05\$ of the power law) --- ***\$55\textbackslash\{\}times\$ to \$378\textbackslash\{\}times\$ below the determined \$0.0539\$*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  ***the requirement stands BECAUSE the determined radiation fraction forces it***  **(2)** over the \$\textbackslash\{\}pm6\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ the arm's own distances allow, \$1.71\$ moves \$2.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- **a number, not an artefact**, and the observed band asks \$86\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of the hard maximum). 
\emph{Computes:} C1 **the banked \$3/(2\textbackslash\{\}sqrt2)\$ check at the banked \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ and \$k\$ is the gate** --- everything below is that instrument or an analytic argument about the same equation  the scaling substitution and the composition identity done **symbolically**, not by hand  the \$\textbackslash\{\}kappa\$-collapse **measured** across four \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ spanning a factor \$100\$  the Coulomb limit's coefficient taken from a **series expansion of \$1/C\_0(\textbackslash\{\}eta)\$**, then compared against measurement at four \$(k,\textbackslash\{\}rho)\$ pairs *all inside the premise*
 \emph{Bound:}  **the analytic limit is NOT measured at large \$k\$, by design** --- the order forbade it and the reason is this seat's own: `r6912` discarded a \$k=10\textasciicircum{}\{5\}\$ reading that was **tolerance-converged to \$2\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-10\}\$ and still meaningless** because \$x\_i=300/k\$ had drifted inside the break. ***No integration above \$k=2000\$ is performed or quoted***  **the differencing convention moves the headline in the third digit**: `r6912`'s centred grid slopes gave \$1.71\$, this \$\textbackslash\{\}pm8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ two-point slope gives \$1.724\$ --- so the requirement is quoted as **\$1.71\$--\$1.72\$** and that \$0.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ spread is *part of* (2)'s answer, not noise  over a **wider** band the requirement moves \$6\$--\$9\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, reported rather than hidden   **the \$\textbackslash\{\}rho\$ bound is NOT converted into a number for \$a\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm eq\}\$ or \$M\$**: `P16`'s own two statements give \$\textbackslash\{\}rho=\textbackslash\{\}sqrt\{2a\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm eq\}/M\}\$ (verified symbolically), but at \$a\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm eq\}=1.49\$ Mpc and the **register's** mass that reads \$1.635\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-2\}\$ against the determined \$0.0539\$ --- a factor \$3.3\$, i.e. two different progenitor masses. ***Flagged, not used***  no progenitor interior, no tilt value, no amplitude; **`PO-31` does not close**, as the order said
