\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_cross_term_is_not_the_channel_and_every_projected_term_carries_the_excess}{P15R187}\textbf{P15R187}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_cross\_term\_is\_not\_the\_channel\_and\_every\_projected\_term\_carries\_the\_excess}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6915`: **which term carries the contrast excess after projection?** --- `cc66.40` located the stage, and the order asks what inside it does the work, naming the monopole--Doppler pair first because *"the monopole enters through \$j\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ and the Doppler through \$j\_\textbackslash\{\}ell'\$, and those are a quarter-period out of phase"*: **(1)** project each term separately, apply the same \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$-space contrast statistic to each piece and then to their sum, with three outcomes named (both pieces near \$1.00\$ and the sum at \$1.045\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **the cross term**; one piece already at \$1.045\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} that term's own projection; neither and the pieces do not sum \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *"the decomposition is not linear where I have assumed it is, and that is the finding"*), **and a forced calibration**: *"the projection is linear in the source, so the separately projected pieces must sum to the full spectrum. Gate that first\ldots{} and read nothing below it if it fails"*; **(2)** report the retained fraction **as a function of \$q\$** rather than as one number per arm --- rising \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} a kernel-phase signature, flat \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *"that kills the reading above"*} \ ( **THE CLOSURE GATE HAD TO BE CORRECTED BEFORE IT COULD BE PASSED, AND THE CORRECTION IS THE ORDER'S OWN FIRST OUTCOME.** *The **transfer** is linear in the source and the pieces do add there; \$C\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ is **quadratic** in the transfer, so the projected **spectra** cannot --- the four diagonal pieces alone fall short of \$D\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ by up to \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{46\textbackslash\{\}\%\}\$.*  *This is not the third outcome: the **first** outcome presupposes a cross term, so the order's two clauses are in tension and the first is the coherent one.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **What closes is the full bilinear decomposition** --- with \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textasciicircum{}a\_\textbackslash\{\}ell(k)=\textbackslash\{\}int\textbackslash\{\}text\{term\}\_a j\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\textbackslash\{\},\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}eta\$, \$C\_\textbackslash\{\}ell=\textbackslash\{\}sum\_\{a\textbackslash\{\}le b\}w\_\{ab\}\textbackslash\{\}sum\_k P\textbackslash\{\},\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textasciicircum{}a\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textasciicircum{}b\$ --- **ten numbers per multipole closing on \$D\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ to \$1.1\$ and \$1.3\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-15\}\$ relative on the two arms.** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE CROSS TERM IS NOT THE CHANNEL: deleting it moves the ratio from \$1.0452\$ to \$1.0451\$**, and over four envelope windows its carry runs \$-0.0013\$ to \$+0.0008\$ of the \$+0.045\$ --- *bounded at about a part in a thousand, so the interference between \$j\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ and \$j\_\textbackslash\{\}ell'\$ is excluded.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **BECAUSE EVERY PROJECTED PIECE ALREADY CARRIES IT, WHICH IS NEITHER OUTCOME NAMED**: monopole \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{1.034\}\$, Doppler \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{1.099\}\$, polarisation \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{1.058\}\$ against the full \$1.045\$ (the ISW alone is the exception at \$0.989\$, and it is \$1\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of \$D\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$). ***So it is not a term and not a pair of terms: the projection raises this arm's contrast on nearly everything it projects.*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE LADDER IS THE SHARPEST FORM OF IT --- every term's SOURCE ratio is at or below \$0.992\$ and every term's PROJECTED ratio is higher**: monopole \$0.992\textbackslash\{\}to1.034\$, Doppler \$0.973\textbackslash\{\}to1.099\$, ISW \$0.972\textbackslash\{\}to0.989\$, polarisation \$0.990\textbackslash\{\}to1.058\$, the whole source \$0.992\textbackslash\{\}to1.045\$. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **THE SPLIT THE ORDER'S REASONING WAS AFTER IS ABOUT \$40/60\$, THE RESPONSE THE LARGER**: the weights *do* differ (the arm's monopole share \$0.5225\$ against \$0.5092\$, its Doppler \$0.1753\$ against \$0.1840\$), and rebuilding the arm's pieces at the control's shares gives \$1.0267\$ --- **the weights carry \$+0.019\$ of the \$+0.045\$ and each piece's own response \$+0.027\$**, with the reverse construction agreeing at \$+0.020\$. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **(2) AND THE RETAINED FRACTION IS NOT FLAT IN \$q\$**: it rises from \$1.030\$ in the lowest band to \$1.089\$ in the highest, and over **twelve settings** (four envelope windows \ensuremath{\times} three band counts) the slope is \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{+0.0139\textbackslash\{\}pm0.0021\}\$ per unit \$q\$, **never once negative** --- *so the branch that would have killed the reading is excluded.*  *The intercept is \$1.017\textbackslash\{\}pm0.010\$ and **straddles one**: the rise is established and a constant offset is not, and the value at \$q=0\$ is an extrapolation from a lowest band centre of \$q=1.20\$*). 
\emph{Computes:} `SRCDEC` is new and wired into `\_project`'s own multipole loop --- **the reporting path** --- so the Bessel evaluation is shared with the reported spectrum and the decomposition costs four trapezoids per multipole rather than a second projection; its default gated **bit-identical** on both arms before any number is read, and every executable line naming it categorised as a binding, a guard or a line inside a guarded block (*a binding is not a use*) rather than counted, because a substring count read the switch's own documentation as code and failed this gate on its first writing; the ten pairs gated to close on \$D\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ and the **non**-closure of the four diagonals gated too, so the premise correction is a measurement and not an argument; this run's \$D\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ gated identical to `r6911+cc66.40`'s to \$10\textasciicircum{}\{-13\}\$ relative, which is what lets a source rung and a projected rung sit in one ladder; every off-diagonal piece that is **not** positive definite named rather than quietly skipped, a relative oscillation about a running mean being undefined where the mean changes sign; the bank gated that the slices tile \$k\$ with no gap or overlap and that the pair **order** is identical slice to slice; and the statistic, abscissa and resolution taken over unchanged from `r6911+cc66.40` rather than re-derived
 \emph{Bound:}  **NO MECHANISM** --- naming that every projected term carries it, and that the rise in \$q\$ is real, is still not a statement about *why*; the order's bound is restated rather than moved  **NOT that the cross term is zero** --- it is \$26\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of \$D\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ and its oscillation is \$59\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of the full's; what is bounded is its contribution to the **difference**  **NOT that the Doppler's \$1.099\$ is as well determined as the monopole's \$1.034\$**: its piece's relative oscillation is \$0.116\$ against \$0.305\$, so it is the weaker signal, and its window spread \$1.0995\$--\$1.1070\$ is quoted beside it  **NOT a fit** --- the weight/response split rescales pieces by their mean share, which also moves the envelope, which is exactly why both directions are reported  **NOT that the \$q=0\$ intercept is one**  nothing here touches `prop:flat`

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_a_source_with_no_physics_reproduces_the_contrast_and_the_two_clocks_part_company_at_the_visibility}{P15R188}\textbf{P15R188}\quad\texttt{P15\_a\_source\_with\_no\_physics\_reproduces\_the\_contrast\_and\_the\_two\_clocks\_part\_company\_at\_the\_visibility}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6919`: **(1) project a source with no physics in it** --- *"feed both arms' projection machinery the same analytic oscillating source --- a pure \$\textbackslash\{\}cos(k r\_s+\textbackslash\{\}phi)\$ on a smooth envelope, no transfer, no terms, no weights --- and measure how much of its oscillation each arm's projection retains, as a function of \$q\$"*, reproducing `cc66.41`'s \$1.054\$ and \$+0.0139\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} the geometry, else \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} the source's \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$-dependence; **(2) then swap the two geometric factors one at a time**, the visibility and \$\textbackslash\{\}chi(\textbackslash\{\}eta)\$, with the candidate named: *"the source is accumulated on one clock and the kernel's argument is a distance read on the other"* --- and the instruction that *"if a swap is not well posed\ldots{} **say that instead of forcing it.** An ill-posed swap reported as a null is exactly the shape of the unwired knob at `r4558`"*} \ ( **A SOURCE WITH NO PHYSICS IN IT REPRODUCES THE EFFECT AND OVER-DELIVERS.** A pure \$g(\textbackslash\{\}eta)\textbackslash\{\}cos(k r\_s(\textbackslash\{\}eta))\$ through each arm's own kernel, visibility, \$k\$ grid and multipole grid gives an arm/control retained ratio of \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{1.066\}\$ at a slope of \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{+0.0226\}\$ per unit \$q\$, against the real source's \$1.054\$ and \$+0.0139\$. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The projection's geometry accounts for the whole of it and then some: the source is irrelevant to it*** --- the order's first branch.  *The injection's phase does not matter (\$1.066/+0.0225\$ at \$\textbackslash\{\}phi=\textbackslash\{\}pi/2\$), and a **standing** oscillation already carries most of it (\$1.059/+0.0119\$).* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THE ORDER'S NAMED CANDIDATE IS IN THE INSTRUMENT BUT NOT WHERE THE ORDER PUTS IT.** *`x0 = eta\_0 - EE` on both arms, so \$\textbackslash\{\}chi(\textbackslash\{\}eta)=\textbackslash\{\}eta\_0-\textbackslash\{\}eta\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}chi/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}eta\textbackslash\{\}equiv1\$ identically on each, with no `Jac`, `Hleaf` or `Hphys` touching `x0` on any path --- **there is nothing there to exchange.*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The two clocks sit between \$r\_s\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$***: conformal time (and so `x0`) is built from `Hphys`, the **stacking** rate, while the acoustic phase accumulates on the **leaf** rate --- `Jac` \$=1.000000\$ everywhere on the control by the rate identity, and \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.789\}\$--\$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.913\}\$ across \$\textbackslash\{\}pm3\$ FWHM on the arm. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **AND THAT IS WHERE THEY PART COMPANY, AS ONE NUMBER PER ARM.** Across the visibility FWHM the accumulated **sound horizon** agrees to \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.08\textbackslash\{\}\%\}\$ (\$17.3074\$ against \$17.2941\$ Mpc) while the **comoving distance** differs by \$\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{14.6\textbackslash\{\}\%\}\$ (\$38.042\$ against \$43.591\$): *the leaf clock makes \$r\_s\$ accumulate more slowly per unit \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$, so a \$15\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ wider window covers the **same** acoustic phase --- and the kernel, which reads \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\$, sees the wider window.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The joint object is \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d r\_s/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}chi\$ across the visibility --- the sound speed the kernel sees --- \$0.4550\$ on the control against \$0.3967\$ on the arm, \$-12.8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$***; term-independent, growing with wavenumber, and vanishing for a window under one acoustic period, which are the three properties `cc66.41` measured. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **(2) AND NEITHER SWAP CLOSES IT ALONE --- THE THIRD OUTCOME.** *The **clock** swap is **ill posed** as an isolation and the statistic says so rather than returning a null: forcing both arms onto the stacking clock **moves the comb**, and band by band the regression **alternates in sign** (\$+0.37/-0.52/+0.42/-0.57\$) --- `cc66.40`'s guard firing on exactly the shape it was built for, and itself evidence that the leaf assignment is what the arm's reported peak positions need.*  *The **width** swap **is** well posed and **overshoots by eight**: giving each arm the other's FWHM takes the slope \$+0.0226\textbackslash\{\}to+0.0931\$ while barely moving the mean, so the width sets the **shape** and not the **level**.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **They close only together, and what they close on is \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d r\_s/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}chi\$**). 
\emph{Computes:} `SRCINJ`, `SRCINJPH`, `SRCINJRS` and `SRCINJVIS` are new, all four gated **bit-identical when unset** on both arms in one result that covers both guards, the edit touching `hier\_run`'s batch loop as well as `\_project`; every load shown to sit inside a guarded block with the four bindings counted apart (*a binding is not a use*); **the solver is skipped on the injected path** by a guarded `continue`, because the analytic source replaces `S` and evolving the hierarchy would build an array nothing reads; the injection carries \$k\textasciicircum{}\{(1-n\_s)/2\}\$ so the smooth part of \$PS\textasciicircum{}2\$ is exactly \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm dk/k\$ on **both** arms and the two tilts cannot enter; the statistic, abscissa and window taken over unchanged from `r6911+cc66.40` so the comparison with the real source is like for like; the emptiness of the \$\textbackslash\{\}chi(\textbackslash\{\}eta)\$ swap gated from the **source text** on code lines with comments stripped, because the `hier\_run` docstring names `x0 = eta\_0 - EE` too and a substring count read the documentation as a second construction; the visibility swap reported as the **construction** it is, \$g\$ rescaled about each arm's own peak with its integral preserved, because the arms' visibilities sit at \$\textbackslash\{\}eta=281.7\$ and \$486.0\$; and the ill-posed clock swap gated on its **sign changes** rather than on its value
 \emph{Bound:}  **NO MECHANISM**, and the order does not ask for one yet: naming \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d r\_s/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}chi\$ is not an account of *why* this construction assigns its scales and its distances to different rates --- that is `P15` \S{}`sec:tensions`' own question and is **not reopened**  **NOT that the injection IS the source**: it has the right oscillation in \$q\$ and the arm's own visibility and nothing else, so over-delivery is sufficiency and not identity, and the excess over \$1.054\$ is not interpreted  **NOT that the visibility width is ruled out** --- its swap overshoots, which makes it inseparable from the clock, not absent  **NOT that `LEAFSCALES=1` is wrong**: the stacking-clock injection puts the arm's comb in the wrong place, which is evidence the leaf assignment is what the peak positions need  **NOT a spectrum** --- every `SRCINJ` run is the projection's transfer of a known input and none may be compared with a banked spectrum or the sky  nothing touches `prop:flat`
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_a\_source\_with\_no\_physics\_reproduces\_the\_contrast\_and\_the\_two\_clocks\_part\_company\_at\_the\_visibility.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_visibilitys_clock_is_the_one_the_kernel_reads_and_the_comb_votes_against_the_alternative}{P15R189}\textbf{P15R189}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_visibilitys\_clock\_is\_the\_one\_the\_kernel\_reads\_and\_the\_comb\_votes\_against\_the\_alternative}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6925`: **which clock is the visibility a density in, and does the rate rule determine it?** --- the rule being *"comoving separations read across leaves take the stacking rate; scales the plasma accumulates take the leaf's"*, and \$g=\textbackslash\{\}tau'e\textasciicircum{}\{-\textbackslash\{\}tau\}\$ **neither**: *"the optical depth is accumulated by the plasma\ldots{} but it is differentiated per unit \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$, and \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$ is built from the stacking rate"*. **(1)** locate every place the visibility and the optical depth touch a rate; recompute \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm dr\_s/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}chi\$ under the other admissible assignment and report what the retained fraction and its \$q\$-slope become --- with the standing guard *"report what it does to the comb beside what it does to the contrast, and if they disagree say so rather than picking one"*, and the third branch *"if the second assignment is **not well posed**\ldots{} say that, and say which step breaks"*. **(2)** one number on where the source's partial cancellation sits, the injection over-delivering at \$1.066/+0.0226\$ against the real \$1.054/+0.0139\$} \ ( **THE AUDIT FINDS THE GAP ALREADY OPEN IN THE CODE, WHICH THE ORDER DID NOT EXPECT.** *Every site where \$\textbackslash\{\}tau\$ or \$g\$ touches a rate is on the **stacking** clock --- the recombination history solved against `Hphys`, `taup\_of` built on `eg`'s conformal time, `tau` integrated over `\_egrid`, `ETA\_LS` and `ETA\_LS\_W` read off that grid.*  ***But `1/k\_D\textasciicircum{}2` twenty lines below IS Jac-weighted under `LEAFSCALES`.*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***So the diffusion length --- a scale the plasma accumulates --- takes the LEAF clock and the optical depth, also accumulated by the plasma, takes the STACKING clock: two objects on the same side of the rule, on opposite clocks, with nothing stating the choice.***  *So the other assignment is not an invention --- `VISLEAF=1` applies to \$\textbackslash\{\}tau\$ exactly the weighting \$1/k\_D\textasciicircum{}2\$ already applies to itself, which is what makes it **admissible** rather than invented.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **ON THE GEOMETRY THE TWO AGREE --- THE ORDER'S SECOND BRANCH.** \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm dr\_s/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}chi\$ goes \$0.396733\textbackslash\{\}to\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.396957\}\$ on the arm, so \$12.80\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ lower becomes \$12.75\$ --- **a move of \$0.06\$ per cent** --- and the control does not move at all. *And structurally rather than luckily: it is a **ratio** of two accumulations across the **same** window, so re-weighting that window's measure re-weights numerator and denominator alike.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The visibility is not where the freedom is, and the \$12.8\$ per cent is forced by the rule as stated.*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **BUT THE CONTRAST AND THE COMB DISAGREE, AND THE ORDER SAID TO REPORT THAT RATHER THAN PICK.** *Read alone the other assignment moves the injection's retained ratio \$1.0850\textbackslash\{\}to1.0695\$ and its slope \$+0.02424\textbackslash\{\}to+0.01332\$ --- **toward** the real source's \$1.0694\$ and \$+0.01167\$, which is the order's first branch.*  *But the arm's first peak moves \$\textbackslash\{\}ell=220\textbackslash\{\}to228\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1/\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ \$0.7290\textbackslash\{\}to\textbackslash\{\}mathbf\{0.7555\}\$ --- **away** from the sky's \$0.7312\$ --- with \$P\_1/P\_2\$ \$2.142\textbackslash\{\}to2.017\$ and **the control's comb bit-identical both ways**.*  *And the contrast improvement is partly an artefact of that same move: band by band the `VISLEAF=1` ratio is **non-monotonic scatter** with a fitted residual **nine times** the current assignment's, because moving \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm LS\}\$ moves \$r\_s(\textbackslash\{\}eta\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm LS\})\$ and so moves the comb, and a band ratio of two oscillations no longer aligned in \$q\$ reads their phase mismatch --- **`cc66.40`'s guard firing a third time on the shape it was built for**.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The geometry says forced, the contrast says improvable, the comb says no; the comb is the only one of the three with an external referent, and neither is picked.*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **(2) AND THE SOURCE'S CANCELLATION IS HALF THE LEVEL AND NONE OF THE SLOPE, so the sector does NOT close into one account.** *Multiplying the injection by `cc66.40`'s measured source deficit --- \$0.9922\$, and **flat** in \$q\$ at a slope of \$-0.00106\$ --- gives \$1.0766\$ at \$+0.02296\$ against the real \$1.0694\$ at \$+0.01167\$: **\$54\$ per cent of the level gap and \$10\$ per cent of the slope gap**.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *So the four trough-filling channels account for about half the level difference and essentially none of the slope; the order's conditional is half satisfied and the closure it hoped for does not arrive. **What flattens the slope is the real source's own \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$-dependence across the visibility --- exactly what the injection replaced --- and that is named, not measured here.***). 
\emph{Computes:} `VISLEAF` is new, gated bit-identical when **unset** on both arms **and when SET on the control** --- two different gates, the second being the rate identity showing in a second place and the one-sidedness of the whole finding in one result; the switch also shown **non**-inert on the arm (\$\textbackslash\{\}eta\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm LS\}\$, the FWHM and \$r\_D\$ all move) so the near-null on the geometry is a connected knob's and not `r4558`'s unwired one; the audit's five sites and the \$1/k\_D\textasciicircum{}2\$ counter-example all located **from the source text** rather than described; the `VISLEAF=1` slope's contamination gated on its **band residual** against the current assignment's rather than asserted; the comb gated on the arm's \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1/\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ moving **away** from the sky **and** the control's being unchanged, so the direction is part of the gate; and the statistic, abscissa and window taken over unchanged from `r6911+cc66.40`
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the two-rate assignment is right** --- that is the row's question now and not this order's, and nothing here settles it  **NOT that `VISLEAF=1` is wrong as physics**: what is measured is that it moves the comb away from the sky while moving the contrast toward the real source, and that its contrast improvement is partly a phase artefact --- *which of the two clocks the optical depth **should** be a density in is not settled here*  **NOT that the \$0.06\$ per cent invariance settles the row** --- it says the visibility is not where the freedom is; the freedom is in the rule's assignment of \$r\_s\$ itself  NOT a mechanism beyond `cc66.42`'s, and nothing touches `prop:flat`  **NOT that the injected runs are spectra** --- the `injvl` arrays are the projection's transfer of a known input and only the `combvl` arrays are spectra, kept under separate keys  **NOT that this receipt's first launcher worked**: it dropped its extra environment through a positional-argument bug and thirty-six slices ran as plain `VISLEAF=0` spectra, *reporting nothing wrong and reproducing the banked spectra* --- recorded in the launcher's own comment, with the fix a smoke test that greps the log for the marker the switch must print
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_visibilitys\_clock\_is\_the\_one\_the\_kernel\_reads\_and\_the\_comb\_votes\_against\_the\_alternative.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_combs_resolution_is_four_multipoles_and_the_skys_own_value_lies_outside_the_family}{P15R190}\textbf{P15R190}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_combs\_resolution\_is\_four\_multipoles\_and\_the\_skys\_own\_value\_lies\_outside\_the\_family}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6929`: **the comb has been promoted to arbiter, so measure what it can actually decide** --- *"you have made the comb the load-bearing reading \ldots{} but the corpus has never measured how sharply it discriminates, and a reading promoted to arbiter needs its resolution stated before it decides anything"*. Scan the assignment **continuously** --- `VISLEAF` as a fraction rather than a flag --- and report against that parameter \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1/\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ against the sky's \$0.7312\$, the retained fraction and its \$q\$-slope, and \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm dr\_s/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d\textbackslash\{\}chi\$; with three branches (**steep comb + shallow contrast** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} the comb pins the assignment; **both shallow, or the comb's spread inside the sky's own locating spread** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} the comb cannot arbitrate, *"and that is the finding, and it is worth more than the scan"*; **an interior fraction fitting better** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} say so plainly, since a fitted clock would be a new free parameter) and the guard *"report whether the comb's motion is the visibility peak relocating or the acoustic phase changing"*} \ ( **EVERY READING IS LINEAR IN THE PARAMETER AND THE COMB'S WHOLE RANGE IS FOUR MULTIPOLES.** \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1 = 221.93 + 4.21f\$ to three hundredths of a multipole, so the family spans \$0.01395\$ in \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1/\textbackslash\{\}ell\_A\$ --- \$4.2\$ of the sky's one-multipole locating widths, \$2.1\$ of its two-multipole ones. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The comb pins \$f\$ to \$\textbackslash\{\}pm0.24\$: it separates the family's ends and comes nowhere near fixing the clock.***  **AND THE ORDER'S FIRST BRANCH IS HALF RIGHT.** The contrast's *level* is shallow (\$1.75\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ against the comb's \$4.21\$) but its \$q\$-slope is not (\$+0.01042\textbackslash\{\}to-0.00039\$, \$3.80\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$, the comb's statistical equal) --- ***what separates them is that the contrast's error GROWS five-fold across the family (band residual \$0.0089\textbackslash\{\}to0.0441\$, slope s.e. \$0.00284\textbackslash\{\}to0.01410\$) while the comb's locating width does not move***, `cc66.40`'s guard firing a fourth time.  **AND THE DECIDING RESULT IS NOT ABOUT THE CHOICE: THE SKY'S VALUE IS NOT IN THE FAMILY.** \$0.7312\$ sits at \$f=-0.304\$ on the family's own line, on the far side of the stacking clock \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***no interior fraction fits better than the endpoint in use (the third branch does not arise and there is no fitted clock to declare), \$f=0\$ is the best point, and the residual first-peak disagreement cannot be absorbed by the clock assignment because the direction it needs is inadmissible.***  \$P\_1/P\_2\$ agrees independently: \$2.141\textbackslash\{\}to2.017\$ against the sky's \$2.217\$.  **THE GUARD, SEPARATED THREE WAYS** because the injection has no plasma dynamics in it: the comb's motion is \$31\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ the peak relocating through Then (3) the three-way separation `cc66.44` built, at every peak index, with the reading fixed in advance; and the guard: **is the four-peak pattern one systematic or two, and do not pick** --- three candidate shapes named and none of them fitting, so the enumeration is probably incomplete*} \ ( **THE STOPPING RULE DOES NOT FIRE, AND IT MISSES BY A FACTOR OF FOUR HUNDRED.** *Run again at `LSTEP=1 LMAXL=2000` --- \$1900\$ multipoles against the reported \$238\$, on both arms --- the reported grid's locator recovers the fine-grid position to \$0.0007/0.0126/0.0283/0.0176\$ of a multipole on the control and \$0.0035/0.0089/0.0167/0.0229\$ on the arm:* ***two hundredths at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_4\$ against a bar of ten***, *and the extremum search is insensitive to its own width (order \$3\$ to \$40\$ identical).*  **WHAT IS IMPRECISE IS THE PARABOLA'S WINDOW, AND IT IS A BIAS.** *Over `PO-47`'s admissible \$W=15\textbackslash\{\}ldots110\$ the located peak moves \$0.08/2.77/4.73/5.82\$ on the arm and \$0.11/2.98/4.97/6.49\$ on the control --- growing with peak index because a wider fit on an asymmetric, damping-suppressed hump pulls its apex down the envelope --- so at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_4\$ the window's bias is comparable to the residual, and it displaces the sky and both models alike, which is what matched-procedure differencing is for* (at \$W=25\$ it agrees with the three-point locator to \$0.2\$).  **AND THE ORDER'S PATTERN IS TWO ARTEFACTS, NEITHER OF THEM PHYSICS.** *First the quartet: \$222/538/818/1134\$ is `sec:refit-bound`'s **line-of-sight** path, while every bank here is the **hierarchy** path, whose raw reading is \$220/540/812/1132\$ --- and **at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_3\$ the two bracketing `LSTEP=8` bins differ by four parts in ten thousand, so which is called the peak is a coin flip**: the paper quotes \$820\$, this locator picks \$812\$, the sub-bin apex is \$815.40\$ and the fine grid's own maximum is at \$815\$. Sub-bin the arm is \$221.96/536.11/815.40/1130.53\$, so the residual is \$+1.56/-1.60/-1.90/+6.63\$ ---* ***peaks two and three were never 'on', they are each about \$1.7\$ LOW, and peak four is \$6.6\$ out rather than \$10.1\$.*** **Second, and decisive: the CONTROL produces four fifths of it** --- \$-0.05/-1.41/-2.97/+5.32\$ through the identical locator --- so ***what is the construction's is \$+1.61/-0.19/+1.07/+1.31\$, a near-constant ONE multipole at all four peaks: a constant \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}ell\$, the FIRST of the order's three shapes, fitting half again better than the ruler's constant \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textbackslash\{\}ell/\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ (rms \$0.68\$ against \$1.02\$) and nothing like a driving error growing with \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$.*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **So the pattern needs ONE systematic and not two**, and every one of the four displacements is inside the sky's own locating spread `PO-47` measured (\$1121.9\textbackslash\{\}pm2.36\$, arm-minus-control \$0.83\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$), so *the sky cannot tell it from zero and it is reported as a shape rather than a disagreement*.  **(3) AND THE SEPARATION AT EVERY PEAK INDEX**: relocation \$31.2\textbackslash\{\}to94.1/83.4/89.3\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, the visibility's weighting \$9.2\textbackslash\{\}to26.4/22.7/23.5\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, the plasma \$59.6\textbackslash\{\}to-20.5/-6.0/-12.8\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- ***\$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1\$ is the outlier and nothing else is: at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_2\$ to \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_4\$ the motion is almost entirely geometric and the plasma's phase partially CANCELS it.*** *By the order's own reading the plasma's share does not grow, so the residual is not in the driving, and the relocation share does, which points at the ruler* ---  *but the third reading is the one that applies:* **the shares are not flat and the residual is not reached**, the family's motion being \$+0.6\$ to \$+0.7\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ with one sign at every peak while the residual alternates. *Motions resolved: fine against coarse they agree to \$0.02\$ of a multipole*).

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_contrast_excess_is_symmetric_about_the_envelope_and_the_troughs_are_where_the_sky_measures_it_best}{P15R192}\textbf{P15R192}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_contrast\_excess\_is\_symmetric\_about\_the\_envelope\_and\_the\_troughs\_are\_where\_the\_sky\_measures\_it\_best}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{node 66's work order `r6955`: **`PO-56` --- the troughs, which is where the \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ actually lives**. *Four routes are eliminated --- the contrast is not in the source at any grain, not the cross term, not absorbable by the clock assignment, not the comb residual --- so what is left is the \$\textbackslash\{\}chi\textasciicircum{}2\$: the spacing is right to a part in six hundred and the arm still scores \$1.43\$ per bin against the control's \$0.99\$.* **66's reading, stated as its own to be knocked down**: *the paper's discriminant of principle \$\textbackslash\{\}theta\_D/\textbackslash\{\}theta\_*\$ is a ratio of two lengths the plasma accumulates, so it rides ONE clock and a common difference cancels --- while a trough DEPTH needs the diffusion scale in multipoles and so needs the projection distance, which rides the other. (2)\textsuperscript{a} Locate the excess before assuming it, decomposing the contrast into what the peak heights carry and what the trough depths carry* --- **if it is in the peaks, stop and report**; *(2)\textsuperscript{b} then the clock sensitivity of whichever carries it, against \$\textbackslash\{\}mathrm d(\textbackslash\{\}theta\_D/\textbackslash\{\}theta\_*)/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm df\$; (2)\textsuperscript{c} and the sky's own spread on it, the way `PO-47` did for the fourth peak. Guards: the window bias applies to troughs and worse; **path provenance in the receipt's header from now on***} \ ( **(2)\textsuperscript{a} THE EXCESS IS SYMMETRIC ABOUT THE ENVELOPE, so it is neither trough- nor peak-dominated.** *Splitting the band variance of the envelope-normalised oscillation at its own zero --- a decomposition, the parts adding to the whole to machine precision --- the arm/control ratio is \$1.0668\$ on the positive side and \$1.0561\$ on the negative; at the literal extrema the arm exceeds the control by \$5.7\$ per cent at the maxima and \$5.3\$ at the minima.* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***It is an AMPLITUDE excess: \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta\$'s trough dominance is a statement about the whitened residual and does not transfer to the contrast's own decomposition*** --- which the order declined to assume, and the answer is that they disagree. *The stop condition does not fire either: the excess is not in the peaks, so the row is not pushed back onto the driving.*  **(2)\textsuperscript{b} THE DEPTHS ARE THREE TIMES THE MORE CLOCK-RESPONSIVE --- \$-2.58\$ per cent across the `VISLEAF` family against the heights' \$-0.77\$ --- BUT \$\textbackslash\{\}theta\_D/\textbackslash\{\}theta\_*\$ MOVES \$-4.08\$, MORE THAN EITHER**, because \$r\_D\$ is itself `Jac`-weighted under `LEAFSCALES` and the \$\textbackslash\{\}tau\$ re-weighting moves it directly. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***The paper's discriminant of principle is the most sensitive of the three and not the insensitive one, so by the order's own rule the depths are not a new handle on the clock.***  **(2)\textsuperscript{c} AND YET THE TROUGHS ARE WHERE THE ASSIGNMENT BECOMES OBSERVABLE, FOR A REASON NEITHER SEAT NAMED: THE SKY MEASURES DEPTHS TWICE AS WELL AS HEIGHTS.** *Same data, same likelihood binning, same anchored locator, `COV\_TT` propagated by Monte Carlo: the sky's spread is \$0.00588\$ on the depth and \$0.01135\$ on the height, seed-stable. The arm-minus-control excess is the same size in both --- \$+0.0106\$ and \$+0.0103\$ --- so it reads* ***\$+1.81\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ in the depths against \$+0.91\$ in the heights.*** *And the CONTROL lands on the sky's depths at \$-0.05\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ while the arm sits \$+1.76\$ above:* ***the first statistic in this sector whose residual is NOT shared with the control***, against the fourth peak's \$0.83\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ with both models high of the sky. \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **So the order's conclusion stands and its argument does not.**  **THE GUARDS**: *the anchored depth has NO window bias --- identical over \$W=20\textbackslash\{\}ldots70\$ on the sky and both arms, because it reads a **value** at a located extremum and not the **location** of one, where `cc66.45`'s parabola apex drifted six multipoles at \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_4\$ --- while a FREE search under noise reproduces `PO-47`'s trap at six times the spread, which is why the anchoring is necessary; and the envelope's abscissa is worth about a third of a sigma and is reported rather than minimised*). 
\emph{Computes:}  **Path provenance in the header, as the order directs**: every model number is the **hierarchy** path (`cc66\_r185\_verify\_*`, the `LSTEP=1` fine references `r6941\_fine\_*`, and the `VISLEAF` family `r6929\_scan\_cr` / `r6941\_fine\_cr\_visleaf`), and `sec:refit-bound`'s quartet is the **line-of-sight** path's and is not read at all --- searched for by grepping the receipt for each of the four values and for every line-of-sight bank name, none of which occurs in its code. *The sky side is `plik\_lite` TT through the likelihood's own binning, with the models binned identically before any comparison, so matched-procedure differencing is by construction and not by assertion.*  **And this revision cost no solver time**: it is analysis on banks `cc66.44` and `cc66.45` already built, which is why the order's three parts and three guards all fit in one revision
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that \$1.8\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ is a detection** --- it is \$1.8\$ of the sky's own spread on one statistic, and the same differencing at the fourth peak gave \$0.83\$  **NOT a mechanism** for the amplitude excess: this locates it and measures how well the sky sees it  **NOT a re-derivation of `PO-47`'s peak-position spreads**, which are cited untouched  **NOT that the depths discriminate the CLOCK** --- they do not, and that branch of the order's reading is refuted here  *no verdict on the two-rate assignment; no refit; nothing touches `prop:flat` or the clock family; no corpus edits*
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_contrast\_excess\_is\_symmetric\_about\_the\_envelope\_and\_the\_troughs\_are\_where\_the\_sky\_measures\_it\_best.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_windows_phase_spread_is_nearly_equal_because_the_visibility_widens_as_the_leaf_clock_slows}{P15R193}\textbf{P15R193}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_windows\_phase\_spread\_is\_nearly\_equal\_because\_the\_visibility\_widens\_as\_the\_leaf\_clock\_slows}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound` --- the normalisation across the visibility is a real channel carrying about a THIRD of the contrast excess, and it is peak-weighted where the excess is symmetric --- so it is the first partial mechanism in this sector and still not the whole one} \ (node 66's `r6959` order, three items. (1)\textsuperscript{a} ***"Measure the source's conformal-time dependence across the visibility window on both arms, and report it as a function rather than as a ratio"***; (1)\textsuperscript{b} ***"imposing one arm's normalisation on the other should move the contrast toward unity, and it should do so by an amount the measurement predicts in advance"*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *"predict the size first, then run it"*; (1)\textsuperscript{c} ***"state in advance what would REFUTE it"*** and ***"say what the normalisation swap does to the comb and to the depths as well as to the contrast"***.  Guards: path provenance in the header (standing), *"keep the two statistics apart"* since two transfers had already failed to hold, and no detection language). 
\emph{Computes:} **(1)\textsuperscript{a} THE FUNCTION, AND A CANCELLATION IN IT.** The source's own weight across the window, band by band in \$q\$, has its spread in the phase variable \$r\_\{s,\textbackslash\{\}rm leaf\}\$ at \$0.06523\$ of \$r\_s\$ on the control and \$0.06424\$ on the arm --- **the arm narrower by one and a half per cent**, and only that much because *the arm's visibility is \$14.6\$ per cent WIDER in conformal time while its phase accumulates \$13.7\$ per cent SLOWER per unit of it* (`Jac` \$0.789\$--\$0.913\$ against an identical \$1\$), the product coming to \$0.9890\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the two-rate structure reaches the window and then very nearly cancels inside it***  **(1)\textsuperscript{b} THE ANALYTIC PREDICTION, PRE-REGISTERED**: \$\textbackslash\{\}mathcal D\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm cr\}/\textbackslash\{\}mathcal D\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm lcdm\}=1.00078\textbackslash\{\}to1.01654\$ against a measured \$1.0215\textbackslash\{\}to1.0762\$ --- **R5 (sign) survives, R2 (size) fires at \$0.223\$ of the bar, R1 (shape) fires because the measured excess extrapolates to \$1.0400\$ at \$q=0\$ where a characteristic function is identically \$1\$**   **AND R3's BRACKET WAS WRONG IN DIRECTION, WHICH IS MINE**: `PREDICTION.md` argued the contrast responds between \$f\$ and \$f\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$; measured it responds \$2.3\$ to \$26\$ times MORE, at both coefficients and in every band, and the condition passed only because its tolerance was written on \$f\$ rather than on \$f-1\$  **THREE WIRINGS, THE FIRST TWO NOT THE OPERATION**: the all-terms taper cuts the ISW's own \$\textbackslash\{\}eta\$-integral to \$0.656\$ and \$0.238\$ of itself (moving \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1\$ by \$7.7\$ and \$27.3\$), and holding the window's total weight fixed then lands on the ISW-preserving run to parts in ten thousand --- ***so that second diagnosis of mine was wrong too, and the over-response is the statistic's own sensitivity***   **THE SIZE, FROM THE INSTRUMENT'S OWN TWO-COEFFICIENT RESPONSE RATHER THAN A MODEL OF IT**: the arm's own narrowing delivers **\$23\$ to \$45\$ per cent of the band excess above \$q=1.9\$**, and the direct swap closes \$20\$ to \$39\$ per cent there --- *the same answer by a different road* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} the narrowing for ALL of it is \$4.5\$ per cent against the arm's \$1.7\$, **a factor \$2.7\$**   **(1)\textsuperscript{c} R4, AND THE REFUTATION FROM 66's OWN PREVIOUS REVISION**: `cc66.46` measured the excess symmetric about the envelope, and this channel is not --- the arm-sized swap delivers **\$81\$ per cent of the peak-height excess and \$14\$ per cent of the trough-depth excess, a \$5.7\$-fold asymmetry** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***a peak-weighted channel cannot carry a symmetric excess alone, whatever its size, and that argument is independent of every size estimate***  the taper that would deliver the whole excess moves \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1\$ by \$18.5\$ multipoles and the heights by \$45\$ per cent; the arm-sized one overshoots \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1\$ past the arm's own value while \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_2\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_3\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_4\$ stay inside the sky's widths
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the normalisation across the window is the mechanism** --- it carries about a third, it is peak-weighted where the excess is symmetric, and R1's \$q\$-independent offset is outside it  **NOT that it is nothing**: it is the first channel in this sector measured to carry any definite share, from a quantity nobody chose  **NOT a claim about the term mix**, which is reported (the arm holds more of its window's power in the monopole in every band, \$0.1837\textbackslash\{\}to0.2171\$ at \$q=1.2\$) and **not swapped**  **NOT a detection**: the \$1.8\$ of `cc66.46` is one statistic's spread and nothing here moves it  NOT a verdict on the two-rate assignment; NOT a re-derivation of `PO-47`'s peak spreads, which are quoted  *The lowest band is the one the inversion cannot be trusted in and that is stated rather than averaged away: there the swap overshoots, taking the excess below unity*  No refit, nothing touching `prop:flat` or the clock family, and **no corpus edits** --- the paper-side consequences are routed to 66
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_windows\_phase\_spread\_is\_nearly\_equal\_because\_the\_visibility\_widens\_as\_the\_leaf\_clock\_slows.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_term_mix_has_the_shape_the_offset_needs_and_over_delivers_its_size}{P15R194}\textbf{P15R194}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_term\_mix\_has\_the\_shape\_the\_offset\_needs\_and\_over\_delivers\_its\_size}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound` --- the term mix has the shape the \$q\$-independent offset needs and over-delivers its size by half again, so the two channels do not add and the "remainder" is a construct of the composition rule} \ (node 66's `r6975` order, three items. (1) ***"Decompose the excess into the peak-weighted part and the remainder, explicitly, and characterise the remainder rather than the whole"***; (2) ***"say what class of mechanism can produce a symmetric excess at all, before hunting one"*** ---  *"not a fitted direction and not a scan over knobs --- a statement about what kind of thing it has to be, with the arithmetic that says so"*; (3) ***"the term mix you reported and did not swap ... swap it and report what it does to the contrast, the depths and the comb, with the prediction stated first"***.  Guards: path provenance (standing);  **the pre-registration lesson from my own `cc66.47`** --- *"write the tolerance on the quantity that would move, not on the quantity it is derived from"*; keep the two statistics apart; no detection language). 
\emph{Computes:} **(1) THE REMAINDER.** Dividing the measured excess by what the window channel delivers at the arm's own size leaves \$1.0184\$ at \$q=0\$ --- **\$46\$ per cent of the offset R1 fired on** --- carrying \$123\$ per cent of the measured \$q\textasciicircum{}\{2\}\$ slope, and trough-weighted at \$0.49\$ where the channel that left it is peak-weighted at \$12.44\$  **(2) AND THE STATISTIC HAS A BASELINE I HAD NOT COMPUTED.** Applying each named class to the control's own spectrum and re-reading it with the same envelope and locator: amplitude \$1.96\$, envelope \$1.96\$, loading \$0.63\$, smearing \$-5.28\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***a SYMMETRIC operation reads \$1.95\$ here and not \$1\$***, stable across a fivefold range of operation sizes, because the running-mean envelope is recomputed and shifts the oscillation upward by a constant \ensuremath{\Rightarrow}  **so the anchored reading CONFIRMS `cc66.46`'s variance split rather than contradicting it** --- \$2.20\$ against \$1.96\$ is agreement to twelve per cent, and my own note that the two statistics disagreed is retracted in the pre-registration before the runs landed  **(3) THE TERM-MIX SWAP, ON A KNOB ALREADY WIRED.** The coefficient giving the control the arm's monopole fraction is solved from the profiles and comes out \$0.860\$--\$0.897\$ in all seven bands --- ***one constant to two per cent, so the difference is a single scalar on the Doppler and not a reshaping***   **T1 the sign passes** (the contrast rises in every band)   **T2 the shape passes and is the finding: the response is \$q\$-INDEPENDENT**, the slope worth three per cent of the intercept (\$0.031\$ of it) with an intercept of \$1.0805\$ at \$q=0\$ --- ***the first channel measured with the signature the offset needs, where a smearing's characteristic function is identically one***   **T3\ensuremath{{}^\prime} the weighting FIRES**: \$2.23\$ and \$2.21\$ at the two coefficients, peak-weighted at the symmetric baseline, where the remainder it was to be is \$0.49\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **the term mix is not the remainder**   **and it OVER-DELIVERS**, raising the control's contrast by \$1.10\$ to \$2.77\$ times the whole excess, mean \$1.58\$   **T4 the comb fires and in the wrong direction**: \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_1\$ \$-1.04\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\_2\$ \$-1.79\$, both outside the sky's widths, where the arm sits at \$+1.61\$   **THE CONSEQUENCE, WHICH IS THE RESULT.** The window channel's third and the term mix's \$1.6\$ times together make \$1.72\$ times what is measured \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***they cannot both be present at their measured sizes and simply compose --- and (1)'s remainder is a RATIO of two responses, so it is a construct of that composition rule and not a residual channel***, which is why its \$0.49\$ and the swap's \$2.23\$ are not in conflict   **AND MY OWN (2) IDENTIFICATION IS REFUTED BY (3)**: I argued the Doppler fills the oscillation and removing it should read as the loading class near \$0.6\$; it reads \$2.23\$, and the profiles say why --- its band-to-band power tracks the monopole's at correlation \$0.95\$, so it is an \item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_lowest_band_is_closed_by_the_fields_and_the_excess_deceleration_is_carried_by_it_alone}{P15R200}\textbf{P15R200}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_lowest\_band\_is\_closed\_by\_the\_fields\_and\_the\_excess\_deceleration\_is\_carried\_by\_it\_alone}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound` --- **the lowest band is closed to any oscillation-amplitude estimator by the FIELDS, at an exact floor**; the excess's deceleration is robust in sign and is **carried by that same band alone** --- and **is not robust to the envelope's definition**, which this seat reports against its own result; and the filter's third condition is permanently unavailable **exactly for the class of candidate the physics says can carry the excess**} \ (node 66's `r7003` order, three items. (1) ***can the lowest band be reached by any estimator, or is it closed by the fields?*** --- *"is that a property of the band's width, of its placement, or of the fields themselves... if the fields close it, then say so as a structural statement"*; (2) ***is the TARGET's own curvature robust enough to carry the claim the paper makes?*** --- *"I would rather learn that from you than from a reader"*; (3) ***what can the filter still decide*** --- *"is a two-condition filter with the third permanently unavailable simply not a filter?"*; (4) ***nothing else***.  Guards: ***a stability check licenses the statistic it was run on and no other***; sign first, growth after; **a width --- or a period, or a curvature --- is not a quantity until its definition is named**; and ***pre-register the ways the measurement can fail to decide, not only its outcomes***). 
\emph{Computes:}  **(1) THE BAND IS CLOSED BY THE FIELDS AND THERE IS AN EXACT FLOOR.** The criterion is named before use and is a necessary condition from identifiability rather than a chosen threshold: *an oscillation amplitude at \$q\_0\$ is identified only if the window holds a TURNING POINT on each side of \$q\_0\$*, because over a span with no turning point the held-period pair is monotone and the baseline polynomial already spans it  **The monopole's turning points are \$0.996,\textbackslash\{\},1.915,\textbackslash\{\},2.912,\textbackslash\{\}dots\$ with NONE below the first** --- the first excursion is one-sided --- giving a floor \$q=1.455\$ against the dipole's \$0.962\$, so **the monopole binds** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the filter's lowest band, \$q\textbackslash\{\}in[0.85,1.55]\$, lies \$86\$ per cent below that floor and every other band lies entirely above it***  **And it is not the window, shown three ways**: the same eight widths at identical conditioning spread \$0.004\$ at \$q\_0=1.90\$ and \$0.016\$ at \$1.20\$ with a sign change (*width*); sweeping the left edge, the departure is negative for every window opening past the monopole's first turning point and positive for every one staying above it, *what lies below being the field's rise from its initial condition and not an acoustic oscillation* (*placement*); and **every centre below the floor spreads by more than every centre above it**, which the floor was not fitted to (*prediction*) \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***no estimator of an oscillation amplitude can reach inside the first excursion***   **(2) AND THE TARGET'S CURVATURE, IN THREE PARTS.**  ****(a)** robust within the statistic as defined**: negative in all six variants moving the envelope width, band edges and sampling, \$-0.00377\$ to \$-0.00291\$, with band 1 stable at \$+0.0201\$ to \$+0.0240\$  ****(b)** and carried by band 1 alone**: with band 1 dropped the curvature runs \$-0.00121\$ to \$+0.00031\$, *sign not determined* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the deceleration is the lowest band being LOW, not the upper bands bending***  ****(c)** and NOT robust to the envelope's DEFINITION** --- a running median, everything else unchanged, ***reverses it to \$+0.00504\$***; that is a different statistic (its envelope absorbs about half the top band's oscillation on both arms) **but it is not obviously the worse envelope**, so the honest reading is that the sign belongs to the statistic rather than to the excess   **(3) AND THE THIRD CONDITION IS NOT UNAVAILABLE IN GENERAL, WHICH IS WORSE THAN IF IT WERE**: it needs a band-1 value, and band 1 is closed to *amplitude* estimators but not to a candidate COMPUTED from the kernel --- `cc66.52`'s projection width had one and was excluded on curvature \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the filter's full strength is available exactly for the class that cannot carry the excess, and its third condition is permanently unavailable exactly for the class that can***, since `cc66.51` settled on physics that what fills a trough is an oscillation amplitude  **As an exclusion device it has lost nothing** --- each remaining condition is necessary and the exclusions already made were made on growth alone --- **and as a confirmation device it never worked, not with three either**: all three are conditions on SHAPE, and the candidate matches sign and growth direction while being \$5.3\$ times smaller at the top band \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *the row's remaining question is the coupling, which is quantitative and needs band 1 not at all*
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that band 1 is hard to measure** --- it is closed, and the distinction is the result  **NOT that the deceleration claim must come out**: it is supported on its own statistic  **NOT that it is independent of that statistic** --- the median envelope reverses it, and that exposure is reported rather than argued away  **NOT that the candidate is confirmed or excluded**: it passes what can be applied, which is not a promotion, and a missing condition is not an acquittal  **NOT a new channel** --- the coupling question is named as the question, not proposed; the order's (4) closes the list  NOT a detection; NOT a verdict on the two-rate assignment  Nothing SOLVED; **no corpus edits**

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_coupling_is_measured_and_negative_and_the_contribution_is_undetermined_because_its_input_is}{P15R201}\textbf{P15R201}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_coupling\_is\_measured\_and\_negative\_and\_the\_contribution\_is\_undetermined\_because\_its\_input\_is}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound` --- **the coupling between the Doppler amplitude and the band contrast is MEASURED, is negative at every band on both envelopes over every interval, and is fixed by the construction**; and the candidate's contribution is **still undetermined, because the two readings of what the coupling multiplies disagree in SIGN** --- the third revision these two readings have decided an answer between them and the first in which they decide its sign} \ (node 66's `r7005` order, three items. (1) ***the size, with the same discipline the shape work had*** --- *"what fraction of the excess does it carry... and say whether the shortfall is constant across the range or grows --- a constant shortfall is a coupling, a growing one is a second shape mismatch"*; (2) ***and what would set it*** --- *"is the coupling... something the instrument fixes, or something the construction fixes? If the construction fixes it, the size is a prediction and not a fit"*; (3) ***and one thing about the statistic*** --- *"does the candidate's contribution reverse with [the median envelope], or does it survive both?"*; (4) ***nothing else***.  Guards: ***a claim and its implied independence are two claims***; **name the criterion before using it, and prefer a necessary condition to a threshold**; a stability check licenses the statistic it was run on and no other; **pre-register the failure modes ahead of the outcomes**.  And: *do not revisit the envelope question to settle it*). 
\emph{Computes:}  **THE COUPLING IS MEASURED, NOT MODELLED.** `DPSRC` scales the Doppler source term, so the three banked control spectra at `DPSRC` \$=1,\textbackslash\{\},0.8794,\textbackslash\{\},0.6\$ give \$d\textbackslash\{\}ln C/d\textbackslash\{\}ln s\$ per band as a finite difference --- ***three points, so the linearity is CHECKED and not assumed***, and it steepens towards \$s=1\$ at all seven bands, so the local slope there is both the applicable one and the one measured over the shortest step  Near \$s=1\$ on the arithmetic envelope it runs \$-0.45\$ to \$-0.80\$ with **no trend in \$q\$** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***NEGATIVE AT EVERY BAND, ON BOTH ENVELOPES, OVER EVERY INTERVAL --- forty-two measurements of the sign and not one positive*** --- *more Doppler fills more trough and LOWERS the contrast, which is `cc66.51`'s own trough-filling physics read forwards*   **(2) AND THE CONSTRUCTION FIXES IT, SHOWN BY WHAT DOES NOT CHANGE IT**: the control's own Doppler-to-monopole band-power share runs \$4.30\$ down to \$0.65\$, **a factor of \$6.6\$ across the bands, and the coupling's sign is the same at every one** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *a quantity whose sign is invariant across a factor of six in the very ratio it depends on is not set by a knob* --- `DPSRC` is the **probe**, not the setter, ***so the size is a prediction and not a fit***  *and the one thing not measured is stated: all three `DPSRC` banks are the CONTROL, so the arm's coupling is assumed to be the control's; its share differs by about a fifth against the factor of six across which the sign does not move, so the assumption cannot reach the sign and is not asked to reach more*   **(1) AND THE CONTRIBUTION IS NOT DETERMINED --- NOT BECAUSE OF THE COUPLING BUT BECAUSE OF ITS INPUT.** The criterion was named before use: *the coupling multiplies a fractional change in the Doppler's oscillation amplitude relative to the monopole's* --- and two measured quantities claim to be it and **disagree in sign**: the FIELD reading (`cc66.51`'s held-period estimator at last scattering) has the arm **higher** by \$+0.5\$ to \$+1.4\$ per cent \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} contribution \$-0.004\$ to \$-0.010\$, **\$-7\$ to \$-13\$ per cent of the excess, the WRONG SIGN**; the PROJECTED reading (`cc66.50`'s quantity, the square root of the banked `w2dp/w2sw` share, **which is what `DPSRC` actually scales**) has it **lower** by \$10\$ to \$13\$ per cent \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} contribution \$+0.066\$ to \$+0.108\$, **\$+188\$ down to \$+94\$ per cent, the RIGHT SIGN** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the coupling turns a disagreement about a quantity into a disagreement about whether the candidate helps at all --- a factor of seven in magnitude even at their closest***  **And the order's second half: the fraction is NOT constant on either reading** --- it grows in magnitude on the field reading and FALLS on the projected one \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***a SHAPE MISMATCH and not a coupling deficit, on either, and the fourth this sector has found***; the coupling being flat, the \$q\$-dependence belongs to the input   **(3) AND THE ENVELOPE QUESTION HAS THE GOOD ANSWER**: the coupling's sign survives both envelopes at every band, so **the contribution does not reverse with the statistic and the coupling question does not inherit the envelope ambiguity** --- though its magnitude does, the median envelope's coupling reaching \$2.2\textbackslash\{\}times\$ the arithmetic one's at the top band, *so a quoted FRACTION inherits the ambiguity while the SIGN does not, said before any fraction is quoted*
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the candidate is excluded** --- one reading of its input has it carrying essentially all of the excess  **NOT that it is the carrier** --- the other has it working against the excess, and neither is chosen  **NOT a resolution of the two readings**: `cc66.51` chose between them once and this revision shows the choice decides the sign, so choosing again is exactly what is not done  **NOT an envelope choice** --- the order forbids it; both are carried and no conclusion rests on one  **NOT a claim about the arm's coupling** --- it is the control's, transferred, and only as far as the sign  **NOT a new channel**: the bank that would settle the input is named, not proposed  Nothing SOLVED; **no corpus edits**
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_coupling\_is\_measured\_and\_negative\_and\_the\_contribution\_is\_undetermined\_because\_its\_input\_is.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_bank_already_existed_and_the_doppler_carries_a_growing_minority_of_the_excess}{P15R202}\textbf{P15R202}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_bank\_already\_existed\_and\_the\_doppler\_carries\_a\_growing\_minority\_of\_the\_excess}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\

(1) ***bank the oscillation amplitude of the projected contribution*** --- *"say what the instrument has to emit for that... and if it cannot emit it without a change this seat should not make unilaterally, say that and stop"*; (2) ***then the contribution, with the fraction carrying both envelopes***, pre-registering every outcome ***"including the one where the right quantity lands between the two wrong ones and settles nothing"***; (3) ***and the fourth shape mismatch counted rather than passed over*** --- *"four is a pattern rather than a run of bad luck... if they share a reason, that reason is the sector's real finding"*; (4) nothing else. And: *do not choose an envelope*, still). 
\emph{Computes:}  **(1) THE INSTRUMENT ALREADY EMITS IT AND THE BANK IS ALREADY ON DISK.** `SRCDEC`, added at `r6915+cc66.41`, writes the full bilinear decomposition --- \$C\_\textbackslash\{\}ell = \textbackslash\{\}sum\_\{a\textbackslash\{\}le b\} w\_\{ab\}\textbackslash\{\}sum\_k P\textbackslash\{\},\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textasciicircum{}a\textbackslash\{\}Delta\textasciicircum{}b\$, ***ten \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$-resolved terms per multipole summing to \$C\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ exactly*** --- and `r6915\_pairs\_\{lcdm,cr\}` carry them for both arms, to \$1.2\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-15\}\$ \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **no run, no instrument change, and the order's escape hatch is not needed**  *And the coarse grid is checked rather than assumed: \$238\$ multipoles against \$1900\$ reads the contrast \$1.9\$ per cent low, and the deficit agrees between the arms to \$3\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-4\}\$, so it CANCELS in the ratio --- the only thing read from it*   **AND THE BANK DISCHARGES A CAVEAT `cc66.55` COULD ONLY STATE**: the \$s\$-scaling of every pair is exact (\$s\textasciicircum{}2\$ on `dp*dp`, \$s\$ on the three `dp` crosses), so \$dD\_\textbackslash\{\}ell/d\textbackslash\{\}ln s\$ is exact per multipole and **the coupling follows analytically FOR THE ARM as well as the control** --- \$-0.48\$ to \$-0.86\$ on each, **equal between the arms to \$4\$ per cent** and agreeing with `cc66.55`'s three-point finite difference to \$7\$   **AND THE FIRST CONSTRUCTION IS REPORTED AS FAILED RATHER THAN DROPPED**: \$\textbackslash\{\}sqrt\{\textbackslash\{\}mathrm\{osc\}(\textbackslash\{\}texttt\{dp*dp\})/\textbackslash\{\}mathrm\{osc\}(\textbackslash\{\}texttt\{sw*sw\})\}\$ is small on the arithmetic envelope and reaches \$+134\$ per cent under a median one, *because the oscillation of a weak smooth term about a median envelope is not well conditioned* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} that definition inherits the envelope ambiguity through its **own** conditioning, so it is not used   **WHAT IS USED NEEDS NO EQUIVALENT-\$s\$ STEP: A ONE-AT-A-TIME SWAP** --- the arm's four Doppler-containing pairs replaced by the control's, envelope-scaled, on the arm's own \$q\$ grid, the share being \$(C\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm arm\}-C\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm swapped\})/(C\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm arm\}-C\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm control\})\$; *same shape of operation as `SRCINJRS`'s clock swap, which the instrument already sanctions*   **(2) AND THE CONTRIBUTION IS A MINORITY SHARE THAT GROWS AND IS SIGN-INDEFINITE AT THE BOTTOM**: \$-12\$ to \$+17\$ per cent on the arithmetic envelope, \$-20\$ to \$+48\$ on the median one, negative at \$q=1.90\$ on both \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the candidate is NOT the carrier***  **It lands between the two wrong readings, which said \$-13\$ and \$+188\$ per cent** --- the outcome the order required be tabled first --- ***and it does not settle nothing: it settles that the candidate is a real but minority contributor, which neither wrong reading said***  *And `cc66.55`'s split is confirmed rather than assumed: the coupling's SIGN is the same on both envelopes while the FRACTION moves by \$2.7\textbackslash\{\}times\$*   **(3) AND THE FOUR MISMATCHES DO SHARE A REASON, AND IT IS ABOUT THE EXCESS.** The excess has ONE dominant feature: ***band 1 is \$0.33\$ of the mean of the rest and sits \$4.9\$ scatters below it***, while above band 1 a constant already fits (\$0.0088\$ scatter on a mean of \$0.0645\$) and a line only halves the residual \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the excess's \$q\$-structure is a STEP at the lowest band, not a trend --- and all four candidates were judged on a trend across bands 2--7, where the excess is very nearly featureless***: a flat candidate matches the flat part and misses the step, a rising one matches the mild rise and misses the step, and there the two are barely distinguishable \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **the sector has been spending its discriminating power where the excess has least structure, because the part that has the structure is the band `cc66.54` showed it cannot measure a candidate in**
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the excess is explained** --- a minority share is not an explanation  **NOT that the candidate is excluded**: it contributes measurably and its share grows  **NOT a definite share at the bottom bands** --- negative at \$q=1.90\$ on both envelopes, so it cannot be told from zero exactly where the excess's structure is  **NOT an envelope choice**: both carried, the order's refusal held for the second revision  **NOT a claim on the failed construction's arithmetic number** --- it is reported as failed, with its reason, and is not quoted as the answer  **NOT a new channel**: nothing proposed; (4) holds  Nothing SOLVED, nothing RUN; **no corpus edits**

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_step_is_the_excesss_own_and_it_sits_at_the_second_acoustic_peak_and_none_of_the_four_produces_it}{P15R203}\textbf{P15R203}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_step\_is\_the\_excesss\_own\_and\_it\_sits\_at\_the\_second\_acoustic\_peak\_and\_none\_of\_the\_four\_produces\_it}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound`, `sec:scope` --- **the step is the excess's and not the estimator's**, surviving three envelopes and one reading that needs none, with the edge hazard ruled out the right way round; **it sits at the SECOND ACOUSTIC PEAK to \$0.9\$ per cent** and is sharper than the window that found it; and **all four candidates can be scored on it and none of them produces it**} \ (node 66's `r7009` order, three items, **sequenced**. (1) ***is the step the excess's or the estimator's --- pre-register that before anything else*** --- *"a step at the lowest band is exactly where an envelope artefact would sit... I would rather lose the step now than build two revisions on it"*; (2) ***then locate it as a scale*** --- *"if the step's location coincides with a scale the construction fixes, that is the carrier's signature and it carries no free coefficient"*; (3) ***and re-score the four candidates on the step rather than on the trend***; (4) nothing else.  Guards: ***a step at the edge band is where an edge artefact lives***; the knob is the probe and not the setter; do not choose between two readings, name the quantity; **report a failed construction as failed and date it**; pre-register the failure modes including the one that embarrasses the revision.  And: *do not choose an envelope --- add a third*). 
\emph{Computes:}  **THE SEQUENCING WAS HONOURED AND IS CHECKED**: the pre-registration says in terms that nothing in (1) had been computed when it was written, names the step's definition before use (*band 1's excess over the mean of bands 2--7*), and **tables FIRST the outcome that withdraws `cc66.56`'s (3)** --- which `r7009` had already landed in two sections   **(1) THE STEP SURVIVES EVERY READING.** Three envelopes differing in **kind** --- running arithmetic mean, running median, local quadratic (Savitzky--Golay) --- and one that needs **no window at all**, the peak-to-trough depth per acoustic cycle: step ratios \$0.333\$, \$0.391\$, \$0.633\$, \$-0.025\$, ***never near one***  *And the envelope-free reading is the sharpest: band 1's excess on it is \$-0.0017\$, indistinguishable from zero, against a bands 2--7 mean of \$+0.0654\$*   **AND THE EDGE HAZARD IS RULED OUT THE RIGHT WAY ROUND, which is what makes it ruled out**: band 1's envelope comes within \$0.018\$ in \$q\$ of truncating, but moving its lower edge UP from \$0.85\$ to \$1.15\$ makes it read \$+0.0215 \textbackslash\{\}to +0.0361\$ --- **higher, where a truncation artefact would have to make it read lower**  *Its SIZE is reading-dependent and is quoted as a range --- band 1 between none and two thirds of the rest --- not as a number; no envelope is chosen*   **(2) AND IT SITS AT THE SECOND ACOUSTIC PEAK.** The half-rise point in sliding windows at three widths is \$q = 1.789,\textbackslash\{\},1.788,\textbackslash\{\},1.805\$ --- **\$1.794 \textbackslash\{\}pm 0.008\$** --- against the second peak at \$1.7775\$ (control) and \$1.7760\$ (arm): ***a match to \$0.9\$ per cent, where the nearest other scale the construction fixes --- the monopole's second turning point at \$1.915\$ --- is seven per cent away and \$q=2\$ is twelve***  And the plateaux are \$0.026\$ below and \$0.053\$ above on all three widths: **a factor of \$2.0\$ reached within about a tenth of a comb period, sharper than the narrowest window that found it** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***the excess is one size in the first acoustic cycle and twice that in every cycle above it***   **(3) AND ALL FOUR CAN BE SCORED ON THE STEP, AND NONE PRODUCES IT.** The window weighting and the term mix were measured **flat in \$q\$**, and a flat candidate's step ratio is exactly one, by construction and without a new number; the **projection width** (kernel-class, reachable at band 1) is fitted by a straight line in \$q\$ to \$2.6\$ per cent of its own range, *so linear, no step*; and the **Doppler by the swap route that reaches band 1** runs \$-0.0016\$ to \$+0.0014\$ in sliding half-period windows with means \$-0.0001\$ below the step and \$-0.0002\$ above --- **noise at the step's resolution and no transition at all** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***this is NOT the identifiability floor closing over the list --- every one of the four WAS evaluable --- it is the list EXHAUSTED against the right feature, for the first time***
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the step is a mechanism** --- *a step at a scale is a signature to be explained*, which the pre-registration said would not be claimed  **NOT a step of quoted size**: band 1 reads between none and two thirds of the rest depending on the reading, and the range is the claim  **NOT an envelope choice** --- a third was added, none chosen, for the third revision running  **NOT that the second-peak coincidence is arm-specific**: the peaks sit at fixed \$q\$ on both arms, so what the step says is that the FIRST acoustic cycle differs  **NOT the identifiability floor closing over the list** --- all four were evaluable, which is a weaker and more useful finding than that would have been  **NOT a new channel**: (4) holds and nothing is proposed  Nothing SOLVED, nothing RUN; **no corpus edits**

\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_window_free_reading_cannot_resolve_the_first_cycle_the_flatness_was_a_trend_and_nine_tenths_of_the_step_is_common_to_both_arms}{P15R204}\textbf{P15R204}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_window\_free\_reading\_cannot\_resolve\_the\_first\_cycle\_the\_flatness\_was\_a\_trend\_and\_nine\_tenths\_of\_the\_step\_is\_common\_to\_both\_arms}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound`, `sec:scope` --- **the window-free reading cannot resolve the first acoustic cycle, and that is the answer**: its band 1 rests on ONE half-cycle transition, so it is consistent with zero AND with no step at once; **the flatness that disposed of two candidates was measured as a TREND, so the list is NOT exhausted**; and **nine tenths of the step is common to BOTH arms**, the excess's step being the tenth that fails to cancel} \ (node 66's `r7011` order, three items. (1) ***is the excess ZERO in the first cycle, or merely smaller*** --- *"that is not 'a third of the rest', that is ZERO"*, asked of the window-free reading *"with the uncertainty it actually has"*, and *"if a finer version of the same window-free quantity exists --- half-cycles, or the depth of the first trough alone --- use it"*; (2) ***and was the flatness of the window weighting and the term mix measured across the full range, or across bands 2--7*** --- *"that reasoning is airtight if the flatness was measured including band 1, and circular if it was measured on the featureless stretch"*; (3) ***and where the step lives: in the arm, in the control, or only in the difference***; (4) nothing else.  Guards: ***a conclusion drawn on the featureless stretch inherits its featurelessness***; rule out a hazard by the sign of its effect, not by a distance; quote a range when the quantity is reading-dependent; **a step at the edge band is where an edge artefact lives**; ***and pre-register the outcome that costs you most, first***). 
\emph{Computes:}  **THE SEQUENCING, CHECKED AGAINST THE FILE**: the pre-registration says in terms that nothing below it had been measured, names each finer window-free member and the definition of a step in an ABSOLUTE contrast *before use* (twice, gated on the count), and **tables the damaging outcome FIRST in every one of the three items**   **(1) THE WINDOW-FREE FAMILY CANNOT ANSWER, AND THE REASON IS STRUCTURAL.** Its band 1 rests on **exactly ONE half-cycle transition**, because the first locatable extremum pair sits at \$q = 1.363\$ and the step is at \$1.794\$: so \$-0.0008\$ is \$0.02\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ from ZERO *and* \$1.60\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ from the bands 2--7 mean --- ***consistent with both and informative about neither***  *And no finer member escapes it: the **extremal envelope** --- successive maxima and successive minima interpolated separately, no running window anywhere --- covers only \$27\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of band 1, rests on the same single extremum, and interpolates ACROSS the step, so its \$+0.0245\$ is biased TOWARD the above-step value*  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **SO THE ANSWER IS THE STATISTICS THAT DO RESOLVE BAND 1, AND THERE THE EXCESS IS \$+0.0215\$**: ***"small but non-zero" is the supported row and the step stays a step rather than becoming an onset***   **`cc66.57`'s "the sharpest of the four" is WITHDRAWN --- it was the COARSEST**, by one datum against four hundred; *and its "none" endpoint goes with it as UNRESOLVED rather than as a reading, which **tightens** the size to between a third and two thirds --- \$0.333\$, \$0.391\$, \$0.633\$ windowed and \$0.338\$ on the extremal envelope*   **(2) THE RANGE WAS FULL, BAND 1 INCLUDED --- ANSWERED FROM `cc66.49`'s OWN FILE AND GATED ON IT --- SO NOT CIRCULAR ON RANGE. BUT THE STATISTIC WAS A TREND.** \$\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}text\{slope\}\textbackslash\{\}times\textbackslash\{\}langle q\textasciicircum{}2\textbackslash\{\}rangle\textbackslash\{\}rvert/\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}text\{intercept\}\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$ cannot exclude a step, which lives in the **residual**: the JOINT reads \$0.004\$ on that statistic while leaving \$61\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of its own range unexplained by that line  *Re-scored on band 1's departure from its own bands 2--7 trend, **across four trend bases with none chosen**: the WINDOW channel steps **UPWARD** \$+45\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ to \$+56\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ --- the wrong sign --- the TERM MIX **DOWNWARD** at \$63\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of the excess's own \$-57\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ to \$-60\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, and the pair as actually composed at \$42\textbackslash\{\}\%\$*  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***`cc66.57`'s (3) IS WITHDRAWN IN PART AND THE LIST IS NOT EXHAUSTED***: *"a flat candidate's step ratio is exactly one, by construction and without a new number" was never earned*  *And the projection width's disposal STANDS, which is what the basis table is for: it is linear in \$q\$, so it **flips sign across the bases** and a log basis would have manufactured a step for it --- `cc66.57` scored that one on a residual from a straight line in \$q\$*   **(3) AND BOTH ARMS STEP, NEARLY EQUALLY.** On every one of the four bases and on **both** the windowed and the window-free contrast: the control departs from its own bands 2--7 trend by \$+30\$ to \$+44\$ per cent at band 1 and the arm by \$+27\$ to \$+39\$, **the arm always the SHALLOWER, by some eleven per cent of the step**  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***SO OF A STEP WORTH TENS OF PER CENT IN EACH SPECTRUM, ABOUT NINE TENTHS IS COMMON TO THE TWO ARMS AND CANCELS IN THE RATIO; THE EXCESS'S STEP IS THE TENTH THAT DOES NOT***  *That is the pre-registration's "both arms" row --- the step belongs to the acoustic physics the two share --- and it explains, without a new number, why `cc66.57` found the location at fixed \$q\$ on BOTH arms*
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the excess is ZERO below the step** --- the order's stronger claim is *not established*, and *not excluded* either, because the one reading that suggested it has no resolving power there  **NOT that any candidate produces the step** --- the term mix carries a minority of it in the right direction and the window channel pushes the wrong way, which is a PARTIAL account and not a producer  **NOT that the flatness was measured circularly** --- the range was full; it is the STATISTIC that was wrong, and that is the half this seat owns  **NOT a mechanism, an envelope, a basis, or a new candidate** --- (4) holds; four bases are reported and none chosen, for the fourth revision running on the same discipline  ***AND THE CAUTION IS THIS SEAT'S, NOT THE ORDER'S***: a small residual of two large common features is exactly where a nearly-common systematic would sit, so the next reading of the step should be **differential by construction** rather than a difference of two large numbers  Nothing SOLVED, nothing RUN; **no corpus edits**
