\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_step_survives_a_differential_estimator_and_no_single_bilinear_term_carries_the_shared_step_though_the_doppler_leads_it}{P15R205}\textbf{P15R205}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_step\_survives\_a\_differential\_estimator\_and\_no\_single\_bilinear\_term\_carries\_the\_shared\_step\_though\_the\_doppler\_leads\_it}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound`, `sec:scope` --- **the step SURVIVES an estimator in which the two large common steps are never formed**, so the tenth that failed to cancel is not the residual of two large numbers; **no single bilinear term carries the shared step and it is a property of the SUM**, though the two highest-leverage terms both carry a Doppler factor; and **the "42 per cent" is 42 per cent of the EXCESS's step**, not of the shared one} \ (node 66's `r7015` order, three items, on this seat's own closing caution. (1) ***build the differential estimator: ratio first, contrast second*** --- *"everything so far forms each arm's contrast and then compares --- two large numbers each carrying a large step, with the object of interest in the difference"*, and the test named in advance: *"does the step survive at the size the present route gives it, and at what significance"*; (2) ***name the shared nine tenths, because the bank already can*** --- *"does the shared step live in one term, or in a few"*, and *"a better-posed residual is worth more than a bigger one"*; (3) ***say which step the 42 per cent is 42 per cent of*** --- *"if the pair carries two fifths of the EXCESS's step that is a large result; if it carries two fifths of the SHARED step, most of what it accounts for cancels"*; (4) nothing else.  Guards: **a statistic that fits a trend cannot see a step --- score on the residual**; "envelope-free" is about bias, not resolution; ***a small residual of two large common features is where a nearly-common systematic sits***; ***and when a feature is mostly common, say which part a share is a share of***; pre-register the outcome that costs you most, first --- **and commit it separately**). 
\emph{Computes:}  **THE SEQUENCING**: `PREDICTION.md` is its own commit ahead of the working script, tables FIRST the outcome that ***ends the row's present object***, and names **both** hazards the new estimator carries --- its phase sensitivity and the conditioning of a division whose divisor passes through the first acoustic trough INSIDE the band under test --- before either was looked at   **(1) THE STEP SURVIVES THE DIFFERENTIAL ESTIMATOR.** Writing each arm as \$\textbackslash\{\}mathcal D = E(1+o)\$, the present route forms \$\textbackslash\{\}operatorname\{std\}(o\_a)\$ and \$\textbackslash\{\}operatorname\{std\}(o\_c)\$ and divides; the ordered route forms \$R = \textbackslash\{\}mathcal D\_a/\textbackslash\{\}mathcal D\_c\$ and takes the contrast of **that**, so it reads \$\textbackslash\{\}operatorname\{std\}(o\_a - o\_c)\$ and **the common oscillation divides out before any width is taken**  Band 1's departure from its own bands 2--7 trend is \$-0.374\$ to \$-0.387\$ per common \$\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ and \$-0.382\$ to \$-0.383\$ per common \$q\$: ***same sign on all four trend bases AND both abscissas, at \$3.2\$--\$3.6\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$, about \$65\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of the present route's fractional size***  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***SO THE TENTH THAT FAILED TO CANCEL IS NOT THE RESIDUAL OF TWO LARGE NUMBERS --- IT EXISTS IN AN ESTIMATOR IN WHICH THE TWO LARGE NUMBERS ARE NEVER FORMED***  *And the conditioning hazard does not fire --- the control never falls below \$0.67\$ of its band median --- while the **smoothed-divisor control** turns the departure POSITIVE, back toward the arms' own \$+0.3\$: **it is the differencing, not the division, that produces the negative step**   **(2) NO SINGLE BILINEAR TERM CARRIES THE SHARED STEP.** The 238-multipole `SRCDEC` total first reproduces the 1900-multipole step to better than \$0.006\$ in departure on **both** arms, which is what licenses a per-term reading; then removing each term in turn, ***the losses sum to \$182\textbackslash\{\}\%\$, far more than one*** \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **the step is not additive and is a property of the SUM** --- the pre-registered null   *But the LEVERAGE is not flat, and that is what the null leaves standing: each term's share of the STEP against its share of the OSCILLATION puts the two highest-leverage terms BOTH on a Doppler factor --- `sw*dp` at \$5.59\textbackslash\{\}times\$ and `dp*dp` at \$3.80\textbackslash\{\}times\$, against the best non-Doppler `sw*isw` at \$1.23\textbackslash\{\}times\$ --- while the monopole `sw*sw` carries \$88\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ of the oscillation at only \$0.72\textbackslash\{\}times\$* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **a step-weighted reading of the bilinear decomposition is led by the Doppler where an amplitude-weighted one is led by the monopole**   **(3) THE 42 PER CENT IS 42 PER CENT OF THE EXCESS'S STEP**, read off `cc66.58`'s own file and gated on it: it divided by the departure of `MEAS - 1.0`, and a channel response and the excess are both ratios to the SAME control \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *the large reading*  *And the two normalisations are ONE departure, \$-0.0323\$ in excess units --- \$-0.601\$ of the excess's own extrapolated size and \$-0.100\$ of the control's contrast departure --- **provable rather than asserted, because the arms' own log-departures differ by that same number to \$4.6\$ per cent**
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the differential estimator measures an AMPLITUDE difference** --- \$\textbackslash\{\}operatorname\{std\}(o\_a - o\_c)\$ is sensitive to a **phase** difference too, named in the pre-registration before the measurement: a null on it would have been strong, a signal on it does not by itself say amplitude, and its absolute size carries no expectation  **NOT that the Doppler PRODUCES the shared step** --- ***leverage is not authorship***, and the non-additivity is the reason it is not  **NOT that every Doppler term leads**: `dp*isw` has NEGATIVE leverage at \$-0.91\textbackslash\{\}times\$, and `isw*isw` at \$-2.07\textbackslash\{\}times\$ sits lower still, so the ordering is not Doppler-versus-not  **NOT a naming of the shared step** --- (2) is a null on "one term" and a naming only on "which sector"  **NOT an envelope, a basis, an abscissa, a candidate or a mechanism** --- (4) holds, and **two abscissas are reported with neither chosen**, the same discipline extended to the degree of freedom this estimator introduces  Nothing SOLVED, nothing RUN; **no corpus edits**
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_step\_survives\_a\_differential\_estimator\_and\_no\_single\_bilinear\_term\_carries\_the\_shared\_step\_though\_the\_doppler\_leads\_it.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_surviving_step_is_amplitude_and_not_phase_and_the_candidate_shares_do_not_survive_the_change_of_denominator}{P15R206}\textbf{P15R206}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_surviving\_step\_is\_amplitude\_and\_not\_phase\_and\_the\_candidate\_shares\_do\_not\_survive\_the\_change\_of\_denominator}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound`, `sec:scope` --- **the surviving step is an AMPLITUDE feature and not a PHASE one**, so the first acoustic cycle is WEAKENED rather than displaced and the row is not reframed; **the candidate shares do not survive the change of denominator**, the window channel reversing sign and the realised pair falling to zero; and **the step does not compose the way the contrast does** --- the two knobs nearly cancel where they multiply} \ (node 66's `r7017` order, three items, on this seat's own pre-registered caveat. (1) ***amplitude or phase --- decompose the surviving step*** --- *"the surviving step might not be a contrast feature at all; it might be a phase feature of the first acoustic cycle, and those are different objects with different physics behind them"*, with the reframing outcome to be tabled first and *"an inseparability is a finding here, not a gap"*; (2) ***re-score the candidates on the differential estimator*** --- *"every share in the row is measured on the ratio-of-contrasts route, where nine tenths of the feature enters and cancels"*; (3) ***does the step compose the way the contrast does*** --- *"if it composes differently, that is a new property of the step and it is worth more than the share"*; (4) nothing else.  Guards: ***a differential estimator is a different quantity, not a cleaner version of the same one***; **a share needs its denominator named in the same breath**; leverage is not authorship and a matching size is not authorship either; a statistic that fits a trend cannot see a step; ***and pre-register the outcome that reframes the row, first***). 
\emph{Computes:}  **THE SEQUENCING**: `PREDICTION.md` is its own commit ahead of the working script, tables **phase** --- the reframing outcome --- first, carries an explicit **inseparable** row, puts a licence gate before the reading, and **names the amplitude/phase trade-off hazard before it was met**   **(1) THE DECOMPOSITION IS AN IDENTITY, NOT A FIT.** With \$o\_c = A\_c\textbackslash\{\}cos\textbackslash\{\}psi\$ and \$o\_a = A\_a\textbackslash\{\}cos(\textbackslash\{\}psi+\textbackslash\{\}Delta)\$, \$\textbackslash\{\}operatorname\{std\}(o\_a-o\_c) = \textbackslash\{\}tfrac\{A\_c\}\{\textbackslash\{\}sqrt2\}\textbackslash\{\}sqrt\{r\textasciicircum{}2 - 2r\textbackslash\{\}cos\textbackslash\{\}Delta + 1\}\$, amplitude-only \$\textbackslash\{\}tfrac\{A\_c\}\{\textbackslash\{\}sqrt2\}\textbackslash\{\}lvert r-1\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$ and phase-only \$\textbackslash\{\}tfrac\{A\_c\}\{\textbackslash\{\}sqrt2\}2\textbackslash\{\}lvert\textbackslash\{\}sin(\textbackslash\{\}Delta/2)\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$ --- **reproducing the difference's own measured comb amplitude to \$0.00\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ pointwise**, so the gate passes exactly  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***AND THE ANSWER IS AMPLITUDE***: the amplitude-only term keeps its sign at every window half-width and tracks the full statistic (\$-0.227\$ to \$-0.260\$ against \$-0.204\$ to \$-0.236\$), supplying \$0.958\$ of it at band 1 against phase's \$0.284\$  ***SO THE STEP IS A WEAKENING OF THE FIRST ACOUSTIC CYCLE, NOT A DISPLACEMENT OF IT, AND THE ROW IS NOT REFRAMED***   *But the phase channel is **unresolved, not merely small**: its departure runs \$+3.54\$, \$+0.66\$, \$-0.46\$ across three half-widths and **changes sign** where the amplitude term's does not --- the pre-registered trade-off firing.  The relative phase is \$0.0068\$ rad, \$0.39\$ degrees, against a relative amplitude of \$0.0554\$* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **amplitude on the sign, inseparable on the share**, and what would separate them is named: a phase read against the comb itself over a longer lever arm in \$q\$   **AND A CORRECTION TO `cc66.59` THE DECOMPOSITION FORCED**: a band spans \$0.70\$ in \$q\$ against a comb period of \$1.00\$, so **a raw band `std` samples an incomplete cycle and is phase-dependent by construction** --- the step reads \$-0.45\$ on that route and \$-0.22\$ on the phase-insensitive one, **surviving on both, neither chosen**   **(2) AND THE SHARES DO NOT SURVIVE THE CHANGE OF DENOMINATOR.** On the differential estimator: the **window channel steps \$+1.05\$ to \$+1.32\$, OPPOSITE in sign to the arm's**; the **term mix is no longer a step** where it carried \$63\textbackslash\{\}\%\$; and the **JOINT changes sign across the bases** and measures zero to within its own scatter where it carried \$42\textbackslash\{\}\%\$  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***A CHANNEL THAT ACCOUNTS FOR TWO FIFTHS OF A MOSTLY-SHARED QUANTITY ACCOUNTS FOR NOTHING OF THE PART THAT IS THIS COSMOLOGY'S***   **(3) AND THE STEP DOES NOT COMPOSE THE WAY THE CONTRAST DOES.** Product, sum and quadrature predict \$+0.84\$, \$+1.03\$, \$+1.19\$ where the realised pair measures \$-0.014\$ --- **six to nine of the joint's own scatters away, none fitting** --- against `cc66.49`'s contrast composing multiplicatively, product 7 of 7 \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *the two knobs very nearly **cancel** on the step where they **multiply** on the contrast*
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the phase channel is measured to be small** --- it is **unresolved**: the trade-off fires, its sign moves with the window, and the honest reading is amplitude-on-the-sign with the share inseparable  **NOT that the term mix and the pair are excluded** --- they are **not resolved** on the differential estimator, which is a weaker statement than exclusion and is the one the scatter supports  **NOT a mechanism** --- *"the first acoustic cycle is weaker in this cosmology" is a signature, not a cause*, and (4) holds  **NOT a chosen aggregation**: the raw band `std` and the held-period amplitude are **both** carried, as the four bases and two abscissas are  **NOT a percentage share on (3)** --- the joint's departure is consistent with zero, so residuals are in departure units against its own scatter; ***a percentage of a near-zero measurement would be meaningless, which is this line's own guard about denominators turned on itself***  Nothing SOLVED, nothing RUN; **no corpus edits**
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_the\_surviving\_step\_is\_amplitude\_and\_not\_phase\_and\_the\_candidate\_shares\_do\_not\_survive\_the\_change\_of\_denominator.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_no_statistic_this_construction_can_build_resolves_the_two_channels_and_the_phase_step_dies_on_a_matched_width_control}{P15R207}\textbf{P15R207}\quad\texttt{P15\_no\_statistic\_this\_construction\_can\_build\_resolves\_the\_two\_channels\_and\_the\_phase\_step\_dies\_on\_a\_matched\_width\_control}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound`, `sec:scope` --- **no statistic this construction can build resolves the two channels against the step**: the minimum resolvable share is \$0.59\$--\$0.75\$ on every route banked while the channels carry \$0.35\$ and \$0.03\$; **the phase step dies on a matched-width control**, so the amplitude verdict is complete rather than sign-only; and **the composition cancellation is aggregation-dependent** rather than exact or approximate} \ (node 66's `r7019` order, three items. (1) ***resolve or exclude the two channels on the differential estimator*** --- *"not resolved is right and it is not a terminal state: it means the measurement has not yet the power to decide, and power is a thing that can be measured and sometimes improved"*, with *"if the answer is that nothing available reduces it enough, THAT is `PO-56`'s terminal exit arriving --- a demonstration and not a shrug"*; (2) ***the phase, against the comb, by your own prescription*** --- *"it also tests (1)'s premise from the other side"*; (3) ***is the cancellation exact or approximate*** --- *"an exact cancellation of two large opposite departures is either a coincidence at the sizes their profiles happen to solve, or a CONSTRAINT"*; (4) nothing else.  Guards: an identity is not a fit; **a band narrower than the period it measures makes its own statistic phase-dependent**; a percentage of a near-zero measurement is not a share; ***"not resolved" is not "excluded" --- and it is also not a finish***; and a share measured against a mostly-shared quantity is not a share of the part that is ours). 
\emph{Computes:}  **THE SEQUENCING**: `PREDICTION.md` is its own commit ahead of the working script, **defines the resolving ratio before computing it**, names four routes to a smaller scatter and commits to testing each, **names in advance the one it expected most from**, tables the terminal outcome, and names (1)'s own abuse hazard before meeting it   **(1) NOTHING BANKED BRINGS THE FLOOR BELOW WHAT THE CHANNELS CARRY.** The minimum resolvable share \$f\_\{\textbackslash\{\}min\} = 2\textbackslash\{\}sigma/\textbackslash\{\}lvert d\_\{\textbackslash\{\}rm arm\}\textbackslash\{\}rvert\$ runs \$0.59\$ to \$0.75\$ across **every** route this construction can build --- both aggregations, both abscissas, a longer lever arm to \$q=6.45\$, and finer bands  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***THE TERM MIX CARRIES \$0.35\$ OF THE STEP AND THE REALISED PAIR \$0.03\$, AGAINST A FLOOR OF \$0.59\$: THEY CANNOT BE RESOLVED BY ANY STATISTIC THIS CONSTRUCTION CAN BUILD***   *And the reason is what the pre-registration did **not** expect: the held-period aggregation lowers \$\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ by \$2.12\textbackslash\{\}times\$ **exactly as predicted** --- and lowers the SIGNAL by \$2.06\textbackslash\{\}times\$ at the same time, so \$f\_\{\textbackslash\{\}min\}\$ moves only \$0.65 \textbackslash\{\}to 0.63\$.  **The candidate named in advance as the one expected to help most does not help***   *And the abuse hazard fires before any power claim can be made: **the term mix and the realised pair CHANGE SIGN between the aggregations**, so their departures are not established at all and more power would not decide them --- only the window channel keeps its sign on all eight readings*   **(2) THE PHASE IS RESOLVED WHERE IT IS NOT NEEDED AND NOT WHERE IT IS.** The long-stretch projection works: over the upper range \$\textbackslash\{\}Delta = +0.007368 \textbackslash\{\}pm 0.000974\$ rad (\$+0.42\textasciicircum{}\textbackslash\{\}circ\$, a \$7.6\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ determination), the whole range as one stretch agreeing at \$+0.007541\$.  Band 1 reads \$-0.002440\$, **\$5.03\$ scatters out on the full-period yardstick**   *But that yardstick is wrong and the comparison is not licensed without the control: band 1 is \$0.70\$ of a period, and a projection over a **non-integer** number of periods leaks the baseline, so a narrow stretch is both noisier AND biased.  On six stretches of the SAME width the scatter is \$3.7\textbackslash\{\}times\$ larger*  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***BAND 1 SITS \$1.52\$ SCATTERS OUT, NOT \$5.03\$: THE PHASE STEP IS NOT RESOLVED AND `cc66.60`'s AMPLITUDE VERDICT STANDS AS THE COMPLETE ONE***   **(3) THE CANCELLATION IS NEITHER EXACT NOR APPROXIMATE --- IT IS AGGREGATION-DEPENDENT.** The joint vanishes within its own scatter on all four bases of the raw reading (\$0.24\$--\$0.54\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$) and on none of the phase-insensitive one (\$4.16\$--\$4.98\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$) \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} *(3) **inherits (1)'s answer rather than choosing a word** --- the third possibility the pre-registration named --- and constraint-versus-coincidence cannot be settled here*
 \emph{Bound:}  **NOT that the two channels are EXCLUDED** --- they are **unresolvable**, which is a statement about what this construction can measure and not about what the channels do  **NOT that the phase difference is zero** --- it is \$+0.42\textasciicircum{}\textbackslash\{\}circ\$ and resolved at \$7.6\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ in the upper range; what is unresolved is whether it **steps** at band 1  **NOT a composition rule** --- (3) returns no word at all, by design, because the two aggregations disagree  **NOT an aggregation, basis or abscissa choice**: every reading is carried and none chosen, and ***a resolution claim is required to hold on the SAME reading that measured the departure***, which is the hazard this file pre-registered against itself  **NOT a mechanism**, and (4) holds  Nothing SOLVED, nothing RUN; **no corpus edits**
\ \emph{Run:} \texttt{python3 receipts/P15\_CR\_cosmology/P15\_no\_statistic\_this\_construction\_can\_build\_resolves\_the\_two\_channels\_and\_the\_phase\_step\_dies\_on\_a\_matched\_width\_control.py}
\item[\rcptlabel{P15_the_likelihood_sees_the_step_and_separates_the_two_channels_so_the_demonstration_does_not_cover_it}{P15R208}\textbf{P15R208}\quad\texttt{P15\_the\_likelihood\_sees\_the\_step\_and\_separates\_the\_two\_channels\_so\_the\_demonstration\_does\_not\_cover\_it}]\hfill\textsf{[OK]}\\
\textit{`sec:refit-bound`, `sec:scope` --- **the likelihood sees the step as a localised contribution to its excess and separates the two channels at band 1 at twenty-five sigma**, so `cc66.61`'s demonstration covers the STATISTIC family and not the instrument the paper runs beside it, and **`PO-56`'s amended clause is NOT met**; and **eight of the eleven instruments this row has used band, three do not**} \ (node 66's `r7021` order, two items, after amending `PO-56`'s terminal clause to this seat's narrower sentence. (1) ***does the likelihood see the step*** --- *"your floor is proved over every STATISTIC this construction can build, but every one of them bands, and the likelihood does not band"*, with *"a demonstration that covers one instrument family and is silent about another the row already uses is not proved over its whole reach, which is your own standard for the word unresolvable"*, and *"if the likelihood turns out to be a banded statistic in disguise, that is the answer and it completes the demonstration rather than failing to"*; (2) ***say whether anything else this row has ever used does not band*** --- *"I do not want to strike on a demonstration with a third instrument outside it"*; (3) nothing else, **and do not soften the floor result**.  Guards: a narrow-window measurement needs a matched-width control; a reduction in scatter is only a gain if the signal does not fall with it; ***"unresolvable" is a claim about the instrument and must be proved over its whole reach***; **narrow a terminal clause before taking it**; and a departure that changes sign between two legitimate aggregations is not a quantity). 
\emph{Computes:}  **THE SEQUENCING, AND THE HARDEST PART OF IT**: `PREDICTION.md` is its own commit ahead of the working script and **says out loud which facts had already been looked at** --- the likelihood's bin structure, a property of the instrument fixed on disk regardless of any spectrum --- **declaring that branch SETTLED rather than tabling it as if open**   **(1)**(a)** THE LIKELIHOOD IS NOT A BANDED STATISTIC IN DISGUISE.** `plik\_lite` TT bins at a median \$0.0298\$ in \$q\$ --- **one thirty-fourth of a comb period** --- with \$24\$ bins inside band 1, and its bin-to-bin correlation is a flat \$\textbackslash\{\}approx 0.15\$ floor rather than a coupling that grows over a period.  *It bins; it does not BAND*   **(1)**(b)** AND THE STEP IS A LOCALISED CONTRIBUTION TO ITS EXCESS.** Band 1's bins carry \$36.4\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ of arm-versus-control separation on their own where its own bands 2--7 trend predicts \$53\$--\$71\$: a departure of \$-0.311\$ to \$-0.489\$ on the four bases at \$2.5\$--\$3.2\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$ of its own residual --- ***the same sign as every other instrument in this row, and the first per-band number in it carrying the INSTRUMENT'S own noise rather than an empirical scatter across a noiseless theory spectrum***   **(1)**(c)** AND IT SEPARATES THE TWO CHANNELS AT BAND 1 AT \$25.1\textbackslash\{\}sigma\$** --- the thing no statistic in this row can do --- with a smallest detectable share of the band-1 difference of \$0.055\$  \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***SO THE DEMONSTRATION COVERS THE STATISTIC FAMILY AND NOT THE INSTRUMENT THE PAPER RUNS BESIDE IT.  `PO-56`'s AMENDED CLAUSE IS NOT MET AND THE ROW HAS A MEASUREMENT TO MAKE RATHER THAN AN EXIT TO TAKE***   *And the two floors are in DIFFERENT UNITS, which the pre-registration required be said before the numbers were in hand: \$0.055\$ is a share of the band-1 DIFFERENCE under real instrument noise and `cc66.61`'s \$0.59\$ is a share of the STEP under an empirical scatter of a NOISELESS spectrum.* **Neither bounds the other**   **(2) THE SCOPE STATEMENT THE CLAUSE NEEDS.** Of **eleven** instruments this row has made a claim on, **eight BAND** at \$0.70\$ of a comb period; \emph{Computes:}  **GATE ZERO** --- **THE INSTRUMENT IS GATED ON A KNOWN INPUT BEFORE ANY MEASUREMENT PRINTS** --- a synthetic comb of amplitude \$0.5000\$ at period \$1.0347\$ recovered to \$0.24\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ in period and \$3.74\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ in amplitude, *and the gate REFUSES to print if it does not*  (1) **\$A\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$'s injection-independence CHECKED, not asserted** --- the two injections' `eta`, `x0`, `vis`, `k`, `P` and \$r\_s\textasciicircum{}\{*\}\$ byte-equal, *which is what licenses ONE `A` per arm instead of two keys holding identical arrays*  (2) **the factorisation EXACT** at \$2.7\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-15\}\$ (control) and \$2.1\textbackslash\{\}times10\textasciicircum{}\{-15\}\$ (arm) on **8 multipoles spread across the reported range**, *and \$A\_\textbackslash\{\}ell\$ RECOMPUTED from `vis`, `x0` and `k` reproducing the banked value to \$0.00\$* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} **the law is re-derived here and not trusted from its own bank**  (3) the acceptance spans \$5.2226\$ (control) against \$4.5364\$ (arm) of acoustic phase  (4) **the law forward, absolute, both arms, nothing fitted**: \$1.063/1.011/0.990/1.012/1.042/1.016/1.017\$ and \$1.067/1.015/0.990/1.012/1.044/1.018/1.016\$  (5) **and the reproduction gate across a CHANGE OF RUN SCHEME** --- \$1.0587\$ unsliced against `r6919`'s \$1.0587\$ sliced on `KBATCH`, \$0.0000\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ apart, *so the agreement covers the scheme and not only the statistic*
 \emph{Bound:}  ***NOT that the injection IS the real source*** --- *it is the projection's transfer of a KNOWN input, and over-delivery is sufficiency and not identity*  ***NOT a convergence claim*** --- *the REPORTED settings only; `r7041`'s sweep is a separate row and is running*  ***NOT an account of why the construction puts its scales and its distances on different rates*** --- *`r6919`'s own reserved bound, untouched here*  **NOT a tighter claim than the instrument supports** --- *six of seven bands sit inside its \$3.74\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ accuracy on a known input and no band is quoted tighter than that*  *and the two earlier measuring instruments are NAMED in the source rather than deleted: a band std \$46\textbackslash\{\}\%\$ wrong on a known input, and a per-band matched filter selecting a period by maximising amplitude inside a band holding less than one cycle* \ensuremath{\Rightarrow} ***`cc66.60`'s error a third time, and the first caught BEFORE a number was reported***
